### [66] [OxyEcomBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models across E-Commerce Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13173)
*Yong Liu,Ximan Liu,Guoqing Yang,Bing Bai,Xiaoqiang Xu,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了OxyEcomBench基准测试，用于系统评估LLM/MLLM在电子商务领域的表现。该基准包含约6300个高质量实例，覆盖6个能力维度和29个任务，涵盖平台运营者、商家和消费者三类角色，支持文本和多模态输入。评测结果显示，即使是最先进的模型在该基准上表现也较为平庸，证实了通用大模型缺乏足够的电子商务领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E-commerce poses distinctive challenges (intricate domain knowledge, long-tail product evidence, heterogeneous visual data, multiple stakeholder roles) that diverge from general world knowledge LLMs are trained on, creating a notable gap between open-domain and e-commerce performance.

Method: Created OxyEcomBench, a unified multimodal benchmark with ~6,300 authentic instances covering 6 capability aspects and 29 tasks across 3 stakeholder perspectives (platform operators, merchants, customers), supporting text-only and mixed-modality inputs with P0-P3 difficulty rubric.

Result: Evaluations on 20 mainstream LLMs and MLLMs show even leading models achieve modest performance; performance gaps narrow on this benchmark compared to general benchmarks, indicating insufficient e-commerce-specific knowledge mutes advantages of advanced general-purpose models.

Conclusion: Advanced general-purpose models lack sufficient e-commerce-specific knowledge infusion, which limits their advantages in e-commerce applications.

Abstract: LLMs and MLLMs have become indispensable tools across a wide range of applications. E-commerce, however, poses distinctive challenges -- including intricate domain knowledge, long-tail product evidence, heterogeneous visual data, and the interplay among multiple stakeholder roles -- that diverge substantially from the general world knowledge these models are primarily trained on, often causing a notable gap between their open-domain and e-commerce performance. To systematically quantify this gap, we introduce OxyEcomBench, a unified multimodal benchmark comprising approximately 6,300 high-quality instances for real-world bilingual Chinese--English e-commerce. Although several e-commerce benchmarks have been proposed, they typically adopt a single stakeholder perspective, target a narrow set of tasks, or address isolated challenges, making it difficult to holistically assess models' understanding of the full e-commerce pipeline. OxyEcomBench addresses these limitations by jointly covering platform operators, merchants, and customers across 6 capability aspects and 29 tasks, supporting text-only and mixed-modality inputs with single-image, multi-image, single-turn, and multi-turn configurations. All data is sourced from authentic e-commerce platforms and verified by domain experts. The benchmark further adopts a difficulty-aware design with a four-level P0--P3 rubric applied to all 29 tasks whose difficulty admits stable expert consensus, and rigorously prioritizes visually salient multimodal cases in which key evidence resides in images rather than text alone. Evaluations on 20 mainstream LLMs and MLLMs show that even the leading models attain modest performance and that performance gaps narrow on OxyEcomBench, suggesting that insufficient e-commerce-specific knowledge infusion mutes the advantages of advanced general-purpose models in this domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [67] [Fine-Tuning Models for Automated Code Review Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12610)
*Smitha S Kumar,Michael A Lones,Manuel Maarek,Hind Zantout*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索了如何利用参数高效微调和提示工程提升开源Code Llama模型生成Java代码反馈的质量，发现微调方法显著优于提示工程，生成的反馈获得学生认可，效果与ChatGPT相当，为开发免费、可扩展的编程教育反馈工具提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源模型存在成本高、计算需求大以及学生代码共享的伦理风险，而开源LLM目前能力不足，需要探索如何高效提升开源模型的代码反馈生成质量。

Method: 采用参数高效微调(PEFT)和提示工程两种方法，利用从大型模型蒸馏的数据集对开源Code Llama进行适配优化。反馈质量评估结合了学生评价、人工标注及自动化指标(BLEU、ROUGE、BERTScore)。

Result: 参数高效微调显著优于提示工程，生成的反馈质量获得学生认可，效果与ChatGPT相当，学生建议在反馈中加入更多技术术语的解释说明以增强实用性。

Conclusion: 经过参数高效微调的Code Llama模型能够生成质量与ChatGPT相当的代码反馈，为可免费部署的教育工具提供了可行路径，同时指出在反馈中加入技术术语解释可进一步提升效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models have introduced new possibilities for programming education through personalized support, content creation, and automated feedback. While recent studies have demonstrated the potential for feedback generation, many techniques rely on proprietary models, raising concerns about cost, computational demands, and the ethical implications of sharing student code. Open LLMs provide an alternative approach, but they do not currently have the capabilities of proprietary models. To address this problem, we investigate whether parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and prompt engineering, both of which distil knowledge from a dataset derived from a large, more capable model, can be used to adapt and enhance the quality of feedback generated by the open LLM Code Llama. Feedback quality on buggy Java code was assessed using a combination of student evaluation, manual annotation and the automated metrics BLEU, ROUGE, and BERTScore. Our findings indicate that PEFT leads to notable improvements in feedback quality and significantly outperforms prompt engineering, providing an avenue for developing freely deployable feedback tools that can be effectively used to guide student learning. Student evaluation indicates that learners value the PEFT model's feedback and see it as being equally effective as the proprietary ChatGPT model. Participants suggested that incorporating additional explanation for technical terms in the PEFT model's feedback could be more beneficial. This study demonstrates that fine-tuned models can effectively support critical thinking and guide the design of scalable pedagogical systems.

</details>


### [68] [User Reviews as a Source for Usability Requirements: A Precursor Study on Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12657)
*Cedric Wellhausen,Laura Reinhardt,Kurt Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用LLM替代传统机器学习方法分析用户评论中的可用性需求，通过300条评论数据集和基于Nielsen启发式的prompt工程，验证了LLM在需求识别任务中的可行性，但同时发现prompt设计对结果影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ML/DL方法需要大量人工标注数据，而LLM基于大规模预训练已具备较强的自然语言理解能力，可实现以用户为中心的需求获取工作流。研究旨在验证LLM在理解用户评论方面的表现是否可与人类评估者相当。

Method: 使用300条用户评论（来自三类应用），通过两轮迭代的prompt工程和基于Nielsen十大可用性启发式的编码指南，让LLM和人工标注者分别进行可用性相关需求标注，并对比F-score等指标评估性能。

Result: LLM总体上能够识别用户评论中的可用性需求（F-score指标良好），但性能和可靠性高度依赖于prompt质量。

Conclusion: LLM可用于辅助需求获取过程中的可用性相关需求识别，但实际应用效果高度依赖于prompt设计质量。该研究为开发团队提供了一种快速、低成本的用户需求收集与分析工作流程。

Abstract: It is known that user-centered approaches to requirements engineering in general lead to a better suited product for the end-users. LLM4RE provides promising approaches to support the requirements elicitation process (e.g. classification of requirements). Previous approaches focus on Machine-Learning (ML) or Deep-Learning (DL) aspects, which require intensive training with a large amount of manually labeled data. LLMs, on the other hand, are pre-trained on large amounts of user-generated text data, enabling a user-centric workflow to analyze requirements. In this paper, we explore the possibility of exploiting the improved natural language understanding of LLMs, rather than strict ML classification, together with the mass extraction of user reviews to analyze if the performance of LLMs in understanding user reviews is comparable to the performance of human raters. This enables a quick and cheap workflow for development teams to gather and process their userś requirements. This paper provides three major contributions: (1) We provide a completely coded dataset of 300 user reviews containing usability-relevant aspects from three different types of apps, that were labeled by two human raters and by an LLM. (2) We build an initial prompt, based on two prompt engineering iterations and specifically developed coding guidelines derived from the 10 Nielsen Usability Heuristics, for LLMs to filter usability relevant user reviews. (3) We determine that LLMs are generally able to recognize usability as a non-functional requirement in user reviews, in terms of their F-score, but the performance and reliability is strongly dependent on the prompt.

</details>


### [69] [Agentic Interpretation: Lattice-Structured Evidence for LLM-Based Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12694)
*Jacqueline L. Mitchell,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"智能体解释"框架，将静态分析的严格性方法（格结构、工作列表驱动）引入LLM驱动的程序推理，通过声明分解和格中判断追踪克服单次LLM查询的不透明性和脆弱性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可访问静态分析器无法获取的信息（如文档、安全公告、版本元数据、非正式API契约），但直接进行单次全程序分析存在问题：将众多依赖证据的判断压缩为单一不透明答案，无法暴露哪些结论被支持或质疑，也无法利用中间发现引导后续更有针对性的搜索。

Method: 提出"智能体解释"框架，将格基静态分析 discipline 引入LLM推理。具体包括：(1)将高层分析目标分解为局部化声明；(2)在有限高度格中追踪LLM对每个声明的判断；(3)使用工作列表算法控制声明及其判断在分析过程中的演变。

Result: 建立了智能体解释的形式化模型；探索了其设计空间；通过分析依赖不透明第三方组件的代码示例验证了方法可行性。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM驱动的程序推理引入了静态分析领域的严格性框架，通过将分析目标分解为局部化声明并在有限高度格中追踪LLM判断，实现了比单次黑盒查询更可控、可解释的程序分析方法。

Abstract: Large language models can consult information that fixed static analyzers cannot, such as documentation, current security advisories, version-specific metadata, and informal API contracts. This makes LLMs a compelling option for program analyses that depend on information beyond the source program, or that are otherwise not amenable to conventional static analyzers. However, directly asking an LLM for a one-shot whole-program analysis is brittle because it compresses many evidence-dependent judgments into a single opaque answer, rather than exposing which conclusions are supported or disputed and using intermediate findings to guide later, more focused searches. In this paper, we propose agentic interpretation, a framework that brings the discipline of lattice-based static analysis to LLM-driven program reasoning. At a high level, agentic interpretation decomposes a high-level analysis goal into localized claims, and tracks the LLM's judgment about each claim in a finite-height lattice. A worklist algorithm governs how claims and their judgments evolve during the analysis. We introduce a formal model of agentic interpretation, explore the design space it opens, and illustrate the approach with a worked example analyzing code that depends on opaque third-party components.

</details>


### [70] [Finding a Crab in the C: Assured Translation via Comparative Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12731)
*Caleb Helbling,Graham Leach-Krouse,Michael Crystal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Cozy工具通过比较二进制分析来验证从内存不安全语言（如C）翻译到内存安全语言（如SPARK/Rust）的代码，通过形式化验证确保两种二进制在除标记差异外的完全等价性，帮助开发者识别和评估翻译错误的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The challenge of integrating legacy non-memory safe code (e.g., C) with modern high-assurance systems using memory safe languages (SPARK/Rust), where automated translation introduces semantic differences that pose risks to system reliability and security.

Method: Cozy is a comparative binary analysis tool that simultaneously analyzes binaries from both original "unsafe" source code and translated memory-safe code, formally verifying equivalence while presenting flagged behavioral differences for developer assessment.

Result: Outside of flagged differences, binaries are formally verified equivalent; the review process guarantees equivalence modulo changes approved by the developer.

Conclusion: Cozy provides a formal verification framework for safely migrating legacy code to memory-safe languages, guaranteeing binary equivalence modulo developer-approved changes, with applications spanning automated translation, bug correction, code review, and operation authorization.

Abstract: Modern high-assurance software systems development favors memory safe languages such as SPARK (ADA) or Rust. However, developers often encounter non-memory safe code (e.g., C) in legacy systems and libraries which would be prohibitively expensive or risky to re-write. In response, developers have begun turning to machine learning/AI systems and other automated code translators. Automated translation comes with its own risks, however. The original and ported code are not precisely the same, semantically - otherwise there would be no point in performing the translation. To reduce these risks, we have developed cozy, a comparative binary analysis tool that simultaneously analyzes a binary compiled from "unsafe" source code and a binary compiled from a translation of the source code to a memory safe language. cozy walks the developer through differences in the behavior of the two binaries, presenting each difference and asking the user to assess whether the difference is intentional (good) or erroneous. Outside of the flagged differences, the binaries are formally verified to be equivalent. Consequently, the review process guarantees equivalence modulo changes approved by the developer. cozy has applications to automated translation, bug correction, code reviews, operation authorization, and automatic translation.

</details>


### [71] [Project Life Cycles in Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12738)
*Sanjiv Das,Andrii Ieroshenko,Piyush Jain,David Qiu,Michael Chin,Brian Granger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将产品生命周期方法应用于开源项目，通过项目生命周期建模开发者参与度，并检测跨项目间的相似动态特征。利用内生增长理论结合微分方程组建模开源软件工程中的增长动态，同时考虑增长水平与开发者活动的时间交互。该模型可良好拟合多个开源项目，并可估算终身开发者参与度、增长情况及开源项目的终身生产价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件（OSS）在现代软件开发中占据重要地位，但缺乏有效评估开源项目长期价值、开发者参与度及增长可持续性的理论框架。传统的产品生命周期方法未被系统性地应用于开源项目分析。

Method: 1. 借鉴产品生命周期理论；2. 应用内生增长理论（endogenous growth theory）于开源软件开发；3. 使用微分方程组（systems of differential equations）建模增长水平与开发者活动的时间交互；4. 对多个开源项目进行模型校准验证。

Result: 模型能够良好拟合多个开源项目的增长数据，成功检测出跨项目间的相似动态特征，并生成终身开发者参与度与增长估计值。

Conclusion: 该模型为开源项目提供了一种可量化的生命周期评估框架，可支持对开源项目终身生产价值的估算，为项目评估、投资决策及生态治理提供理论依据。

Abstract: Using methods previously applied to product life cycles, this paper models developer engagement through the project life cycle for open-source projects, and detects similar dynamics in a cross section of projects. Endogenous growth theory is used to model growth dynamics in open-source software engineering, while incorporating the interactions between growth levels and developer activity over time using systems of differential equations. The solution to this model calibrates well to many open-source projects. The model generates an estimate of the lifetime developer engagement and growth, which supports estimating a lifetime production value of open-source projects.

</details>


### [72] [AgentLens: Revealing The Lucky Pass Problem in SWE-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12925)
*Priyam Sahoo,Gaurav Mittal,Xiaomin Li,Shengjie Ma,Benjamin Steenhoek,Pingping Lin,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentLens框架解决SWE智能体评估中仅关注最终结果的问题，通过过程级分析发现10.7%的通过轨迹存在Lucky Pass行为，并证明不同模型后端的Lucky率差异显著（0.5%-23.2%），仅用通过率评估会严重低估模型能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SWE智能体评估仅依赖二元信号（通过/失败），将原则性解决方案与混沌试错过程等价对待，缺乏对执行过程质量的细粒度评估能力。

Method: 构建了包含1,815条轨迹的AgentLens-Bench数据集，通过合并同一任务的多个通过解决方案生成47个任务级Prefix Tree Acceptor（PTA）引用；采用上下文敏感的意图标注器，基于轨迹历史而非工具身份将动作分类为Exploration、Implementation、Verification或Orchestration。

Result: 发现47个任务具有足够多的通过轨迹可供分析，其中10.7%的通过轨迹表现出Lucky Pass行为（回归循环、盲目重试、缺失验证、时间顺序混乱的探索/实现/验证）。基于质量评分排名时，部分模型的位置变化高达5位。

Conclusion: AgentLens框架通过质量评分可将通过轨迹分为Lucky、Solid、Ideal三个层级，并识别出五种Lucky Pass机制。不同模型后端的Lucky率差异显著（0.5%-23.2%），仅用通过率评估会严重低估模型差距。项目已在GitHub开源，包含完整数据集和SDK。

Abstract: Evaluation of software engineering (SWE) agents is dominated by a binary signal: whether the final patch passes the tests. This outcome-only view treats a principled solution and a chaotic trial-and-error process as equivalent. We show that this equivalence is empirically false. We evaluate 2,614 OpenHands trajectories from eight model backends on 60 SWE-bench Verified tasks. Of these, 47 have enough passing trajectories to construct task-level process references, yielding a 1,815-trajectory evaluation subset. Among passing trajectories in this subset, 10.7% exhibit behavior we call a Lucky Pass: regression cycles, blind retries, missing verification, or temporally disordered exploration, implementation, and verification.
  We introduce AgentLens, a framework for process-level assessment of SWE-agent trajectories, and release AgentLens-Bench, a dataset of 1,815 trajectories annotated with quality scores, waste signals, divergence points, and 47 task-level Prefix Tree Acceptor (PTA) references. AgentLens builds PTA references by merging multiple passing solutions for the same task, and uses a context-sensitive intent labeler to assign actions to Exploration, Implementation, Verification, or Orchestration based on trajectory history rather than tool identity alone.
  On AgentLens-Bench, the quality score separates passing trajectories into Lucky, Solid, and Ideal tiers and further decomposes Lucky Passes into five recurring mechanisms. Across the eight model backends, Lucky rates range from 0.5% to 23.2%, and some models move by as many as five rank positions when ranked by quality score instead of pass rate. We release the anonymized project repository, including the AgentLens-Bench dataset and AgentLens SDK, at https://github.com/microsoft/code-agent-state-trajectories/.

</details>


### [73] [Protocol-Driven Development: Governing Generated Software Through Invariants and Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12981)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出协议驱动开发(PDD)方法，通过定义结构、行为、操作三类不变量构成的协议作为主要软件制品，为自动化程序综合提供治理框架。实现仅在满足协议并产生可验证证据链时方可 admitted，实现"代码是临时的，协议是 sovereign"的原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化程序综合降低了生成候选实现的成本，但引入了更困难的治理问题：如何确定哪些生成的制品在软件系统中是可接受的。自然语言规范在语义上存在歧义，基于示例的测试仅采样行为空间的一部分，两者单独使用都不能为自动化软件构建提供足够的控制边界。

Method: 提出协议驱动开发(PDD)模型，以机器可执行的协议作为核心软件制品。协议定义为三元组P=(S, B, O)：S定义结构不变量，B定义行为不变量，O定义操作不变量，三者合取界定软件组件的 admissible 实现空间。实现被视为可替换的发现物，通过约束搜索获得，仅当满足治理协议且产生可验证的合规证据链时才被接纳。

Result: 通过将协议满意度与记录的证据结合，Admission不再依赖对生成器的信任，而是基于协议满足和证据链验证。PDD在实现和协议之间建立了可验证的合规性边界。

Conclusion: PDD provides a governance framework for automated software engineering by combining formal methods, property-based testing, policy-as-code, and software provenance. Its核心原则是"代码是临时的，协议是 sovereign"——实现可以被替换，只要满足协议并产生可验证的合规证据链，Admission基于协议满足和记录证据，而非对生成器的信任。

Abstract: Automated program synthesis has reduced the cost of producing candidate implementations, but it introduces a harder governance problem: determining which generated artifacts are admissible in a software system. Natural-language specifications remain semantically ambiguous, and example-based tests sample only part of the behavioral space. Used alone, neither provides a sufficient control boundary for automated software construction. We introduce Protocol-Driven Development (PDD), a development model in which the primary software artifact is a machine-enforceable protocol rather than implementation code. We define a protocol as the triplet P = (S, B, O), where S specifies structural invariants, B specifies behavioral invariants, and O specifies operational invariants. Their conjunction defines the admissible implementation space of a software component. Under PDD, implementations are treated as replaceable realizations discovered through constrained search. An implementation is admitted if and only if it satisfies the governing protocol and produces a verifiable Evidence Chain of compliance. Admission is therefore grounded not in trust in the generator, but in protocol satisfaction and recorded evidence. By combining ideas from formal methods, property-based testing, policy-as-code, and software provenance, PDD defines a governance layer for automated software engineering. Its organizing principle is simple: code is transient; protocol is sovereign.

</details>


### [74] [Code-Centric Detection of Vulnerability-Fixing Commits: A Unified Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13138)
*Nils Loose,Joseph Bienhüls,Kristoffer Hempel,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对基于代码语言模型的漏洞修复提交（VFC）检测进行了全面评估。通过统一框架整合20+数据集、180,000+提交和125M-14B参数模型进行180+次实验，发现：模型无法仅从代码变更中获取可迁移的安全相关代码理解能力；提交信息会主导模型注意力；分组评估显示性能下降约17%；在0.5%误报率下代码模型遗漏超过93%的漏洞。研究发布了统一框架和评估套件以支持未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据库滞后于补丁发布平均25天，且大量安全修复从未获得官方公告，亟需自动化VFC检测方案

Method: 构建统一评估框架，整合20+碎片化数据集（超180,000次提交），对125M至14B参数的微调模型开展180+次实验，采用注意力归因分析和分组/时序分层评估

Result: （1）模型无法从代码变更中习得可迁移的安全理解；（2）提交信息可用时主导注意力，去除后语义增强无法改变注意力分布；（3）分组评估性能下降约17%；（4）时序分层因项目分布成分变化不可靠；（5）0.5%误报率下代码模型遗漏超93%漏洞；（6）更大更多样的训练数据或生成式方法有初步改善但未解决根本问题

Conclusion: 当前代码语言模型在纯代码VFC检测上存在根本性局限，提交信息的语义主导作用和缺乏安全知识迁移是核心挑战。研究发布的统一框架为代码中心VFC检测的进一步研究提供了标准化评估基础。

Abstract: Automated detection of vulnerability-fixing commits (VFCs) is critical for timely security patch deployment, as advisory databases lag patch releases by a median of 25 days and many fixes never receive advisories. We present a comprehensive evaluation of code language model based VFC detection through a unified framework consolidating over 20 fragmented datasets spanning more than 180000 commits. Across over 180 experiments with fine-tuned models from 125 M to 14 B parameters, we find no evidence that models acquire transferable security-relevant code understanding from code changes alone. When commit messages are available, they dominate model attention, and when removed, an attribution analysis shows that enriching diffs with additional intra-procedural semantic context does not shift model attention toward the code changes. Group-stratified evaluation exposes approximately 17% performance drops compared to random splits, while temporal splits on aggregated datasets prove unreliable due to compositional shift in the underlying project distributions. At a false positive rate of 0.5% all fine-tuned code-only models miss over 93% of vulnerabilities. Larger and more diverse training data or generative approaches show preliminary improvements but do not resolve the underlying limitations. To support future research on code-centric VFC detection, we release our unified framework and evaluation suite.

</details>


### [75] [SWE-Cycle: Benchmarking Code Agents across the Complete Issue Resolution Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13139)
*Hao Guan,Lingyue Fu,Shao Zhang,Yaoming Zhu,Kangning Zhang,Lin Qiu,Xunliang Cai,Xuezhi Cao,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Cycle是一个包含489个经过严格筛选实例的基准测试，用于评估自主代码智能体在环境重建、代码实现、验证测试生成三个独立任务以及一个整合所有阶段的FullCycle端到端任务上的能力。SWE-Judge结合静态代码审查与动态测试，准确验证功能正确性。研究对六种最先进的LLM进行了评估，发现从独立任务到FullCycle执行，解决率显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试在预配置环境中测试智能体，隐藏了实际摩擦；静态评估管道在解析完全自主轨迹时常失效。因此需要一个新的基准和评估框架来准确衡量自主软件智能体的端到端能力。

Method: 1. 构建SWE-Cycle基准：489个严格筛选的实例，涵盖三类隔离任务和FullCycle端到端任务；2. 开发SWE-Judge评估系统：结合静态代码审查与动态测试，消除传统静态解析器的系统性测量误差。

Result: 从独立任务过渡到FullCycle执行时，解决率急剧下降，暴露了处理跨阶段依赖和维护代码质量的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: SWE-Cycle和SWE-Judge共同提供了一个全面的框架，能够准确测量自主软件智能体的端到端能力，揭示了当前智能体在完整开发流程中的局限性。

Abstract: As autonomous code agents move toward end-to-end software development, evaluating their practical autonomy becomes critical. Current benchmarks hide friction by testing agents in pre-configured environments, and their static evaluation pipelines frequently fail when parsing fully autonomous trajectories. We address these limitations with SWE-Cycle, a benchmark of 489 rigorously filtered instances. SWE-Cycle evaluates agents across three isolated tasks, including environment reconstruction, code implementation, and verification test generation, as well as an end-to-end FullCycle task that integrates all three. The FullCycle task requires agents to work autonomously in a bare repository without human scaffolding. To reliably assess these complex execution paths, we developed SWE-Judge. By combining static code review with dynamic testing, this execution-capable evaluation agent accurately verifies functional correctness and eliminates the systematic measurement errors of traditional static parsers. We evaluate code agents powered by six state-of-the-art LLMs across these four tasks. The results reveal a sharp drop in solve rates when transitioning from isolated tasks to FullCycle execution, exposing critical bottlenecks in handling cross-phase dependencies and maintaining code quality. Together, SWE-Cycle and SWE-Judge provide a comprehensive framework for accurately measuring the end-to-end capabilities of autonomous software agents.

</details>


### [76] [Robust Mutation Analysis of Quantum Programs Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13279)
*Sophie Fortz,Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Shaukat Ali,Paolo Arcaini,Mohammad Reza Mousavi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对量子程序变异分析中的噪声影响问题，研究发现密度矩阵度量效果最佳但需模拟环境，输出分布度量是实际可行替代方案，准确率可达73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变异分析假设理想无噪声执行环境，忽略了量子硬件噪声的实际影响，无法准确评估量子软件测试技术的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用41个量子程序，在无噪声和有噪声模拟器上执行（模拟3个不同噪声配置的IBM设备），比较多种距离度量和阈值策略，评估噪声环境下变异检测效果。

Result: 噪声显著改变程序与变体间的行为距离，使等价变异体难以与真实缺陷区分。密度矩阵度量区分效果最好（误分类率达16.77%），但无法在真实硬件上使用；输出分布度量作为实用替代方案，准确率达73.03%，F1分数达74.89%；噪声专用阈值优于无噪声阈值；噪声影响与算法和电路特性相关性更高，而非变异类型。

Conclusion: 需要针对目标量子设备的噪声特征调整变异分析方法和量子程序比较技术；密度矩阵度量性能最佳但需要模拟环境，实际硬件上输出分布度量是更可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Mutation analysis has long been used in classical software testing and has recently been adopted for assessing the robustness of quantum software testing techniques. However, existing studies assume ideal, noiseless execution, overlooking the impact of quantum hardware noise. In this paper, we present an empirical study of noise-aware mutation analysis for quantum programs. We analyze how noise affects mutant detection using 41 quantum programs, executed on noiseless and noisy simulators emulating three IBM devices with different noise profiles. We compare several distance metrics and thresholding strategies to evaluate mutant detection under realistic noise. Our results show that noise significantly alters the behavioral distance between programs and mutants, making equivalent mutants harder to distinguish from real faults. Density-matrix metrics achieve the best discrimination, with misclassification rates up to 16.77%, but are not accessible on real hardware. Among practical alternatives, output-distribution metrics reach up to 73.03% accuracy and 74.89% F1-score. Noise-specific thresholds further improve detection compared to noiseless thresholds. We also find that noise effects correlate more with algorithm and circuit characteristics than with mutation types. Overall, our results highlight the need to adapt mutation analysis, and more generally quantum program comparison, to the noise profiles of target quantum devices.

</details>


### [77] [The Readability Spectrum: Patterns, Issues, and Prompt Effects in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13280)
*Hengzhi Ye,Fengyuan Ran,Weiwei Xu,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地研究了LLM生成代码的可读性问题。通过建立综合可读性模型（含文本、结构、程序、视觉四维特征），在5,869个场景上评估主流LLM，发现LLM生成代码的可读性整体与人类代码相当，但存在独特的问题模式。Prompt设计虽有影响但作用有限，揭示了潜在技术债务，为未来改进LLM生成代码可读性研究提供了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM改变软件开发范式，生成代码的功能质量已成为关注焦点，但可读性这一关键非功能属性尚未得到充分研究。鉴于LLM生成的代码在采用前仍需人工审查，理解其可读性特征及其与人类代码的差异，以及prompt设计的作用，具有重要价值。

Method: 建立综合可读性模型（融合代码的文本、结构、程序和视觉特征），在来自World of Code和LeetCode的5,869个场景中评估主流LLM生成代码的可读性，并分析不同prompt维度的影响。

Result: 当前LLM生成的代码整体可读性与人类代码相当，但存在独特的可读性问题模式；prompt设计中函数签名、约束和风格描述是最具影响力的因素，但prompt设计的整体影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码在可读性方面与人类代码相当，验证了其整合到软件工作流程中的潜力；但存在独特的可读性问题模式和有限 prompt 工程效果，揭示了潜在技术债务，呼吁未来研究改进LLM生成代码的可读性以确保长期可维护性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming software development, the functional quality of generated code has become a central focus, leaving readability, one of critical non-functional attributes, understudied. Given that LLM-generated code still needs human review before adoption, it is important to understand its readability especially compared with human-written code and the role of prompt design in shaping it. We therefore set out to conduct a systematic investigation into the code readability of LLM-generated code. To systematically quantify code readability, We establish a comprehensive readability model that synthesizes textual, structural, program, and visual features of code. Based on the model, we evaluate the readability of code generated by the mainstream LLMs under 5,869 scenarios extracted from large code base including World of Code (WoC) and LeetCode. We find that current LLMs produce code with overall readability comparable to human-written code, but displaying distinct readability issue patterns. We further examine how different prompt dimensions affect the readability of LLM-generated code, and find that function signatures, constraints and style descriptions emerge as the most influential factors, while the overall impact of prompt design remains limited. Our findings indicate that, on one hand, LLM-generated code is at least comparable to human-written code in readability, validating its potential for systematic integration into software workflows from a non-functional perspective; on the other hand, distinct readability issue patterns and limited effectiveness of prompt engineering reveal a latent technical debt, highlighting the need for future research to improve the readability of LLM-generated code and thus ensure long-term maintainability.

</details>


### [78] [SieveFL: Hierarchical Runtime-Aware Pruning for Scalable LLM-Based Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13491)
*Mahdi Farzandway,Fatemeh Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SieveFL 通过五阶段层次化过滤框架解决自动化缺陷定位问题，先用向量检索和运行时追踪大幅削减候选集，再由 LLM 筛选确认嫌疑人，在 commodity 硬件上即可超越前沿方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯统计方法定位精度有限，而 LLM 因 token 成本高昂和信号稀释问题无法在完整项目规模上处理缺陷定位任务。

Method: 提出 SieveFL，一个五阶段层次化框架：(1) 将失败的测试转换为自然语言失败描述；(2) 使用密集向量检索将搜索范围缩小到可疑文件集；(3) 通过 JaCoCo 运行时追踪消除未在失败测试中执行的方法；(4) 仅将幸存候选者传递给 LLM 进行单独筛选；(5) 在单次比较中重新排序确认的嫌疑人。

Result: 在 Defects4J v1.2.0 的 395 个缺陷上，Top-1 准确率达到 41.8%，MRR 为 0.469，较最强基线 AgentFL 提升 2.1 pp。运行时剪枝移除 79% 的候选方法，输入 token 消耗减少 49%，同时 Top-3 至 Top-10 提升达 2.4 pp。

Conclusion: 通过适当的多级过滤架构，即使使用 commodity 硬件上的中型 MoE 模型，也能实现有效的缺陷定位，无需专有前沿模型。

Abstract: Automated fault localization requires connecting an observed test failure to the responsible method across thousands of candidates--a task that purely statistical approaches handle with limited precision and that LLMs cannot yet handle at full project scale due to prohibitive token cost and signal dilution. We present SieveFL, a five-stage hierarchical framework that resolves this tension through aggressive pre-LLM filtering. SieveFL converts a failing test into a natural-language failure description, uses dense vector retrieval to narrow the search to a small set of suspicious files, and then eliminates any method not executed during the failing test via JaCoCo runtime traces. Only the surviving candidates are passed to the LLM, which screens each method individually and re-ranks the confirmed suspects in a single comparative pass. We evaluate SieveFL on 395 bugs from Defects4J v1.2.0 using a mid-sized, openly available MoE model deployed on a commodity workstation (32 GB RAM, 8 GB GPU) via Ollama--no frontier APIs or datacenter hardware required. Treating 12 incomplete runs as failures, SieveFL achieves Top-1 accuracy of 41.8% (165/395 bugs) and an MRR of 0.469, outperforming the strongest prior agent-based baseline (AgentFL) by 2.1 pp in Top-1. Runtime pruning removes 79% of candidate methods and reduces input token consumption by 49%, while simultaneously improving ranking quality: Top-1 is preserved exactly and Top-3 through Top-10 improve by up to 2.4 pp. These results demonstrate that, with the right filtering architecture, capable fault localization does not require proprietary frontier models.

</details>


### [79] [Scalable Deductive Verification of Data-Level Parallel Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13616)
*Lars B. van den Haak,Anton Wijs,Marieke Huisman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两项技术提升数组/矩阵程序的演绎验证可扩展性：量词表达式重写技术和重叠数组规范构造。在VerCors验证器中实现后，GPU内核验证平均加速9倍（最高150倍），并解决了此前无法验证的实际案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演绎验证数据级程序（尤其涉及数组和矩阵的程序）时面临可扩展性挑战，嵌套量词推理和重叠数组处理是主要瓶颈。

Method: 1. 提出了针对（嵌套）量词表达式的重写技术以生成合适触发器，该技术在定理证明器中证明了正确性；2. 引入了两个新的规范构造——非别名声明和不可变性声明，用于处理潜在重叠数组，使数组可被建模为数学序列；3. 所有技术集成于VerCors程序验证器。

Result: 在典型GPU内核验证中，平均加速9倍，极端案例加速150倍；此外，原本无法验证的射电望远镜管道案例现在可被验证，时间也大幅缩短。

Conclusion: 所提出的量词重写技术和别名/不可变数组规范构造显著提升了数组/矩阵程序演绎验证的可扩展性，平均加速9倍，最高可达150倍。该方法已在VerCors验证器中实现并成功应用于GPU内核和射电望远镜管道等实际案例。

Abstract: This paper introduces several techniques that improve the scalability of the deductive verification of data-level programs working on arrays and matrices. First of all, we introduce a technique to rewrite expressions with (nested) quantifiers, so suitable triggers can be generated for these expressions. We have proven this rewrite technique correct in a theorem prover. Second, we make reasoning about potentially overlapping arrays easier, by providing specification constructs to indicate and verify that two arrays are not aliases, or that they are immutable, so they can be modelled as mathematical sequences. All our techniques are implemented in the VerCors program verifier. We illustrate how our techniques improve scalability through a large number of experiments. Using our techniques on a set of typical GPU kernels, we achieve a reduction of verification time by, on average, a factor of 9, with outliers being up to 150 times faster. Additionally, applying these techniques to earlier experiments and an earlier case study of a radio telescope pipeline permitted the verification of results which were previously unobtainable and significantly reduced the verification time.

</details>


### [80] [SkillOps: Managing LLM Agent Skill Libraries as Self-Maintaining Software Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13716)
*Hongji Pu,Xinyuan Song,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillOps是一个用于维护技能库的方法无关插件框架，通过将技能表示为类型化技能契约(P,O,A,V,F)并构建层级技能生态系统图，从效用、兼容性、风险和验证四个维度诊断库健康状态，在ALFWorld上实现79.5%任务成功率，并可作为低开销架构层提升现有检索型智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前技能库智能体主要关注任务时的检索、规划和修复，而库级维护问题尚未被充分探索。技能库在技能添加、复用、修补和依赖链接过程中会积累持久性缺陷（技能技术债务），这些缺陷可能不会破坏单个技能的本地执行，但会损害未来的检索、组合和执行。

Method: 1) 将技能表示为类型化技能契约(P,O,A,V,F)；2) 构建层级技能生态系统图(Hierarchical Skill Ecosystem Graph)组织技能；3) 从效用(utility)、兼容性(compatibility)、风险(risk)和验证(validation)四个维度诊断库健康状态；4) 基于规则的维护实现，几乎零库时LLM调用或token消耗。

Result: 作为独立智能体在ALFWorld上达到79.5%任务成功率，比最强基线提升8.8个百分点且无需额外任务时LLM调用；作为插件层可提升检索密集型基线0.68至2.90个百分点。

Conclusion: 技能库维护可以作为低开销架构层添加到现有智能体系统中，无需修改检索或规划智能体的内部代码，且基于规则的维护实现几乎不消耗LLM资源，表明库时维护的可行性和实用性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on skill libraries for multi-step tasks, yet these libraries can accumulate persistent defects as skills are added, reused, patched, and linked to changing dependencies. We call this failure mode skill technical debt: library-level defects that may not break a single skill locally but can harm future retrieval, composition, and execution. Existing skill-based agents mainly focus on task-time retrieval, planning, and repair, while library-time maintenance remains underexplored. We propose SkillOps, a method-agnostic plug-in framework for maintaining skill libraries. SkillOps represents each skill as a typed Skill Contract (P, O, A, V, F), organizes skills with a Hierarchical Skill Ecosystem Graph, and diagnoses library health across utility, compatibility, risk, and validation dimensions. Given a raw skill library, SkillOps produces a maintained library that can be used by existing retrieval or planning agents without changing their internal code. On ALFWorld, SkillOps achieves 79.5 percent task success as a standalone agent, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.8 percentage points with no additional task-time large language model calls. As a plug-in layer, it improves retrieval-heavy baselines by 0.68 to 2.90 percentage points. The current rule-based maintenance implementation uses nearly zero library-time large language model calls or tokens, showing that skill-library maintenance can be added as a low-overhead architectural layer.

</details>


### [81] [(How) Do Large Language Models Understand High-Level Message Sequence Charts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13773)
*Mohammad Reza Mousavi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种大型语言模型（Gemini-3、GPT-5.4、Qwen-3.6）对高级消息序列 charts（HMSCs）形式语理的理确程度。通过129项语义任务的实验发现，LLMs对HMSCs形式语理的总体理解水平有限（准确率约52%），尤其在抽象组合和轨迹计算方面表现不佳，且未能正确处理共区域和因果依赖等高级语义概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件开发生命周期中广泛应用LLM自动化任务，但缺乏对LLM是否一致理解所处理工件语义的研究，特别是架构设计规格方面。HMSCs作为具有严格形式语义的视觉模型，是理想的研究对象。

Method: 实验研究法。选取三种LLM（Gemini-3、GPT-5.4、Qwen-3.6），设计129项语义任务，涵盖HMSCs的基本语义构造查询、语义保持的抽象与组合操作、以及轨迹和轨迹等价标签转换系统的计算。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）LLMs整体准确率约52%，仅具有中等程度的语义理解；2）基础概念理解较好（~88%），但抽象组合和轨迹/LTS计算能力较弱（分别为36%和42%）；3）所有模型均未能在语义保持转换中正确使用共区域和显式因果依赖概念。

Conclusion: LLMs仅具有中等程度的HMSCs形式语义理解能力，整体准确率约52%。不同语义概念间表现差异显著：基础概念理解较好（88%），但抽象/组合推理（36%）和轨迹/LTS计算（42%）方面表现不佳。三种模型均未能正确处理共区域概念和显式因果依赖关系。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being employed widely to automate tasks across the software development life-cycle. It is, however, unclear whether these tasks are performed consistently with respect to the semantics of the artefacts being handled. This question is particularly under-researched concerning architectural design specification. In this paper, we address this question for High-Level Message Sequence Charts (HMSCs). These are visual models with a rigorous formal semantics that have been used for various purposes, including as a foundation for Sequence Diagrams in the Unified Modelling Language (UML). We examine whether LLMs "understand" the semantics of HMSCs by examining three LLMs (Gemini-3, GPT-5.4, and Qwen-3.6) on how they perform 129 semantic tasks ranging from querying basic semantic constructs in HMSCs (i.e., events and their ordering) to semantic-preserving abstractions and compositions, and calculating the set of traces and trace-equivalent labelled transition systems. The results show that LLMs only have a modest understanding of the formal semantics of HMSCs (ca. 52% overall accuracy), with great variability across different semantic concepts: while LLMs seem to understand the basic semantic concepts of MSCs (ca. 88% accuracy), they struggle with semantic reasoning in tasks involving abstraction and composition (ca. 36% accuracy) and traces and LTSs (ca. 42% accuracy). In particular, all three LLMs struggle with the notions of co-region and explicit causal dependencies and never employed them in semantic-preserving transformations.

</details>

### [82] [Efficient and Portable Support for Overdecomposition on Distributed Memory GPGPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12734)
*Aditya Bhosale,Anant Jain,Shourya Goel,Ritvik Rao,Peddoju Sateesh Kumar,Laxmikant Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper addresses the challenge of supporting overdecomposition—a parallel programming technique used in Charm++—on GPGPU systems. The higher overhead from overpartitioning (multiple objects per GPU) and portability across GPU vendors are the main concerns. The authors propose techniques and software to enable efficient, portable overdecomposition across different GPU types and networks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPGPUs have become dominant compute components, but overdecomposition (overpartitioning with multiple objects per GPU device) introduces higher overhead, posing challenges to the Charm++ parallel programming paradigm.

Method: Developing techniques and software to support overdecomposition on GPU-based systems.

Result: Techniques and software demonstrating efficient and portable support for overdecomposition across different GPU vendor types and interconnection networks. (Proposed, not yet validated)

Conclusion: Overdecomposition can be efficiently and productively supported on combinations of different GPU vendor types and interconnection networks, enabling portability.

Abstract: Overdecomposition has emerged as a powerful and sometimes essential technique in parallel programming. Many application domains or frameworks, including those based on adaptive mesh refinements, or tree codes use it. Charm++ is a parallel programming system which has demonstrated the utility of overdecomposition for many applications and in multiple contexts. However, the emergence of GPGPUs as a dominant compute component has created some real and perceived challenges for this paradigm, especially regarding the higher overhead brought about by overpartitioning -- having multiple objects assigned to the same GPGPU device. We address this issue as well as the issue of portability by developing techniques and software that demonstrate that overdecomposition can be efficiently and productively supported on combinations of GPU vendor types, and interconnection networks.

</details>


### [83] [Communication Efficient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12935)
*Muhammad Ayaz Dzulfikar,Seth Gilbert*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本论文证明拜占庭协议中，高通信量（Ω(n³)）和预测信息共享并非预测增强方法的必要代价。通过无认证算法O~(n^{2.5})和有认证算法O(n²κ)两种方案，在不牺牲最优轮复杂度的前提下大幅降低了通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拜占庭协议存在Ω(f)轮数下界。现有的预测增强算法存在两个问题：(1)只能处理有限的预测错误；(2)通信复杂度高达Ω(n³)，足以让进程大致共识预测分类。

Method: 提出两个新算法：(1) 无认证算法，采用O~(n^{2.5})通信复杂度；(2) 有认证算法，达到最优O(n²κ)通信复杂度（κ为安全参数）。两算法均能容忍任意数量的预测错误。

Result: 所有算法对任意数量的预测错误均具有最优轮复杂度，且在有认证情况下达到最优通信复杂度O(n²κ)。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了高通信量并非不可避免，提供了更高效的拜占庭协议解决方案；其中无认证算法达到O~(n^{2.5})通信复杂度，有认证算法达到最优O(n²κ)复杂度，且所有结果对任意数量的预测错误均具有最优轮复杂度。

Abstract: In Byzantine agreement with predictions each process begins with an input value and some (unreliable) prediction bits. Recently, it has been shown that with \emph{classification predictions} -- where the predictions predict each process to be honest or faulty -- Byzantine agreement can be completed more quickly than without predictions, circumventing the traditional $Ω(f)$ round lower bound. However, existing algorithms either handle limited prediction errors or send too many messages. Moreover, they all exchange $Ω(n^3)$ bits -- enough to allow the processes to approximately agree on the classifications. In fact, it almost seemed necessary to share a significant number of prediction bits if one wanted to tolerate a high number of incorrect predictions.
  In this paper, we show that this high level of communication (and sharing of predictions) is not inherent by developing an unauthenticated algorithm with $\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$ communication complexity. Furthermore, with authentication, we give an algorithm with optimal $O(n^2κ)$ communication complexity (where $κ$ is a security parameter). All of our results have optimal round complexity for any number of errors in the predictions.

</details>


### [84] [Comparing the Performance of Heterogeneous Conjugate Gradient and Cholesky Solvers on Various Hardware Using SYCL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13209)
*Tim Thüring,Alexander Strack,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出基于SYCL的异构CG与Cholesky分解实现，在大矩阵场景下相比GPU-only方案性能提升12%-32%，并支持NVIDIA、AMD、Intel多供应商GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高规模线性系统求解是许多实际应用（如高斯过程系统辨识）的核心需求，现有方法仅依赖GPU而未充分利用现代HPC系统中强大的CPU资源。

Method: 利用SYCL实现多供应商异构编程，同时调度CPU和GPU协同计算CG方法与Cholesky分解。

Result: 大矩阵场景下，异构CG实现比GPU-only快32%，Cholesky分解快29%；跨NVIDIA/AMD/Intel平台均实现至少12%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 异构实现能够在不同供应商（NVIDIA、AMD、Intel）的GPU上均超越传统的GPU-only方法，为高性能计算系统中的线性系统求解提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Many important real-world applications, such as System Identification with Gaussian Processes, involve solving linear systems with symmetric positive-definite matrices. The iterative CG method and direct solvers based on the Cholesky decomposition are two popular methods that can be applied in this case. Since often very large systems have to be solved when dealing with such real-world scenarios, GPUs are commonly used to accelerate the computations. However, homogeneous approaches that only leverage the GPU in the system do not take full advantage of the often powerful CPUs located in modern HPC systems. In this work, we present multi-vendor, heterogeneous implementations of the CG method and the Cholesky decomposition that leverage the CPU and GPU of a heterogeneous system simultaneously using SYCL. Furthermore, we compare their runtime behavior to traditional, homogeneous approaches. The results show that for large matrices, our heterogeneous implementation is up to 32 percent faster for the CG method and up to 29 percent faster for the Cholesky decomposition compared to the corresponding GPU-only implementations. In addition, for large matrices, our heterogeneous implementation of the Cholesky decomposition can achieve at least 12 percent faster runtimes across several systems with GPUs from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel.

</details>


### [85] [PipeSD: An Efficient Cloud-Edge Collaborative Pipeline Inference Framework with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13319)
*Yunhe Han,Yunqi Gao,Bing Hu,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Yitong Duan,Pei Xiao,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PipeSD是一个高效的云边协同推理框架，通过动态规划优化的流水线调度重叠token生成与通信，并利用双阈值NAV触发机制和贝叶斯优化实现自适应验证，在真实环境中实现1.16x-2.16x加速和14.3%-25.3%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云边协同推理框架存在两个关键问题：(i) token生成与通信顺序执行导致资源利用率低；(ii) 云端非自回归验证(NAV)触发机制不灵活，容易导致过早验证或昂贵的回滚操作。

Method: PipeSD提出两个核心创新：1) 基于动态规划优化的token-batch流水线调度机制，将token生成与通信过程重叠执行；2) 双阈值NAV触发机制配合轻量级贝叶斯优化自动调优器，实现灵活的验证时机控制。

Result: 在真实云边测试环境中，使用两对draft-target模型在四种场景下评估，PipeSD相比最优基线方法实现1.16x-2.16x加速比，同时降低能耗14.3%-25.3%。

Conclusion: PipeSD通过流水线调度和自适应验证触发机制，有效解决了传统云边协同推理中资源利用率低和验证不灵活的问题，在保持系统鲁棒性的同时实现了显著的性能提升和能耗降低。

Abstract: Speculative decoding can significantly accelerate LLM inference, especially given that its cloud-edge collaborative deployment offers cloud workload offloading, offline robustness, and privacy enhancement. However, existing collaborative inference frameworks with speculative decoding are constrained by (i) sequential token generation and communication with low resource utilization, and (ii) inflexible cloud non-autoregressive verification (NAV) triggering that induces premature verification or costly rollbacks. In this paper, we propose PipeSD, an efficient cloud-edge collaborative pipeline inference framework with speculative decoding. PipeSD overlaps token generation and communication by a token-batch pipeline scheduling mechanism optimized by dynamic programming, and improves verification flexibility through a dual-threshold NAV triggering mechanism with a lightweight Bayesian optimization autotuner. We implement PipeSD using llama-cpp-python, PyTorch, and FastAPI, and evaluate it on a real-world cloud-edge testbed with two draft-target model pairs across four scenarios. Results show that PipeSD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 1.16x-2.16x speedup and reducing energy consumption by 14.3%-25.3%.

</details>


### [86] [TurboGR: An Accelerated Training System for Large-Scale Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13433)
*Huichao Chai,Zhixin Wu,Xuemiao Li,Shiqing Fan,Hengfeng Wang,Maojun Peng,Lu Xu,Yaoyuan Wang,Yibo Jin,Wei Guo,Yongxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对Ascend NPU上的生成式推荐系统训练提出\model系统，通过参差加速、分布式通信优化和负采样优化三大创新，解决了稀疏计算与NPU架构不匹配的关键瓶颈，实现了94%的NPU利用率和近线性扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐（GR）作为统一Transformer架构的新范式展现出良好的扩展定律，但在大规模Ascend NPU上部署面临系统性挑战，主要源于参差算子的高性能实现缺失以及不规则稀疏原语与NPU密集计算优化架构之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出\model系统，包含三大核心创新：（i）Ascend亲和的参差（jagged）加速，通过融合算子消除填充冗余，动态负载均衡将设备间不平衡从47%降至2.4%；（ii）分布式通信优化，采用分层稀疏并行、半异步训练（带收敛保证）及细粒度流水线编排；（iii）负采样优化，通过异步卸载、参差感知的FP16量化及批次内logit共享扩展有效负空间。

Result: 在KuaiRand-27K数据集上验证，支持最高0.2B参数规模训练，达到54.71%的MFU利用率，近线性扩展性达0.97。

Conclusion: 所提系统在Ascend NPU上成功解决了生成式推荐系统部署的瓶颈问题，通过三种核心创新实现了高NPU利用率（94%）和近线性扩展性（0.97），为大规模生成式推荐训练提供了可行的Ascend生态解决方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm that replaces fragmented, scenario-specific architectures with unified Transformer-based models, exhibiting scaling-law behavior where recommendation quality improves systematically with increased model capacity and training data. However, deploying GR at scale on Ascend NPUs faces fundamental system-level challenges. These challenges are further exacerbated on Ascend NPUs due to the absence of high-performance implementations for jagged operators and the architectural mismatch between irregular sparse primitives and NPU's dense-computation-optimized design. In this paper, we present \model, an Ascend-affinity training system for generative recommendation that systematically addresses these bottlenecks through three core innovations: (i) Ascend-affinity jagged acceleration, including fusion operators that eliminate padding redundancy and dynamic load balancing that reduces inter-device imbalance from 47\% to 2.4\%; (ii) distributed communication optimization, comprising hierarchical sparse parallelism, semi-asynchronous training with proven convergence guarantees, and fine-grained pipeline orchestration that sustains 94\% NPU utilization; and (iii) negative sampling optimization via asynchronous offloading, jaggedness-aware FP16 quantization, and intra-batch logit sharing that expand the effective negative space without additional embedding lookups. Evaluated on the KuaiRand-27K dataset, \model supports training at up to 0.2B parameters and achieves 54.71\% MFU with near-linear scalability (0.97).

</details>


### [87] [MARLIN: Multi-Agent Game-Theoretic Reinforcement Learning for Sustainable LLM Inference in Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13496)
*H. Moore,S. Qi,D. Milojicic,C. Bash,S. Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MARLIN框架，利用多智能体博弈论强化学习协同优化大语言模型推理的多个指标，包括首token响应时间(TTFT)、碳排放、水资源消耗和能源成本，在云数据中心环境中实现显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理请求占LLM整个生命周期能源消耗的90%，远超训练成本；随着LLM服务请求量增长，碳排放和水资源消耗等环境足迹问题日益严峻，亟需在保证服务质量的同时实现可持续发展的推理服务框架。

Method: 提出名为MARLIN的多智能体博弈论强化学习框架，通过协同优化四个关键指标（TTFT、碳排放、水资源使用和能源成本）来管理LLM推理服务。

Result: 相比当前最先进的LLM推理管理框架，MARLIN实现了：TTFT降低至少18%、碳排放降低33%、水资源使用降低43%、能源成本降低11%。

Conclusion: MARLIN框架能有效平衡LLM推理服务的性能与可持续性，为云数据中心环境下的绿色AI服务提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become increasingly prevalent in cloud-based platforms, propelled by the introduction of AI-based consumer and enterprise services. LLM inference requests in particular account for up to 90% of total LLM lifecycle energy use, dwarfing training energy costs. The rising volume of LLM inference requests is increasing environmental footprints, particularly carbon emissions and water consumption. To improve sustainability for LLM inference serving in cloud datacenter environments, we propose a novel multi-agent game-theoretic reinforcement learning framework called MARLIN to co-optimize time-to-first token (TTFT), carbon emissions, water usage, and energy costs associated with LLM inference. MARLIN demonstrates a reduction of at least 18% in TTFT, 33% in carbon emissions, 43% in water usage, and 11% in energy costs compared to state-of-the-art LLM inference management frameworks.

</details>

### [88] [Learning When to Act: Communication-Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Run-Time Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12561)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Cody Fleming,Tristan Schuler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合学习控制输入与通信时序决策的安全RL框架，在点对点Lyapunov安全屏蔽下实现稀疏通信与稳定性保证。通过CARE-based LQR备份和RTA层，实验表明自适应时序决策（而非降低平均采样率）才是稀疏通信安全的关键，CARE导出的Lyapunov奖励可跨环境迁移，RTA层对性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全强化学习通常关注“做什么”，而忽视“何时行动”的问题；现有方法仅在期望意义上约束安全，缺乏严格保证；经典Lyapunov-STC在高位系统中不可行；需要自适应时序决策以实现稀疏通信同时保证稳定性。

Method: 提出点对点Lyapunov安全屏蔽下的单一策略，联合学习控制输入与通信高效时序决策；采用CARE-based LQR备份进行策略优化；运行时保证层（RTA）通过单步前瞻Lyapunov预测与预计算LQR备份覆盖策略，提供比期望约束更强的严格保证；偏好条件扩展从单一模型恢复完整权衡前沿。

Result: 倒立摆、Cart-pole、平面四旋翼上，策略实现1.91×、1.45×、3.51×更高的平均采样间隔；同速率固定LQR在三个系统上均不稳定；移除RTA后MSI下降1.27~1.84×；12状态3D四旋翼对±30%质量变化和扰动具有优雅降级特性。

Conclusion: 自适应时序决策（而非更低的平均采样率）使稀疏通信变得安全；CARE导出的Lyapunov奖励可跨环境迁移无需重新设计；RTA屏蔽层至关重要；该框架可扩展至高维系统（12状态3D四旋翼），对±30%质量变化和扰动具有鲁棒性，在离散和连续动作空间中均适用（SAC验证）。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) typically asks $\textit{what}$ an agent should do. We ask $\textit{when}$ it needs to act, and show that a single policy can jointly learn control inputs and communication-efficient timing decisions under a pointwise Lyapunov safety shield. We focus on stabilization around a known equilibrium, where CARE-based LQR backups, Lyapunov certificates, and classical Lyapunov-STC are well defined, enabling clean comparison against analytical baselines. A run-time assurance (RTA) layer overrides the policy via a one-step-ahead Lyapunov prediction and a precomputed LQR backup, providing a strictly stronger guarantee than constrained MDP methods that enforce safety only in expectation. On an inverted pendulum, cart--pole, and planar quadrotor, the learned policy achieves $1.91\times$, $1.45\times$, and $3.51\times$ higher mean inter-sample interval (MSI) than a Lyapunov-triggered baseline; a fixed LQR controller at the same average rate is unstable on all three plants, showing that adaptive timing, not a lower average rate, makes sparsity safe. A CARE-derived Lyapunov reward transfers across environments without redesign, with a single weight $w_c$ controlling the stability--communication tradeoff; ablations confirm the RTA shield is essential, with its removal reducing MSI by $1.27$--$1.84\times$ and degrading state norms. A preference-conditioned extension recovers the full tradeoff frontier from one model at $\tfrac{2}{11}$ of training compute, and SAC experiments show the results are algorithm-agnostic across discrete and continuous domains. A 12-state 3D quadrotor case study extends the framework to higher-dimensional systems where classical STC is intractable, and robustness to $\pm30\%$ mass variation and disturbances shows graceful degradation, with the RTA absorbing what the learned policy cannot.

</details>


### [89] [Towards Robust Federated Multimodal Graph Learning under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12584)
*Sirui Zhang,Haonan Wang,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦多模态图学习中模态缺失与数据孤岛的双重挑战，提出FedMPO框架。该框架通过拓扑感知跨模态生成、缺失感知专家路由和可靠性感知聚合三阶段设计，有效解决了本地模态补全利用全局语义不足、聚合阶段可靠性失衡的问题，在多个场景下验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中多模态图数据面临双重挑战：跨参与方的数据共享受限导致图数据孤立，且各参与方普遍存在模态不完整问题。现有方法存在明显局限——集中式多模态图学习方法忽视联邦场景中的知识共享与泛化需求，而现有联邦多模态学习方法主要面向非图数据，难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出FedMPO框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)拓扑感知的跨模态生成，利用全局图上下文信息恢复缺失特征；(2)缺失感知的专家路由，本地过滤噪声恢复信号；(3)可靠性感知的聚合，对不可靠更新进行适当降权。框架采用客户端-服务器架构，客户端完成缺失模态重建，服务器聚合模态生成器和骨干模型的参数更新。

Result: 在6个数据集、3项任务上的广泛实验表明，FedMPO显著优于基线方法，在高缺失率和非独立同分布场景下分别获得4.10%和5.65%的性能提升。

Conclusion: FedMPO通过三阶段技术设计有效解决了联邦多模态图学习中模态缺失与数据孤岛的挑战，在高缺失率和非独立同分布场景下均取得显著性能提升，为跨方图数据协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal graph learning (MGL) has garnered significant attention for integrating diverse modality information and structured context to support various network applications. However, real-world graphs are often isolated due to data-sharing limitations across multiple parties, and their modalities are frequently incomplete. This highlights an urgent need to develop a robust federated approach. However, we find that existing methods remain insufficient. On the one hand, centralized MGL methods that handle missing modalities overlook the knowledge sharing and generalization in federated scenarios. On the other hand, while federated MGL methods have become increasingly mature, they primarily target non-graph data. Based on these technologies, we identify a two-stage pipeline wherein client-side completion reconstructs missing modalities, and server-side aggregation integrates the client-updated parameters of both the modality generator and the backbone models. Although this serves as a general solution, we identify two primary challenges in achieving greater robustness: (1) Topology-Isolated Local Completion: Client-side modality generation struggles to effectively leverage global semantics. (2) Reliability-Imbalanced Global Aggregation: Server-side multi-party collaboration is hindered by client updates with varying modality availability and recovery reliability. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{FedMPO}, which utilizes topology-aware cross-modal generation to recover missing features using comprehensive graph context, missing-aware expert routing to locally filter out noisy recovered signals, and reliability-aware aggregation to appropriately down-weight unreliable updates. Extensive experiments on 3 tasks across 6 datasets demonstrate that FedMPO outperforms baselines, achieving performance gains of up to 4.10% and 5.65% in high-missing and non-IID settings.

</details>


### [90] [OceanCBM: A Concept Bottleneck Model for Mechanistic Interpretability in Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12639)
*Sanah Suri,Kieran Ringel,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OceanCBM是首个用于海洋动力学的时空预测和机械可解释性分析的概念瓶颈模型，通过混合监督方法预测混合层热含量，并结合基于地球物理流体力学的概念和一个"自由"概念作为中间层，在保持预测能力的同时提供可解释的物理表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋极端现象难以预测和诊断，现有的机器学习方法虽然预测性能强，但缺乏可解释性，无法保证对真实物理的保真度。需要一种既能准确预测又能揭示物理驱动机制的方法。

Method: OceanCBM采用概念瓶颈模型架构，使用混合监督策略，将基于地球物理流体力学的物理概念和一个"自由"概念作为中间层，实现软物理约束而不过度约束模型。

Result: 混合监督方法产生一致的机械表征，而仅预测和仅处方基线方法虽然预测性能相似，但学习了高度可变的潜在结构。OceanCBM在保持预测技能的同时实现了可解释的、基于物理的表征。

Conclusion: OceanCBM首次将概念瓶颈模型应用于海洋动力学领域，成功在可解释性和预测性能之间建立明确的权衡关系，为海洋现象的机械理解和预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Extreme ocean phenomena are challenging not only to predict but to diagnose, as accurate forecasts alone do not reveal the underlying physical drivers. While recent machine learning approaches achieve strong predictive skill, they remain largely opaque and provide limited guarantees of fidelity to ground-truth physics. We introduce OceanCBM, the first concept bottleneck model (CBM) for spatiotemporal prediction and mechanistic interrogation of ocean dynamics. OceanCBM uses mixed supervision to predict mixed layer heat content, a key precursor of marine heatwaves, while routing information through an intermediate layer of prescribed concepts derived from geophysical fluid dynamics and a 'free' concept. This design imposes soft physical structure without over-constraining the model, and the free concept both regularizes concept predictions and captures residual physical processes. Across ensemble initializations, we show that mixed supervision yields consistent mechanistic representations, whereas prediction-only and prescription-only baselines learn highly variable latent structures despite similar predictive performance. OceanCBM achieves interpretable, physically grounded representations without sacrificing skill, explicitly characterizing the interpretability-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [91] [Learning to Decide with AI Assistance under Human-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12646)
*Nina Corvelo Benz,Eleni Straitouri,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究人工智能辅助决策中AI置信度与人类置信度对齐对学习复杂度的影响。在二元预测和二元决策的规范场景下，将问题转化为双臂在线上下文学习，证明完美对齐可将遗憾界从Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))降低至O(√T log T)，并通过两个真实人类被试实验验证了理论结果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助决策系统中，即使AI模型传递了置信度信息，决策者仍难以判断何时应信任预测。近期研究表明AI置信度与人类置信度之间的对齐程度与决策效用正相关，但尚不清楚这种对齐如何影响学习最优决策的复杂度。

Method: 1) 将AI辅助二元决策问题形式化为双臂在线上下文学习问题（完全反馈）；2) 建立遗憾下界Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))；3) 在完美对齐条件下设计算法达到O(√|H|·T log T)的遗憾界；4) 当√|H|=O(log T)且B可数时，利用DKW不等式的广义形式改进遗憾界至O(√T log T)；5) 使用两个不同的人类被试实验数据集进行实证验证。

Result: 1) 任何学习器的遗憾下界为Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))；2) 完美对齐条件下遗憾上界为O(√|H|·T log T)；3) 特殊条件下遗憾可进一步降至O(√T log T)；4) 真实数据实验表明理论结果对完美对齐假设的违反具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AI置信度与人类置信度的对齐能够降低学习最优决策的复杂度。当对齐程度较高时，学习问题所需样本量显著减少，这对设计更有效的人机协作决策系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: It is widely agreed that when AI models assist decision-makers in high-stakes domains by predicting an outcome of interest, they should communicate the confidence of their predictions. However, empirical evidence suggests that decision-makers often struggle to determine when to trust a prediction based solely on this communicated confidence. In this context, recent theoretical and empirical work suggests a positive correlation between the utility of AI-assisted decision-making and the degree of alignment between the AI confidence and the decision-makers' confidence in their own predictions. Crucially, these findings do not yet elucidate the extent to which this alignment influences the complexity of learning to make optimal decisions through repeated interactions. In this paper, we address this question in the canonical case of binary predictions and binary decisions. We first show that this problem is equivalent to a two-armed online contextual learning problem with full feedback, and establish a lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{|H| \cdot |B| \cdot T} )$ on the expected regret any learner can attain, where $H$ and $B$ denote the sets of human and AI confidence values. We then demonstrate that, under perfect alignment between AI and human confidence, a learner can attain an expected regret of $O(\sqrt{|H| \cdot T\log T})$ and, when $\sqrt{|H|} = O(\log T)$ and $B$ is countable, a non-trivial generalization of the Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz inequality improves the regret bound to $O(\sqrt{T\log T})$. Taken together, these results reveal that alignment can reduce the complexity of learning to make decisions with AI assistance. Experiments on real data from two different human-subject studies where participants solve simple decision-making tasks assisted by AI models show that our theoretical results are robust to violations of perfect alignment.

</details>


### [92] [Runtime Monitoring of Perception-Based Autonomous Systems via Embedding Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12651)
*Parv Kapoor,Abigail Hammer,Ashish Kapoor,Karen Leung,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），直接在嵌入空间中执行运行时监控，通过距离谓词捕获高层感知概念，避免了传统方法对低维状态映射的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖低维状态变量的离散逻辑命题进行监控，但在感知驱动场景中需要额外的学习模块，这些模块计算代价高、脆弱且语义对齐困难。

Method: 提出ETL（嵌入时序逻辑），通过定义观测嵌入与参考嵌入之间的距离谓词来进行监控；引入有界嵌入轨迹的ETL监控器；提供基于共形校准的可靠性与安全导向谓词评估程序。

Result: 在多个操控环境中验证，ETL与真值语义达成强经验一致性，包括对时间组合行为的高精度监控。

Conclusion: ETL通过在嵌入空间中直接执行监控，实现了与传统方法相当的语义一致性验证，特别是在处理时间组合行为方面表现出色。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring of autonomous systems traditionally relies on mapping continuous sensor observations to discrete logical propositions defined over low-dimensional state variables. This abstraction breaks down in perception-driven settings, where such mappings require additional learned modules that are often computationally expensive, brittle, and semantically misaligned. In this work, we propose Embedding Temporal Logic (ETL), a temporal logic that performs monitoring directly in learned embedding spaces. ETL defines predicates through distances between observed embeddings and target embeddings derived from reference observations. This formulation allows specifications to capture high-level perceptual concepts, such as similarity to visual goals or avoidance of semantic regions, that are difficult or impossible to express using traditional predicates. By composing these predicates with temporal operators, ETL naturally expresses temporally extended and sequential perceptual behaviors. We introduce ETL monitors for evaluating specifications over bounded embedding traces, along with a conformal calibration procedure that provides reliable and safety-oriented predicate evaluation. We evaluate our approach across multiple manipulation environments to show that ETL achieves strong empirical agreement with ground-truth semantics, including accurate monitoring of temporally composed behaviors.

</details>


### [93] [Do Fair Models Reason Fairly? Counterfactual Explanation Consistency for Procedural Fairness in Credit Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12701)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了"Regime B"（相同结果但不同推理）作为现有公平性指标的盲点，提出CEC框架通过反事实解释一致性来检测和缓解隐藏的程序偏差，在多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性指标仅关注预测结果的均等化，无法保证模型对不同群体使用相同的推理方式，导致"隐藏的程序偏差"成为被忽视的关键盲点。

Method: CEC框架通过最近邻反事实生成方法、改进的积分梯度比较基线、个体级程序公平性度量及其对应的训练损失来检测和缓解隐藏的程序偏差。

Result: 实验表明，即使满足结果公平性的基线模型仍存在显著的隐藏偏差，而CEC能够在保持适度效用成本的前提下大幅减少该偏差。

Conclusion: CEC能够以适度的效用成本显著减少隐藏的程序偏差，为公平性评估提供了一种新的视角。

Abstract: Machine learning algorithms in socially sensitive domains (e.g., credit decisions) often focus on equalizing predictive outcomes. However, satisfying these metrics does not guarantee that models use the same reasoning for different groups. We show that existing outcome-fair models can still apply fundamentally different reasoning to individuals, a ``hidden procedural bias'' missed by standard fairness metrics and algorithms. We propose Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC), a framework that detects and mitigates this bias by aligning feature attributions between individuals and their counterfactual counterparts. Key contributions include a nearest-neighbor counterfactual generation method, a modified baseline for integrated gradient comparisons, an individual-level procedural fairness metric, and a corresponding training loss. We introduce a taxonomy identifying ``Regime B'' (same outcome, different reasoning) as a critical blind spot. Experiments on synthetic data, German Credit, Adult Income, and HMDA mortgage data demonstrate that outcome-fair baselines exhibit substantial hidden bias, while CEC substantially reduces it with modest utility cost.

</details>


### [94] [Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13810)
*Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Dmitry Krachun,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于随机Hadamard变换的抖动量化方法，用于向量量化。该方法通过在Hadamard变换后添加随机标量偏移来注入额外随机性，以极低开销提升量化性能。理论证明该方法是无偏的，且均方误差界限渐近等价于使用真正随机旋转矩阵所能达到的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机Hadamard变换方法虽将计算复杂度从Θ(d²)降至O(d log d)，但其离散结构导致理论分析困难，压缩保证较弱或仅依赖经验验证。需要一种既能保持高效计算又能获得严格理论保证的方法。

Method: 采用抖动量化（dithered quantization）结合单次随机Hadamard变换：1) 对输入向量应用Hadamard变换；2) 在量化前减去随机标量偏移以注入额外随机性；3) 标准标量量化。研究证明该方法是无偏估计量。

Result: 抖动版TurboQuant在每坐标b比特下达到均方误差(π√3/2 + o(1)) · 4^(-b)，其中o(1)项在高数量化级别下对所有单位向量和所有维度一致消失。理论上证明了该方法的MSE界限渐近匹配使用真正随机旋转矩阵所能获得的性能。

Conclusion: 本文桥接了高效Hadamard方法与严格理论保证之间的鸿沟，为相似性搜索、联邦学习和KV缓存压缩等应用提供了兼具效率与理论严谨性的量化方案。

Abstract: Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees.
  In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.

</details>


### [95] [Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation via Peer Successes and Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12652)
*Weichen Yu,Xiaomin Li,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Haixin Wang,Yinyi Luo,Chen Henry Wu,Gaurav Mittal,Matt Fredrikson,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOPD提出了一种利用学生多 rollout 群体信息的新型在线蒸馏框架，通过条件化教师模型同时学习成功和失败的同伴轨迹，产生更丰富的token级监督信号，在编程、数学、科学问答和工具使用等多个基准上持续优于标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线蒸馏方法对每个 rollout 独立进行蒸馏，忽略了同一 prompt 下采样的其他尝试提供的信息，导致监督信号不够密集和有效。

Method: MOPD采用同伴条件蒸馏框架：1) 正向同伴模仿——让教师学习成功轨迹的推理模式；2) 对比成功-失败条件——让教师同时获取正向和负向证据，明确哪些模式是有效的、哪些是需要避免的。核心创新在于利用学生的本地 rollout 群组来构建更具信息量的教师信号。

Result: 在竞争性编程、数学推理、科学问答和工具使用基准上的实验表明，MOPD始终优于标准在线蒸馏基线。教师信号分析显示，混合成功-失败上下文能更好地将教师评分与验证器奖励对齐，说明收益来源于更忠实、实例自适应的监督。

Conclusion: 有效的在线蒸馏应该利用学生的多 rollout 试错行为，而非将 rollouts 视为独立样本。peer-conditioned 方法通过整合成功和失败的同伴信息，产生了更密集、更可靠的 token 级监督信号。

Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained with sparse verifier rewards, which indicate whether a sampled trajectory succeeds but provide limited guidance about where reasoning succeeds or fails. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers denser token-level supervision by training on student-generated trajectories, yet existing methods typically distill each rollout independently and ignore the other attempts sampled for the same prompt. We introduce Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), a peer-conditioned distillation framework that uses the student's local rollout group to construct more informative teacher signals. MOPD conditions the teacher on both successful and failed peer rollouts: successes provide positive evidence for valid reasoning patterns, while failures provide structured negative evidence about plausible mistakes to avoid. We study two peer-context constructions: positive peer imitation and contrastive success-failure conditioning. Experiments on competitive programming, mathematical reasoning, scientific question answering, and tool-use benchmarks show that MOPD consistently improves over standard on-policy baselines. Further teacher-signal analysis shows that mixed success-failure contexts better align teacher scores with verifier rewards, indicating that the gains arise from more faithful, instance-adaptive supervision. These results indicate that effective on-policy distillation should exploit the student's multi-rollout trial-and-error behavior rather than treating rollouts as isolated samples.

</details>


### [96] [Plan Before You Trade: Inference-Time Optimization for RL Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12653)
*Eun Go,Rohan Deb,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FPILOT是一个金融插件推理时优化框架，将模型预测控制（MPC）思想融入强化学习组合管理。核心创新点在于利用价格预测轨迹而非传统强化学习的动作条件展开来优化策略，实现推理时的即时适配，且兼容任意预训练智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习组合管理智能体以静态策略形式训练和部署，无法在推理时利用价格预测信息。这限制了智能体适应市场变化的能力，因为市场预测与策略优化是分离的。

Method: 提出FPILOT框架，核心洞察是未来价格主要由市场因素决定而非单一智能体的仓位配置。利用预测模型生成多步价格轨迹（无需迭代式动作条件展开），在每个决策点根据预测轨迹构建基于分配的收益目标函数，推理时优化策略后执行一步交易。框架兼容任意预训练智能体，无需重训练即可适配预测器。

Result: 在TradeMaster DJ30基准上针对五种策略学习算法进行评估，结果显示：总收益和风险调整指标（Sharpe、Sortino、Calmar比）均获得一致提升；随机策略比确定性策略受益更多；使用校准质量的合成预测验证，收益随预测质量提升而持续改善。

Conclusion: FPILOT作为通用插件框架，可无缝集成现有预训练智能体。随着金融预测技术的进步，该方法性能上限有望进一步提升，为强化学习在量化交易中的应用提供了推理时自适应的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents for portfolio management are typically trained and deployed as static policies, with no mechanism for using price forecasts at inference time. We propose $\text{FPILOT}$ (**Fin**ancial **P**lugin **I**nference-time **L**earning for **O**ptimal **T**rading), a plugin inference-time optimization framework inspired by Model Predictive Control (MPC). Our key structural insight is that future prices mostly do not depend on one agent's portfolio allocation, so a suitable predictive model can produce a multi-step price trajectory without iterative action-conditioned rollouts as in typical reinforcement learning. At each decision step, we use the forecaster's predicted price trajectory to construct an allocation-based imagined return objective, and optimize the policy at inference-time before executing one step of the trade. Our framework is compatible with any pre-trained agent and adapts the policy to the forecaster's predictions without any retraining. Evaluated across five policy learning algorithms on the TradeMaster DJ30 benchmark, $\text{FPILOT}$ produces consistent improvements in total return and return-based risk-adjusted metrics (Sharpe, Sortino, Calmar), with stochastic policies benefiting more than deterministic ones. Further, using synthetic forecasts at calibrated quality levels, we show that gains consistently improve with forecaster quality, suggesting that our performance will improve based on advances in financial forecasting.

</details>


### [97] [scShapeBench: Discovering geometry from high dimensional scRNAseq data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12662)
*Andrew J Steindl,João Felipe Rocha,Brian Tshilengi Di Bassinga,Zachary Warren,Matthew Scicluna,César Miguel Valdez Córdova,Shabarni Gupta,Leire Torices,Daniel Neumann,Timothy J. Mann,Ihuan Gunawan,Dhananjay Bhaskar,John G Lock,Christine L Chaffer,Guy Wolf,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了scShapeBench基准数据集和scReebTower方法，用于自动检测单细胞数据的拓扑结构（聚类、轨迹、分支等），以自动化选择下游分析流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维点云数据（如单细胞数据）具有不同的拓扑结构（聚类、轨迹、原始型等），现有分析流程需要人工预先假设数据结构，导致分析策略选择依赖生物信息学家的主观判断。随着AI科学家的自动化需求增长，自动化形状检测变得至关重要。

Method: 1) 构建scShapeBench基准数据集，包含合成数据（从真实骨架图采样）和专家标注的真实单细胞数据（分为聚类、单轨迹、多分支、原始型四类）；2) 提出scReebTower基线方法，利用扩散几何提取Reeb图，将拓扑结构与可视化及流程选择相连接；3) 设计拓扑感知评估指标。

Result: scReebTower在合成数据和真实数据上均优于PAGA和Mapper等现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文贡献涵盖基准数据集、评估指标和自动化形状检测基线方法，为单细胞数据的智能分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: High-dimensional point cloud data arise across many scientific domains, especially single-cell biology. The shapes or topologies of these datasets determine the types of information that can be extracted. For example, clustered data supports cell-type identification, trajectory structures support transition analysis, and archetypal structures capture continua of cellular behaviors. Existing analysis pipelines often assume a specific shape. The standard Seurat pipeline combines UMAP visualization with Louvain clustering and therefore assumes clustered data, while tools such as Monocle and SPADE assume tree-like structures, and flow-based models such as MIOFlow and Conditional Flow Matching target trajectories. Choosing which pipeline to apply is therefore often left to bioinformaticians who visually inspect datasets before selecting an analysis strategy. With the rise of agentic AI scientists, automating shape detection is increasingly important for selecting downstream analysis pipelines. To address this problem, we introduce scShapeBench, a benchmark dataset for shape detection containing both synthetic and expert-annotated single-cell datasets. Synthetic datasets are sampled from ground-truth skeleton graphs with controlled variance. Real single-cell datasets are curated from diverse sources and annotated by experts into four categories: clusters, single trajectory, multi-branching, and archetypal. We additionally introduce scReebTower, a baseline method that uses diffusion geometry to extract Reeb graphs and connect visualization with pipeline selection. We provide topology-aware evaluation metrics and compare scReebTower against PAGA and Mapper on synthetic and real data. Our results indicate that scReebTower outperforms existing baselines. Overall, our contributions span benchmarks, evaluation metrics, and a baseline for automated shape detection in single-cell data.

</details>


### [98] [ODRPO: Ordinal Decompositions of Discrete Rewards for Robust Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12667)
*Nirmal Patel,Fei Wang,Inderjit Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLAIF中自动评分器的随机性问题，提出ODRPO框架，通过将离散奖励序数分解为二元指标序列来结构性地隔离评估噪声，在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升了策略优化稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型对齐的RLAIF场景中，自动评分器存在固有的随机性（源于提示敏感性和采样随机性），导致采样噪声会传播并破坏标准优势估计器（如GRPO和MaxRL），使归一化统计量偏移，全局学习信号退化。虽然可通过多次采样和多数投票降低噪声，但计算成本高昂。

Method: 将离散奖励（如1-10评分）分解为一系列序数二元指标（即按阈值逐步二值化），对每个成功阈值独立计算和累积优势，从而在结构上隔离评估噪声，防止异常值评价污染全局更新，并隐式建立方差感知的课程学习。

Result: ODRPO在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上取得了稳健的性能提升，在FACTS-grounding-v2任务上相对提升达14.8%，在Alpaca-Evals上提升7.5%，且在不增加每步额外计算量的情况下实现了这些改进。

Conclusion: ODRPO通过将离散奖励分解为序数二元指标序列，在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上实现了稳健的性能提升，在FACTS-grounding-v2上提升达14.8%，在Alpaca-Evals上提升7.5%，且训练时间开销可忽略不计，同时得到理论分析支持其优化稳定性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) utilizes Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback (RLAIF) for non-verifiable domains such as long-form question answering and open-ended instruction following. These domains often rely on LLM based auto-raters to provide granular, multi-tier discrete rewards (e.g., 1-10 rubrics) that are inherently stochastic due to prompt sensitivity and sampling randomness. We empirically verify the stochasticity of auto-raters that can propagate and corrupt standard advantage estimators like GRPO and MaxRL, as a noisy reward samples can skew normalization statistics and degrade the global learning signal. Empirically, sampling more rewards and taking majority voting may reduce the noise and improve performance, but this approach is computationally expensive. To address this bottleneck, we introduce $\textbf{O}$rdinal $\textbf{D}$ecomposition for $\textbf{R}$obust $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{O}$ptimization ($\textbf{ODRPO}$), a framework that structurally isolates evaluation noise by decomposing discrete rewards into a sequence of ordinal binary indicators. By independently computing and accumulating advantages across these progressively challenging success thresholds, ODRPO prevents outlier evaluations from corrupting the global update while establishing an implicit, variance-aware learning curriculum. Empirically, ODRPO achieves robust performance on Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-4B models, outperforming baselines with relative improvements of upto 14.8% on FACTS-grounding-v2 and 7.5% on Alpaca-Evals. Critically, these gains are achieved with negligible training-time overhead, as ODRPO requires no additional compute per step compared to standard estimators. Supported by theoretical analysis confirming its optimization stability, ODRPO provides a scalable and robust framework for aligning models within the noisy, discrete evaluation landscape of modern RLAIF.

</details>


### [99] [Rescaled Asynchronous SGD: Optimal Distributed Optimization under Data and System Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13434)
*Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD) is a standard way to exploit heterogeneous compute resources in distributed learning: instead of forcing fast workers to wait for slow ones, the server updates the model whenever a gradient arrives. Vanilla ASGD applies each arriving gradient with the same weight. When local data distributions are heterogeneous, this becomes problematic: faster workers contribute more updates, and we show theoretically that the method is biased toward a frequency-weighted average of the local objectives rather than the desired global objective. Existing remedies typically move away from the simple ASGD template by introducing gathering phases, buffering, or extra memory. We show that this is unnecessary. Keeping the standard ASGD mechanism, we recover the correct objective by rescaling worker-specific stepsizes in proportion to their computation times, so that each worker contributes the same aggregate learning rate over a cycle. In the non-convex setting, under smoothness and bounded heterogeneity assumptions, we prove that the resulting method, Rescaled ASGD, converges to stationary points of the correct global objective in the fixed-computation model. Its time complexity matches the known lower bound in the leading term, while the effects of staleness and data heterogeneity appear only in lower-order terms. Experiments confirm that the method converges to the correct objective and is competitive with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>
