We evaluate IV-ICL on synthetic and semi-synthetic IV benchmarks and show it produces intervals that are more reliably valid and more informative compared to efficient semi-parametric, Bayesian, and plug-in baselines, at 20-500x lower inference time. Beyond methodology, we propose a procedure to convert randomized controlled trials into IV benchmarks with provably preserved ground-truth causal effects that enables a more realistic evaluation of partial-identification methods.

</details>


### [133] [The Efficiency Gap in Byte Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12928)
*Celine Lee,Jing Nathan Yan,Chen Liang,Jiaxin Shi,Yin Zhang,Jeremiah Liu,Pengcheng Yin,Fernando Pereira,Ed Chi,Derek Cheng,Alexander M. Rush,Ruoxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一项计算匹配的扩展研究揭示，字节级建模对掩码扩散模型的影响比对自回归模型的影响更大，性能惩罚更严重。这种差异源于"上下文脆弱性"——AR 的因果历史有助于自然重新发现子词模式，而 MDM 破坏了字节级语义解析所需的局部连续性。未来模态无关设计需要替代性结构先验来维持字节 regime 中的可行扩展轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型长期依赖两个主导设计选择：子词标记化（subword tokenization）和自回归（AR）排序。这些设计决策内置的先验会决定模型的学习方式。两种替代范式（字节级建模和掩码扩散建模 MDM）的交叉代表了一个完全端到端的模态无关生成原型，但移除这些结构先验会带来显著的计算成本。

Method: 计算匹配扩展研究（compute-matched scaling study）和受控置换实验（controlled permutation experiments）。

Result: 字节建模的性能惩罚并不均匀；在扩展过程中，字节建模的扩展开销对 MDM 的影响比对 AR 的影响更大。差异源于"上下文脆弱性"：虽然 AR 稳定的因果历史允许模型自然地重新发现子词模式，但 MDM 目标破坏了从原始字节有效解析语义所需的局部连续性。

Conclusion: 未来模态无关的设计必须纳入替代性结构先验，以在字节 regime 中保持可行的扩展轨迹。

Abstract: Modern language models have historically relied on two dominant design choices: subword tokenization and autoregressive (AR) ordering. These design decisions bake in priors that dictate a model's learning. Recently, two alternative paradigms have challenged this: byte-level modeling, which bypasses static statistically-derived token vocabularies, and masked diffusion modeling (MDM), which conducts parallel, non-sequential generation. Their intersection represents a fully end-to-end modality-agnostic generative prototype; however, removing these structural priors incurs a significant computational cost. In this work, we investigate this cost through a compute-matched scaling study. Our results reveal that the performance penalty of byte modeling is not uniform; across scale, the scaling overhead of byte modeling is worse for MDM than for AR. We hypothesize that this disparity stems from context fragility: while AR's stable causal history allows models to naturally rediscover subword patterns, the MDM objective destroys the local contiguity required to efficiently resolve semantics from raw bytes. Our findings from controlled permutation experiments suggest that future modality-agnostic designs must incorporate alternative structural biases to maintain viable scaling trajectories in the byte regime.

</details>


### [134] [The Expressivity Boundary of Probabilistic Circuits: A Comparison with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12940)
*Zhiyu Zhao,Xuejie Liu,Muhan Zhang,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在统一的自回归框架下，系统性地分析了概率电路(PCs)在语言建模中落后于LLMs的根本原因，发现了两大瓶颈：输出瓶颈（概率空间参数化难以表达语言的尖锐分布）和上下文编码瓶颈（固定路由结构限制了对异构依赖拓扑的适应性），并通过理论证明和实验验证了这些问题，同时指出可分解PCs具有更强的表达能力但优化困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) 虽然支持精确高效的概率推理，但在自回归语言建模中仍落后于基于Transformer的大型语言模型(LLMs)，提示存在重要的表达力差距，需要深入理解其根本原因。

Method: 采用统一的自回归公式框架进行比较分析；通过理论证明（分离秩匹配、表达能力强弱关系）和实证实验两种方式验证问题；针对输出瓶颈采用logit空间参数化进行改进验证。

Result: 1. 输出瓶颈：采用logit空间参数化可显著缩小差距；2. 上下文编码瓶颈：结构可分解PCs只能在固定路由结构对齐的分区上匹配Transformer的分离秩，当数据呈现异构依赖拓扑时会严重退化；3. 理论上证明可分解PCs比结构可分解PCs表达能力更强，但有效优化仍是开放挑战。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了PCs在自回归语言建模中落后于LLMs的两个关键瓶颈，并提供了理论证明和实证支持，为未来PCs的改进指明了方向（需解决异构依赖拓扑问题和优化可分解PCs的表达能力）。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are deep generative models that support exact and efficient probabilistic inference. Yet in autoregressive language modeling, PCs still lag behind Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), suggesting an important expressivity gap. In this work, we compare PCs and LLMs under a unified autoregressive formulation. First, an output bottleneck: PCs parameterize predictions as convex combinations in probability space, which struggles to represent the sharp distributions typical of language; adopting a logit-space parameterization substantially narrows this gap. Second, a context-encoding bottleneck: we prove that structured-decomposable PCs can match Transformer separation rank on vtree-aligned partitions, but show, both theoretically and empirically, that this capacity is limited to partitions aligned with the fixed routing structure, leading to severe degradation when the data exhibits heterogeneous dependency topologies. We further prove that decomposable PCs are strictly more expressive than structured-decomposable ones, though effectively optimizing them remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [135] [From Instance Selection to Fixed-Pool Data Recipe Search for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12944)
*Haodong Wu,Jiahao Zhang,Lijie Hu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 SFT 数据选择从实例排序问题重新建模为固定池数据配方搜索问题，提出了 AutoSelection 双层求解器框架，通过解耦固定池材料化与昂贵全量评估，在有限评估预算下高效发现高质量训练子集。实验验证了该方法在分布内外的有效性，并揭示了数据配方结构比单一选择算子更具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SFT 数据选择仅关注实例排序，忽视了实际训练中过滤、混合、去重等操作的协同效应，无法捕捉完整数据配方的效果，因此需要将问题重新建模为固定池数据配方搜索。

Method: 提出了 AutoSelection 双层求解器：利用缓存的任务侧、数据侧和模型侧信号进行固定池材料化，结合预热探测、贪心搜索、高斯过程辅助排序和停滞触发重播种等策略，在有限的全量 SFT 评估预算内高效搜索最优配方。

Result: 在 90K 指令池上，AutoSelection 在三个基座模型上均达到最强的分布内推理平均性能，优于全量数据训练、随机配方搜索、随机 top-k 及单算子选择器；分布外图推理任务、搜索稳定性分析、结构消融和 1.5B 到 7B 规模迁移验证进一步表明，配方结构本身的重要性超越单一选择算子。

Conclusion: AutoSelection 通过双层求解器框架，在固定池材料化与全量评估之间建立解耦机制，显著提升了 SFT 数据选择的效率与效果，证明数据配方结构本身比单一选择算子更具价值。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data selection is commonly formulated as instance ranking: score each example and retain a top-$k$ subset. However, effective SFT training subsets are often produced through ordered curation recipes, where filtering, mixing, and deduplication operators jointly shape the final data distribution. We formulate this problem as fixed-pool data recipe search: given a raw instruction pool and a library of grounded operators, the goal is to discover an executable recipe that constructs a high-quality selected subset under a limited budget of full SFT evaluations, without generating, rewriting, or augmenting training samples. We introduce AutoSelection, a two-layer solver that decouples fixed-pool materialization based on cached task-, data-, and model-side signals from expensive full evaluation, using warmup probes, realized subset states, local recipe edits, Gaussian-process-assisted ranking, and stagnation-triggered reseeding. Experiments on a 90K instruction pool show that AutoSelection achieves the strongest in-distribution reasoning average across three base models, outperforming full-data training, random recipe search, random top-$k$, and single-operator selectors. Additional Out-of-distribution graph-reasoning results, search-stability analyses, structural ablations, and 1.5B-to-7B transfer checks further show that recipe structure matters beyond individual selection operators. Code is available at https://github.com/w253/AutoSelection.

</details>


### [136] [Separating Shortcut Transition from Cross-Family OOD Failure in a Minimal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12945)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个包含不变特征和快捷方式特征的最小二元模型，解析地证明了训练相关性、学习到的快捷方式使用和OOD失败之间并不必然一致，发现岭正则化在确定性情况下可防止OOD失败，而当不变特征有噪声时，训练侧的快捷方式规则转换会导致保留测试集上的失败，但失败模式取决于保留家族的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究混淆了训练相关性、学习到的快捷方式使用与测试时失败这三个不同概念，需要一个最小化模型来分离这些现象并理解它们之间的真实关系。

Method: 构建了一个最小二元模型，包含一个不变坐标和一个家族依赖的快捷方式坐标；采用logistic ERM（经验风险最小化）和岭正则化；通过解析推导和合成实验验证不同制度下的行为。

Result: 在确定性情况下，正的快捷方式相关性会将logistic ERM拉向正的快捷方式权重，但岭正则化保持分类器以不变特征为主导；在不变坐标有噪声时，岭-logistic ERM会在训练快捷方式信号超过不变信号时切换到快捷方式规则；这种转换是否导致失败取决于保留家族的特性——较弱的快捷方式相关性产生正的额外风险，符号反转的家族产生高于随机水平的错误。

Conclusion: Shortcut attraction、shortcut规则转换和跨家族OOD失败这三个阶段可以被明确区分开来；弱快捷方式相关性和符号反转的保留家族会导致不同的失败模式，而岭正则化可以在确定性情况下防止OOD失败。

Abstract: Shortcut features are often invoked to explain out-of-distribution (OOD) failure, but training correlation, learned shortcut use, and test-time failure need not coincide. We study a minimal binary model with one invariant coordinate and one family-dependent shortcut coordinate. In the deterministic regime, positive average shortcut correlation pulls logistic ERM toward positive shortcut weight, but ridge regularization keeps the classifier invariant-dominated and prevents deterministic OOD failure. When the invariant coordinate is noisy, ridge-logistic ERM switches to the shortcut rule once the training shortcut signal exceeds the invariant signal. Whether that transition causes failure depends on the held-out family: weaker shortcut correlation yields positive excess risk, and sign-flipped families yield above-chance error. Synthetic checks match these analytic regimes and show that the same training-side transition can have different held-out consequences. The model separates shortcut attraction, shortcut-rule transition, and cross-family OOD failure.

</details>


### [137] [U-HNO: A U-shaped Hybrid Neural Operator with Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing for Non-stationary PDE Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12965)
*Yingzhe Ma,Xiao Yang,Yuxin Xie,Zihan Xiong,Jinliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: U-HNO is a U-shaped hybrid neural operator that uses Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing (SPAR) to dynamically select between global Fourier and local Gaussian branches at each spatial location based on local feature contrast. It achieves SOTA performance across multiple PDE benchmarks, especially on problems with sharp localized features.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many PDE solutions exhibit coexisting smooth global transport and localized sharp features (shock fronts, thin interfaces, concentrated high-frequency content) within single trajectories. Existing neural operators face a tradeoff: Fourier-based architectures efficiently handle non-local interactions but under-resolve localized non-smooth features; spatially local architectures recover fine details but sacrifice long-range propagation and rollout stability. Current hybrid operators use fixed spatially uniform fusion, forcing the same trade-off everywhere without adapting to local feature characteristics.

Method: The paper proposes U-HNO with Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing (SPAR): a per-pixel hard mask at each spatial location selects whether the global Fourier branch or local multi-scale Gaussian branch dominates, with sparsity ratio as a function of local contrast in the routing signal. This is embedded in a hierarchical encoder-bottleneck-decoder backbone with skip connections, where dual branches and gate operate at every resolution. Training combines pointwise supervision with a finite-difference H^1 gradient term and a band-wise spectral consistency regularizer.

Result: U-HNO achieves state-of-the-art rollout accuracy on the majority of benchmarks (1D Burgers, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, KdV, 2D advection, Allen-Cahn, Navier-Stokes, Darcy flow, and 3D transonic compressible Navier-Stokes from PDEBench) in both relative L^2 and H^1 metrics, with the largest gains on problems dominated by sharp localized features.

Conclusion: Ablation studies confirm that removing any single component substantially degrades rollout error, validating the necessity of each design element in U-HNO.

Abstract: Solutions to many partial differential equations (PDEs) display coexisting smooth global transport and localized sharp features within a single trajectory: shock fronts, thin interfaces, and concentrated high-frequency content sit on top of slowly varying backgrounds. This poses a challenge for neural operators: Fourier-based architectures mix nonlocal interactions efficiently but tend to under-resolve localized non-smooth features, whereas spatially local architectures recover fine detail at the cost of long-range propagation and rollout stability. Existing hybrid operators paper over this tension with a fixed, spatially uniform fusion that forces the same trade-off everywhere.
  We propose U-HNO, a U-shaped hybrid neural operator whose central design is Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing (SPAR): at every spatial location, a per-pixel hard mask selects whether the global Fourier branch or the local multi-scale Gaussian branch should dominate, and the sparsity ratio is a function of the local contrast of the routing signal, so smooth and shock-aligned regions receive different mixtures of global and local computation. SPAR is embedded in a hierarchical encoder-bottleneck-decoder backbone with skip connections so that the dual branches and the gate operate at every resolution. Training combines pointwise supervision with a finite-difference H^1 gradient term and a band-wise spectral consistency regularizer.
  Across benchmarks spanning 1D Burgers, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, KdV, 2D advection, Allen-Cahn, Navier-Stokes, Darcy flow, and 3D transonic compressible Navier-Stokes from PDEBench, U-HNO achieves state-of-the-art rollout accuracy on the majority of tasks in both relative L^2 and H^1 metrics, with the largest gains on problems dominated by sharp localized features. Ablations show that removing any single component substantially degrades rollout error.

</details>


### [138] [Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12968)
*Hisashi Miyashita,Mgnite Inc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数本体投影(AOP)方法，通过将LLM隐藏状态投影到有限域F2中，在不使用模型微调的情况下实现高达93.33%的零样本包含关系推理准确率，并揭示了LLM内部存在可形式化验证的代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)是否在内部以形式化可验证的代数结构编码本体论关系？现有研究多将LLM的逻辑推理视为隐式近似，缺乏可形式化验证的方法论。

Method: 提出代数本体投影(AOP)方法：(1)将LLM隐藏状态投影至伽罗瓦域F2；(2)应用Liskov替换原则约束；(3)仅使用42对关系对作为代数密钥；(4)通过提示工程实现零样本推理，无需模型微调。

Result: 在Gemma-2 Instruct模型上达到93.33%零样本准确率，多个模型家族保持86.67%一致准确率；语义结晶(SC)指标可预测零样本准确率；系统提示作为代数边界条件，与指令微调协同防止"后期层崩溃"现象（7/10条件下观察到此退化）。

Conclusion: LLM的前向计算可重新理解为迭代代数组织过程，为构建形式化可验证逻辑结构的LLM提供了理论基础和方法路径。

Abstract: Do large language models internally encode ontological relations in a formally verifiable algebraic structure? We introduce Algebraic Ontology Projection (AOP), which projects LLM hidden states into the Galois Field F2 under Liskov Substitution Principle constraints, using only 42 relational pairs as algebraic keys. AOP achieves up to 93.33% zero-shot inclusion accuracy on unseen concept pairs (Gemma-2 Instruct with optimized prompt), with consistent 86.67% accuracy observed across multiple model families -- with no model tuning, but through prompt alone.
  This algebraic structure is strongly layer-dependent. We introduce Semantic Crystallisation (SC), a metric that quantifies F2 constraint satisfaction relative to a random baseline and predicts zero-shot accuracy without held-out data. System prompts act as algebraic boundary conditions: only their combination with instruction tuning prevents Late-layer Collapse -- a systematic degradation of logical consistency in the final layers, observed in 7 of 10 conditions. These findings reframe forward computation as an iterative process of algebraic organisation, and open a path toward LLMs whose logical structure is not merely approximated, but formally accessible.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Think Twice, Act Once: Verifier-Guided Action Selection For Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12620)
*Nishad Singhi,Christian Bialas,Snehal Jauhri,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VeGAS框架，通过在测试时引入生成式验证器来改进多模态大语言模型(MLLM)具身智能体的鲁棒性。核心挑战是直接使用现成MLLM作为验证器无效，因此采用LLM驱动的失败案例数据合成策略来训练验证器。在Habitat和ALFRED环境中，该方法在复杂长视野任务上实现最高36%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based具身智能体在分布外(out-of-distribution)场景下表现脆弱，尽管具备视觉-语言知识和思维链(CoT)推理能力，但单次解码动作的策略缺乏可靠性保障。

Method: 提出测试时(Test-time)框架VeGAS：1)采样多个候选动作形成动作集成；2)使用生成式验证器评估并选择最可靠动作；3)设计LLM驱动的数据合成策略，自动构建失败案例课程用于训练验证器。核心洞察是现成MLLM作为验证器无法提升性能，必须通过针对性训练。

Result: 在Habitat和ALFRED两个具身推理基准上，VeGAS持续提升泛化能力，在最具挑战性的多目标、长视野任务上相比强CoT基线实现最高36%相对性能提升。

Conclusion: 验证器引导的动作选择是提升MLLM具身智能体鲁棒性的有效途径；失败案例的针对性训练对于构建有效验证器至关重要；该框架无需修改底层策略即可实现性能提升。

Abstract: Building generalist embodied agents capable of solving complex real-world tasks remains a fundamental challenge in AI. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of such agents through strong vision-language knowledge and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, yet remain brittle when faced with challenging out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this, we propose Verifier-Guided Action Selection (VegAS), a test-time framework designed to improve the robustness of MLLM-based embodied agents through an explicit verification step. At inference time, rather than committing to a single decoded action, VeGAS samples an ensemble of candidate actions and uses a generative verifier to identify the most reliable choice, without modifying the underlying policy. Crucially, we find that using an MLLM off-the-shelf as a verifier yields no improvement, motivating our LLM-driven data synthesis strategy, which automatically constructs a diverse curriculum of failure cases to expose the verifier to a rich distribution of potential errors at training time. Across embodied reasoning benchmarks spanning the Habitat and ALFRED environments, VeGAS consistently improves generalization, achieving up to a 36% relative performance gain over strong CoT baselines on the most challenging multi-object, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [140] [Macro-Action Based Multi-Agent Instruction Following through Value Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12655)
*Wo Wei Lin,Ethan Rathbun,Enrico Marchesini Xiang Zhi Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAVIC是一种解决多智能体强化学习中指令干扰问题的算法，通过在指令边界修正Bellman备份目标来解决价值估计不一致问题，实现指令遵从与基础任务性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中的多智能体强化学习需要适应外部自然语言指令，这些指令会中断当前行为并与长期目标冲突。现有方法在指令中断宏动作时存在根本性失败模式：Bellman更新跨指令上下文耦合，导致价值估计不一致。

Method: 在指令边界处修正Bellman备份，纠正传入的指令目标并恢复当前目标下的延续值。与奖励塑形不同，MAVIC直接修改自举目标本身，并提供了Actor-Critic实现。

Result: 在日益复杂的多智能体合作环境中，MAVIC实现了高指令遵从度，同时保持了基础任务性能。

Conclusion: MAVIC通过修正引导目标实现了在指令随机切换下的一致性价值估计，在保持基础任务性能的同时实现了高指令遵从度。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in real-world use cases may need to adapt to external natural language instructions that interrupt ongoing behavior and conflict with long-horizon objectives. However, conditioning rewards on instructions introduces a fundamental failure mode as Bellman updates couple value estimates across instruction contexts, leading to inconsistent values when instructions interrupt macro-actions. We propose Macro-Action Value Correction for Instruction Compliance (MAVIC), which corrects Bellman backups at instruction boundaries by correcting the incoming instruction objective and restoring the continuation value under the current objective. Unlike reward shaping, MAVIC modifies the bootstrapping target itself, enabling consistent value estimation under stochastic instruction switching within a unified policy. We provide theoretical analysis and an actor-critic implementation, and show that MAVIC achieves high instruction compliance while preserving base task performance in increasingly complex cooperative multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [141] [Do Androids Dream of Breaking the Game? Systematically Auditing AI Agent Benchmarks with BenchJack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12673)
*Hao Wang,Hanchen Li,Qiuyang Mang,Alvin Cheung,Koushik Sen,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前前沿AI智能体基准测试存在严重的安全漏洞——智能体可以通过"奖励黑客"（reward hacking）在不完成任务的情况下获得近乎完美的分数。作者提出了BenchJack系统，能够自动化审计基准测试并发现219个不同类型的漏洞，并通过迭代对抗流程将可被黑客攻击的任务比例从近100%降至10%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿AI模型的评估严重依赖基准测试，但这些基准测试容易受到"奖励黑客"攻击——智能体通过非预期方式最大化分数而非真正完成任务。这种漏洞在未过拟合的前沿模型中也会自发出现，需要从设计阶段就确保基准测试的安全性。

Method: 1）从历史奖励黑客事件中归纳出8类重复性缺陷模式，形成Agent-Eval Checklist；2）开发BenchJack自动化红队系统，以有远见（clairvoyant）方式驱动编码智能体审计基准测试；3）扩展为迭代生成-对抗流程，持续发现新漏洞并自动修补。实验覆盖10个主流智能体基准测试，涵盖软件工程、网页导航、桌面计算和终端操作等领域。

Result: BenchJack在大多数基准测试上发现了可导致接近满分但实际未完成任何任务的奖励黑客漏洞，共识别出8类共219个独特缺陷。迭代对抗流程将4个基准测试的可被黑客攻击任务比例从接近100%降至10%以下，WebArena和OSWorld在3次迭代内完全修复。

Conclusion: 当前评估流程缺乏对抗性思维定式，主动式审计可以有效填补快速发展的基准测试领域的安全缺口。基准测试设计必须从一开始就将安全性纳入考量。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks have become the de facto measure of frontier AI competence, guiding model selection, investment, and deployment. However, reward hacking, where agents maximize a score without performing the intended task, emerges spontaneously in frontier models without overfitting. We argue that benchmarks must be secure by design. From past incidents of reward hacks, we derive a taxonomy of eight recurring flaw patterns and compile them into the Agent-Eval Checklist for benchmark designers. We condense the insights into BenchJack, an automated red-teaming system that drives coding agents to audit benchmarks and identify possible reward-hacking exploits in a clairvoyant manner. Moreover, we extend BenchJack to an iterative generative-adversarial pipeline that discovers new flaws and patches them iteratively to improve benchmark robustness. We apply BenchJack to 10 popular agent benchmarks spanning software engineering, web navigation, desktop computing, and terminal operations. BenchJack synthesizes reward-hacking exploits that achieve near-perfect scores on most of the benchmarks without solving a single task, surfacing 219 distinct flaws across the eight classes. Moreover, BenchJack's extended pipeline reduces the hackable-task ratio from near 100% to under 10% on four benchmarks without fatal design flaws, fully patching WebArena and OSWorld within three iterations. Our results show that evaluation pipelines have not internalized an adversarial mindset, and that proactive auditing could help close the security gap for the fast-paced benchmarking space.

</details>


### [142] [Revealing Interpretable Failure Modes of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12674)
*Isha Chaudhary,Vedaant V Jain,Kavya Sachdeva,Sayan Ranu,Gagandeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REVELIO是一个用于系统揭示VLMs可解释失败模式的框架，通过组合多样性感知束搜索和高斯过程汤普森采样，在自动驾驶和室内机器人领域发现了SOTA VLMs的先前未报告漏洞，为VLM安全改进提供可操作的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLMs在安全关键应用中的广泛部署，尽管其具有广泛推理能力和最小化任务特定工程的泛化能力，但它们在特定现实场景中可能出现灾难性失败。现有方法缺乏系统性、可解释地识别和理解这些失败模式的机制。

Method: REVELIO框架结合两种搜索策略：(1)多样性感知束搜索(diversity-aware beam search)，用于高效绘制失败景观图；(2)高斯过程汤普森采样策略(Gaussian-process Thompson Sampling)，用于更广泛地探索复杂失败模式。通过将失败模式定义为可解释的领域相关概念组合，在指数级离散的组合空间中搜索。

Result: 在自动驾驶领域，VLMs表现出弱空间定位能力，未能充分考虑主要障碍物，导致模拟碰撞风险增加。在室内机器人任务中，VLMs存在两类问题：遗漏安全危害（不安全性）或行为过度保守（产生误报并降低操作效率）。

Conclusion: REVELIO成功揭示了当前SOTA VLM在自动驾驶和室内机器人领域的多个先前未报告的漏洞，包括空间定位能力弱、对障碍物考虑不足导致模拟碰撞、以及在室内任务中的安全隐患遗漏或过度保守行为，为VLM安全改进提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in safety-critical applications because of their broad reasoning capabilities and ability to generalize with minimal task-specific engineering. Despite these advantages, they can exhibit catastrophic failures in specific real-world situations, constituting failure modes.
  We introduce REVELIO, a framework for systematically uncovering interpretable failure modes in VLMs. We define a failure mode as a composition of interpretable, domain-relevant concepts-such as pedestrian proximity or adverse weather conditions-under which a target VLM consistently behaves incorrectly. Identifying such failures requires searching over an exponentially large discrete combinatorial space. To address this challenge, REVELIO combines two search procedures: a diversity-aware beam search that efficiently maps the failure landscape, and a Gaussian-process Thompson Sampling strategy that enables broader exploration of complex failure modes.
  We apply REVELIO to autonomous driving and indoor robotics domains, uncovering previously unreported vulnerabilities in state-of-the-art VLMs. In driving environments, the models often demonstrate weak spatial grounding and fail to account for major obstructions, leading to recommendations that would result in simulated crashes. In indoor robotics tasks, VLMs either miss safety hazards or behave excessively conservatively, producing false alarms and reducing operational efficiency. By identifying structured and interpretable failure modes, REVELIO offers actionable insights that can support targeted VLM safety improvements.

</details>


### [143] [Learning Transferable Latent User Preferences for Human-Aligned Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12682)
*Alina Hyk,Sandhya Saisubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIPR框架，使大语言模型能够从有限对话中推断用户的隐性偏好，生成可迁移的自然语言规则，通过自适应反馈迭代优化，在多个数据集和用户研究中均优于现有方法，显著提升对齐效果并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models (LLMs) struggle to produce human-aligned solutions in ambiguous situations, and existing approaches to incorporating latent user preferences either rely on extensive user interactions or fail to generalize across tasks and contexts.

Method: CLIPR (Conversational Learning for Inferring Preferences and Reasoning) learns actionable, transferable natural language rules representing latent user preferences from minimal conversational input. These rules are iteratively refined through adaptive feedback and applied to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution ambiguous tasks.

Result: Evaluations on three datasets and a user study demonstrate that CLIPR consistently outperforms existing methods.

Conclusion: CLIPR improves alignment and reduces inference costs compared to existing methods.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as reasoning modules in many applications. While they are efficient in certain tasks, LLMs often struggle to produce human-aligned solutions. Human-aligned decision making requires accounting for both explicitly stated goals and latent user preferences that shape how ambiguous situations should be resolved. Existing approaches to incorporating such preferences either rely on extensive and repeated user interactions or fail to generalize latent preferences across tasks and contexts, limiting their practical applicability. We consider a setting in which an LLM is used for high-level reasoning and is responsible for inferring latent user preferences from limited interactions, which guides downstream decision making. We introduce CLIPR (Conversational Learning for Inferring Preferences and Reasoning), a framework that learns actionable, transferable natural language rules that represent latent user preferences from minimal conversational input. These rules are iteratively refined through adaptive feedback and applied to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution ambiguous tasks across multiple environments. Evaluations on three datasets and a user study show that CLIPR consistently outperforms existing methods in improving alignment and reducing inference costs.

</details>


### [144] [On the Size Complexity and Decidability of First-Order Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12691)
*Jens Classen,Daxin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在 Situation Calculus 框架下，证明了局部效应、正规和非循环动作类的一阶 progression 仅以多项式增长，且当知识库属于可判定片段时，progression 保持可判定性，为动作推理的实际应用提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Progression（ progression，通过更新知识库来反映动作效果）通常需要二阶逻辑，识别其一般一阶特殊情况是动作推理领域的核心课题，但此前缺乏对 progression 大小的系统性分析，这对实际应用至关重要。

Method: 在 Situation Calculus 框架下，对三种动作类（局部效应、正规和非循环动作）的一阶 progression 进行系统性分析。

Result: 在合理假设下，这三种动作类的一阶 progression 仅以多项式增长；当知识库属于可判定片段（如两变量一阶逻辑或带常量的通用理论）时，progression 保持在同一片段内，确保了可判定性。

Conclusion: 该工作确保了局部效应、正规和非循环动作类的一阶 progression 的可判定性和实际应用性，为动作推理的实际应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Progression, the task of updating a knowledge base to reflect action effects, generally requires second-order logic. Identifying first-order special cases, by restricting either the knowledge base or action effects, has long been a central topic in reasoning about actions. It is known that local-effect, normal, and acyclic actions, three increasingly expressive classes, admit first-order progression. However, a systematic analysis of the size of such progressions, crucial for practical applications, has been missing. In this paper, using the framework of Situation Calculus, we show that under reasonable assumptions, first-order progression for these action classes grows only polynomially. Moreover, we show that when the KB belongs to decidable fragments such as two-variable first-order logic or universal theories with constants, the progression remains within the same fragment, ensuring decidability and practical applicability.

</details>


### [145] [DisaBench: A Participatory Evaluation Framework for Disability Harms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12702)
*Eugenia Kim,Ioana Tanase,Christina Mallon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DisaBench introduces a co-created benchmark with 12 disability harm categories and 175 prompts to evaluate disability-related harms in LLMs. Key findings show current safety benchmarks miss most disability harms, which are context-dependent and require lived experience to fully recognize.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: General-purpose safety benchmarks for LLMs inadequately evaluate disability-related harms. Existing benchmarks fail to capture the nuanced, context-dependent nature of disability harm, missing subtle harms that require domain expertise to recognize.

Method: The study employed a co-creation methodology with people with disabilities and red teaming experts to develop a taxonomy of 12 disability harm categories. Evaluation methodology pairs benign and adversarial prompts across 7 life domains. A dataset of 175 prompts with 525 annotated prompt-response pairs was created, with annotations provided by 4 evaluators with lived disability experience.

Result: Three key findings: (1) Harm rates vary significantly by disability type and will compound in non-text modalities; (2) Terminology-driven harm is culturally and temporally bound rather than universally assessable; (3) Standard safety evaluation catches overt failures while missing subtle harms recognizable only by domain experts. Disability harm is personal, intersectional, and community-defined.

Conclusion: DisaBench provides a comprehensive framework for evaluating disability-related harms in LLMs. The research demonstrates that existing safety benchmarks systematically fail to capture the nuanced, context-dependent nature of disability harm, which is simultaneously personal, intersectional, and community-defined. The released resources enable direct integration into existing safety pipelines.

Abstract: General-purpose safety benchmarks for large language models do not adequately evaluate disability-related harms. We introduce DisaBench: a taxonomy of twelve disability harm categories co-created with people with disabilities and red teaming experts, a taxonomy-driven evaluation methodology that pairs benign and adversarial prompts across seven life domains, and a dataset of 175 prompts with human-annotated labels on 525 prompt-response pairs. Annotation by four evaluators with lived disability experience reveals three findings: harm rates vary sharply by disability type and will compound in non-text modalities, terminology-driven harm is culturally and temporally bound rather than universally assessable, and standard safety evaluation catches overt failures while missing the subtle harms that only domain expertise can recognize. Disability harm is simultaneously personal, intersectional, and community-defined: it cannot be isolated from the full context of who a person is, and general-purpose benchmarks systematically miss it. We will release the dataset, taxonomy, and methodology via Hugging Face and an open-source red teaming framework for direct integration into existing safety pipelines with no additional infrastructure.

</details>


### [146] [CHAL: Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12718)
*Tommaso Giovannelli,Griffin D. Kent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHAL是一个多智能体辩证框架，通过图结构信念模式与梯度引导的信念修正机制，在可废止论证领域实现结构化信念优化，并证明元认知价值系统可作为可配置超参数调控辩论行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前多智能体辩论方法存在结构性问题：辩论在信念轨迹上呈鞅特性、收益主要来自多数投票、LLM表现出信心升级而非校准；认为辩论的真正价值不在真值任务而在可废止领域。

Method: 提出CHAL框架，采用图结构CHAL信念模式(CBS)，通过梯度信息动态机制实现贝叶斯启发的信念修正；引入跨认识论、逻辑、伦理的元认知价值系统作为可配置超参数；开展系列消融实验验证价值系统对信念空间的决定性影响。

Result: 消融实验证明：裁判价值系统决定辩论整体轨迹方向；议会多样性为所有参与者精炼信念；框架可跨领域泛化。

Conclusion: CHAL是首个将多智能体辩论视为可废止领域结构化信念优化的框架，产出可审计的信念产物，为可废止论证评估奠定基础，对构建推理透明、价值一致且受人类监督的AI系统具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate has emerged as a promising approach for improving LLM reasoning on ground-truth tasks, yet current methodologies face certain structural limitations: debate tends to induce a martingale over belief trajectories, majority voting accounts for most observed gains, and LLMs exhibit confidence escalation rather than calibration across rounds. We argue that the genuine value of debate, and dialectic systems as a whole, lies not in ground-truth tasks but in defeasible domains, where every position can in principle be defeated by better reasoning. We present the Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language (CHAL), a multi-agent dialectic framework that treats defeasible argumentation as an engine for belief optimization. Each agent maintains a CHAL Belief Schema (CBS), a graph-structured belief representation with a Bayesian-inspired architecture, that facilitates belief revision through a gradient-informed dynamic mechanism by leveraging the strength of the belief's thesis as a differentiable objective. Meta-cognitive value systems spanning epistemology, logic, and ethics are elevated to configurable hyperparameters governing agent reasoning and adjudication outcomes. We provide a series of ablation experiments that demonstrate systematic and interpretable effects: the adjudicator's value system determines the debate's overall trajectories in latent belief space, council diversity refines beliefs for all participants, and the framework generalizes across broad fields. CHAL is, to our knowledge, the first framework to treat multi-agent debate as structured belief optimization over defeasible domains. Further, the auditable belief artifacts it produces establish the foundation for dedicated evaluation suites for defeasible argumentation, with broader implications for building AI systems whose reasoning and value commitments are transparent, aligned, and subject to human oversight.

</details>


### [147] [BEHAVE: A Hybrid AI Framework for Real-Time Modeling of Collective Human Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12730)
*Helene Malyutina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper proposes BEHAVE, a mathematical framework treating groups of interacting humans as complex dynamical systems exhibiting emergence, nonlinearity, feedback loops, and phase transitions. It models collective behavior as continuous behavioral fields derived from kinematic micro-signals (position, velocity, orientation, gestures), structured via directed interaction graphs. The framework includes theoretical characterizations (tension field, field basis, criticality index) and neural-network-based perception/forecasting layers. Demonstrated on a 7-agent negotiation with potential applications in crowd safety, crisis management, education, and clinical settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing AI systems for modeling human behavior operate at the individual level or detect events retrospectively. Consequently, they fail to capture collective dynamics that determine whether a group remains stable or transitions into escalation/breakdown. There is a need for a paradigm shift from individual-centric to group-level dynamical systems modeling.

Method: The authors introduce BEHAVE (Behavioral Engine for Human Activity Vector Estimation), which models collective dynamics as continuous behavioral fields over an interaction space derived from observable physical signals. Kinematic micro-signals (position, velocity, body orientation, gestural activity) are structured into a directed interaction graph and aggregated into a basis of behavioral fields. The framework rests on one theorem and two structural propositions characterizing the tension field, the field basis, and the criticality index. Neural models implement perception and forecasting layers for data-driven learning and system dynamics approximation.

Result: The paper demonstrates a working pipeline on a 7-agent negotiation snapshot. The framework's generality is shown through recalibration to multiple domains including crowd safety, crisis-team dynamics, education, and clinical contexts. The theoretical foundations (theorem and propositions) provide formal characterization of tension fields, field basis, and criticality index.

Conclusion: BEHAVE offers a unified computational framework for learning, representing, and forecasting collective dynamics across diverse domains including negotiation, crowd safety, crisis management, education, and clinical contexts. The work demonstrates a working pipeline on a 7-agent negotiation scenario, establishing the applicability of the behavioral field approach to real-world collective behavior analysis.

Abstract: Existing AI systems for modeling human behavior operate at the level of individuals or detect events after they occur. As a result, they systematically fail to capture the collective dynamics that determine whether a group remains stable or transitions into escalation or breakdown. We propose a different foundation: a group of interacting humans constitutes a complex dynamical system in the precise mathematical sense, exhibiting emergence, nonlinearity, feedback loops, sensitivity near critical points, and phase transitions between qualitatively distinct regimes. The state of such a system is not located within any single participant; it is distributed across mutual influence loops and observable through the micro-dynamics of the body.
  We introduce BEHAVE (Behavioral Engine for Human Activity Vector Estimation), a formal framework that models collective dynamics as continuous behavioral fields defined over an interaction space derived from observable physical signals. Kinematic micro-signals (position, velocity, body orientation, gestural activity) are structured into a directed interaction graph and aggregated into a basis of behavioral fields capturing distinct, non-redundant axes of collective state. The framework rests on one theorem and two structural propositions characterizing the tension field, the field basis, and the criticality index. Perception and forecasting layers are implemented using neural models, enabling data-driven learning and approximation of system dynamics. BEHAVE is formulated as a computational system for learning, representing, and forecasting collective dynamics from data. A working pipeline is demonstrated on a 7-agent negotiation snapshot. The same fields, recalibrated, apply to crowd safety, crisis-team dynamics, education, and clinical contexts.

</details>


### [148] [State-Centric Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12755)
*Sungheon Jeong,Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SDP通过让智能体在运行时逐谓词构建状态表示来解决语言环境缺乏MDP结构的问题，在5个基准测试上取得最佳免训练性能，并支持多种高级分析功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言环境（如Web浏览器、代码终端、交互模拟）输出原始文本而非状态，缺乏MDP分析所需的运行时结构，包括：显式状态空间、观察-状态映射、认证转换和终止标准。

Method: SDP是一种运行时框架，通过让智能体在acting过程中逐谓词地构建缺失的MDP输入。核心机制为：(1)智能体承诺一个描述世界状态的自然语言谓词；(2)采取行动使谓词为真；(3)将观察结果与谓词对照验证。通过的谓词成为认证状态，形成包含任务诱导状态空间、观察-状态映射、认证转换和终止标准的完整轨迹。

Result: 在涵盖规划、科学探索、Web推理和多跳问答的5个基准测试上，SDP在所有任务上实现了最佳的免训练性能，且优势随任务 horizon 增长而扩大。

Conclusion: SDP框架生成的认证轨迹支持多种高级分析功能，包括按谓词进行信用分配、故障定位、部分进度测量和模块化算子替换，这些功能对于反应式智能体是不可用的。

Abstract: Language environments such as web browsers, code terminals, and interactive simulations emit raw text rather than states, and provide none of the runtime structure that MDP analysis requires. No explicit state space, no observation-to-state mapping, no certified transitions, and no termination criterion. We introduce the State-Centric Decision Process (SDP), a runtime framework that constructs these missing inputs by having the agent build them, predicate by predicate, as it acts. At each step the agent commits to a natural-language predicate describing how the world should look, takes an action to make it true, and checks the observation against it. Predicates that pass become certified states, and the resulting trajectory carries the four objects language environments do not provide, namely a task-induced state space, an observation-to-state mapping, certified transitions, and a termination criterion. We evaluate SDP on five benchmarks spanning planning, scientific exploration, web reasoning, and multi-hop question answering. SDP achieves the best training-free results on all five, with the advantage widening as the horizon grows. The certified trajectories additionally support analyses unavailable to reactive agents, including per-predicate credit assignment, failure localization, partial-progress measurement, and modular operator replacement.

</details>


### [149] [Retrieval-Augmented Tutoring for Algorithm Tracing and Problem-Solving in AI Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12988)
*Mragisha Jain,Tirth Bhatt,Griffin Pitts,Aum Pandya,Peter Brusilovsky,Narges Norouzi,Arto Hellas,Juho Leinonen,Bita Akram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KITE 是一个基于检索增强生成(RAG)的智能辅导系统，用于算法推理和问题解决教学，采用意图感知的 Socratic 策略提供个性化提示和 scaffolded 支持，通过多模态 RAG 从课程材料中检索相关信息确保响应与教学内容一致。评估结果显示其在响应质量和教学效果上表现良好，能够帮助学生在算法问题解决上取得进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习算法的学生在解释追踪、调试推理错误以及跨陌生问题应用程序时需要支持，但现有教学工具难以提供针对性和个性化的辅导。

Method: KITE 使用多模态 RAG pipeline 从课程材料中检索相关信息以保持响应与课程内容的一致性；采用意图感知的 Socratic 响应策略，通过目标提示、引导性问题逐步引导学生解决问题，旨在增强学生的算法问题解决能力。

Result: 三个层面的评估表明：(1) RAGAs 指标显示响应具有良好的上下文一致性和质量；(2) 专家评估确认了教学法的适当性；(3) 模拟学生 pipeline 显示较弱模型在获得 KITE 反馈后能产生更准确的程序性和追踪问题的跟进响应。

Conclusion: 该研究贡献了一个 tutoring 架构和评估方法，用于评估基于检索的解释和 scaffolded 问题解决反馈。KITE 能够产生上下文接地且符合教学法的响应，其反馈能够帮助学生模型在程序性和追踪问题上有更准确的跟进响应，表明其 scaffolding 可以支持算法问题解决。

Abstract: Students learning algorithms often need support as they interpret traces, debug reasoning errors, and apply procedures across unfamiliar problem instances. In this paper, we present KITE (Knowledge-Informed Tutoring Engine), a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-based intelligent tutoring system designed to serve as a classroom teaching assistant for algorithmic reasoning and problem-solving tasks. KITE uses an intent-aware Socratic response strategy to tailor support to different student needs, responding with targeted hints, guiding questions, and progressive scaffolding intended to strengthen students' algorithmic problem-solving ability. To keep responses aligned with course content, KITE uses a multimodal RAG pipeline that retrieves relevant information from course materials. We evaluate KITE using three forms of assessment: RAGAs-based metrics for response grounding and quality, expert evaluation of pedagogical quality, and a simulated student pipeline in which a weaker language model interacts with KITE across two-turn dialogues and produces revised answers after receiving feedback. Results indicate that KITE produces contextually grounded and pedagogically appropriate responses. Further, using simulated students, KITE's feedback helped the student models produce more accurate follow-up responses on procedural and tracing questions, suggesting that its scaffolding can support algorithmic problem-solving. This work contributes a tutoring architecture and an evaluation approach for assessing retrieval-grounded explanations and scaffolded problem-solving feedback.

</details>


### [150] [Multimodal Hidden Markov Models for Persistent Emotional State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12838)
*Anamika Ragu,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于粘性因子分解HDP-HMM的轻量级框架，通过多模态（视频、音频、文本） valence-arousal 表示建模对话中的潜在情绪状态，并用于临床场景的情感动态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别方法仅在话语级别操作，掩盖了真实对话动态中的持续性阶段。临床对话场景需要可解释的情感弧线追踪来理解和引导沟通，因此需要一种能捕捉对话中情绪状态演变的模型。

Method: 采用粘性因子分解 HDP-HMM（Hierarchical Dirichlet Process-Hidden Markov Model）将对话情感建模为潜在情绪状态的序列。基于多模态输入（视频、音频、文本）提取 valence-arousal 表示。评估采用 LLM-as-Judge、几何一致性和时间一致性指标。

Result: 粘性 HDP-HMM 相比高斯 HMM 基线产生更可解释的情绪状态序列，计算成本远低于基于 LLM 的对话状态跟踪方法。在临床数据集上的问答实验表明，可从多模态 valence-arousal 轨迹中可靠地恢复有意义的情绪阶段，并用于通过上下文增强改善不稳定情绪状态下的 LLM 响应质量。

Conclusion: 该框架为可解释、轻量级、可操作的大规模对话情感动态分析开辟了新路径，特别是在临床应用场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Tracking an interpretable emotional arc of a conversation via the sentiment of individual utterances processed as a whole is central to both understanding and guiding communication in applied, especially clinical, conversational contexts. Existing approaches to emotion recognition operate at the utterance level, obscuring the persistent phases that characterize real conversational dynamics. We propose a lightweight framework that models conversational emotion as a sequence of latent emotional regimes using sticky factorial HDP-HMMs over multimodal valence-arousal representations derived from simultaneous video, audio and textual input. We evaluate the quality of regime prediction using LLM-as-a-Judge, geometric, and temporal consistency metrics, demonstrating that the sticky HDP-HMM produces more interpretable regime sequences than the baseline Gaussian HMM at a fraction of the computational cost of LLM-based dialogue state tracking methods. In addition, Question-Answer experiments in a clinical dataset suggest that meaningful emotional phases can reliably be recovered from multimodal valence-arousal trajectories and used to improve the quality of LLM responses in unstable affective regimes via context augmentation. This framework thus opens a path toward interpretable, lightweight, and actionable analysis of conversational emotion dynamics at scale.

</details>


### [151] [Moltbook Moderation: Uncovering Hidden Intent Through Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12856)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Nafis Tripto,Abolfazl Ansari,Jason Lucas,Suhang Wang,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Bot-Mod is an intent-based moderation framework for multi-agent systems that uses multi-turn agent engagement and Gibbs-based sampling to detect malicious behaviors, moving beyond traditional content-level filtering. Evaluated on a Moltbook-derived dataset, it demonstrates effective detection with low false positives.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-agent systems face moderation challenges where malicious agents contribute seemingly benign content while exhibiting harmful patterns across their overall interaction behaviors, evading traditional content-based filtering approaches.

Method: Bot-Mod engages target agents in multi-turn exchanges guided by Gibbs-based sampling over candidate intent hypotheses, progressively narrowing the space of plausible objectives to identify underlying malicious behaviors.

Result: Bot-Mod reliably identifies agent intent across various adversarial configurations while maintaining a low false positive rate on benign behaviors.

Conclusion: Bot-Mod provides a scalable foundation for intent-aware moderation in open multi-agent environments, effectively balancing detection accuracy with low false positive rates.

Abstract: The emergence of multi-agent systems introduces novel moderation challenges that extend beyond content filtering. Agents with {\em malicious intent} may contribute harmful content that appears benign to evade content-based moderation, while compromising the system through exploitative and malicious behavior manifested across their overall interaction patterns within the community. To address this, we introduce \textsc{\textbf{Bot-Mod}} (\textsc{\textbf{Bot-Mod}}eration), a moderation framework that grounds detection in agent intent rather than traditional content level signals. \method{} identifies the underlying intent by engaging with the target agent in a multi-turn exchange guided by Gibbs-based sampling over candidate intent hypotheses. This progressively narrows the space of plausible agent objectives to identify the underlying behavior. To evaluate our approach, we construct a dataset derived from Moltbook that encompasses diverse benign and malicious behaviors based on actual community structures, posts, and comments. Results demonstrate that \textsc{\textbf{Bot-Mod}} reliably identifies agent intent across a range of adversarial configurations, while maintaining a low false positive rate on benign behaviors. This work advances the foundation for scalable, intent-aware moderation of agents in open multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Cooperative Simulators: Generating Realistic User Personas for Robust Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12894)
*Harshita Chopra,Kshitish Ghate,Aylin Caliskan,Tadayoshi Kohno,Chirag Shah,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona Policies (PPol)，通过进化程序搜索自动生成多样化、类人用户模拟器，以解决现有LLM模拟器过于合作和同质化的问题。实验表明该方法在多个领域取得显著改进，模拟器被评估为人类的比例接近真实用户水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM-based user simulators inherit cooperative and homogeneous behavior from their underlying models, making agents trained on these simulators fail when encountering the diverse communication patterns of real users. Since collecting real interaction data at scale is expensive, there is a need for better user simulators that capture realistic behavioral variation.

Method: The authors propose Persona Policies (PPol), a plug-and-play control layer that induces realistic behavioral variation in user simulators. PPol casts persona generation as an LLM-driven evolutionary program search that optimizes a Python generator to discover behaviors and translate them into task-preserving roleplay policies. The search is guided by a multi-objective fitness score combining human-likeness with broad coverage of human behavioral patterns.

Result: Experimental results on tau^2-bench retail and airline domains show that evolved PPol programs yield 33-62% absolute gains in fitness score over baseline simulators. In blinded evaluations, annotators rated PPol-conditioned users as human 80.4% of the time, close to real human traces and nearly twice as frequently as baseline simulators. Agents trained with PPol demonstrate +17% relative improvement in task success when facing challenging, out-of-distribution behaviors.

Conclusion: PPol provides a novel method to strengthen simulator-based evaluation and training without changing tasks or rewards. The approach offers a scalable solution for creating diverse, human-like user simulators that better prepare LLM agents for real-world deployment scenarios.

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in settings where they interact with a wide variety of people, including users who are unclear, impatient, or reluctant to share information. However, collecting real interaction data at scale remains expensive. The field has turned to LLM-based user simulators as stand-ins, but these simulators inherit the behavior of their underlying models: cooperative and homogeneous. As a result, agents that appear strong in simulation often fail under the unseen, diverse communication patterns of real users. To narrow this gap, we introduce Persona Policies (PPol), a plug-and-play control layer that induces realistic behavioral variation in user simulators while preserving the original task goals. Rather than hand-crafting personas, we cast persona generation as an LLM-driven evolutionary program search that optimizes a Python generator to discover behaviors and translate them into task-preserving roleplay policies. Candidate generators are guided by a multi-objective fitness score combining human-likeness with broad coverage of human behavioral patterns. Once optimized, the generator produces a diverse population of human-like personas for any task in the domain. Across tau^2-bench retail and airline domains, evolved PPol programs yield 33-62% absolute gains in fitness score over the baseline simulator. In a blinded evaluation, annotators rated PPol-conditioned users as human 80.4% of the time, close to real human traces and nearly twice as frequently as baseline simulators. Agents trained with PPol are more robust to challenging, out-of-distribution behaviors, improving task success by +17% relative to training only on existing simulated interactions. This offers a novel approach to strengthen simulator-based evaluation and training without changing tasks or rewards.

</details>


### [153] [IdeaForge: A Knowledge Graph-Grounded Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Methodology Innovation Analysis and Patent Claim Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13311)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IdeaForge是一个基于FalkorDB知识图谱的多智能体框架，通过整合TRIZ、Design Thinking和SCAMPER三种创新方法论，利用CONVERGENT关系连接跨方法论支持的候选权利要求，实现高置信度创新方案识别和结构化专利草稿生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助创新系统存在根本性局限：单一方法论（TRIZ或Design Thinking等）的顺序式提示工作流无法保留中间推理结构，导致跨方法论洞察碎片化，缺乏可追溯性、综合性和系统性新颖性评估能力。

Method: IdeaForge采用多智能体架构，通过FalkorDB知识图谱整合TRIZ、Design Thinking和SCAMPER三种创新方法论。各专项智能体在持久化知识图谱上操作，贡献结构化实体和关系表示矛盾、创新原理、用户需求、转换、类比和候选权利要求。核心机制为基于图的跨方法论汇聚，通过CONVERGENT关系连接多方法论独立支持的候选方案，利用图遍历识别高置信度创新候选。下游专利起草智能体基于汇聚权利要求子图生成结构化专利草稿。创新评分公式(InnovationScore)综合汇聚支持度、方法论多样性、权利要求强度和现有技术挑战数量进行排序。

Result: 在法律技术用例上的实验表明，基于图谱的多方法论综合相比单一方法论基线能产生更多样化且可追溯的创新候选方案。

Conclusion: IdeaForge通过图谱原生架构实现了多方法论创新综合，实验证明能产生更多样化和可追溯的创新候选方案。该框架对计算创造力、可解释AI辅助发明及图原生创新系统具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Current AI-assisted innovation systems typically apply a single ideation methodology (such as TRIZ or Design Thinking) using sequential prompt-based workflows that do not preserve intermediate reasoning structure. As a result, insights generated across methodologies remain fragmented, limiting traceability, synthesis, and systematic evaluation of novelty. We present IdeaForge, a knowledge graph-grounded multi-agent framework for innovation analysis and patent claim generation. IdeaForge integrates multiple innovation methodologies (TRIZ, Design Thinking, and SCAMPER) through specialist agents operating over a persistent FalkorDB knowledge graph. Each agent contributes structured entities and relationships representing contradictions, inventive principles, user needs, transformations, analogies, and candidate claims. The central contribution of IdeaForge is a cross-methodology convergence mechanism implemented through graph-based claim linkage. Claims independently supported by multiple methodologies are connected using CONVERGENT relationships, enabling identification of high-confidence innovation candidates through graph traversal. A downstream patent drafting agent generates structured patent drafts grounded in convergent claim subgraphs, reducing reliance on unconstrained language model generation. An InnovationScore formula ranks claims by convergent support, methodology diversity, claim strength, and prior art challenge count. We describe the graph schema, agent architecture, convergence detection pipeline, and patent synthesis workflow. Experiments on a legal technology use case demonstrate that graph-grounded multi-methodology synthesis produces more diverse and traceable innovation candidates compared to single-methodology baselines. We discuss implications for computational creativity, explainable AI-assisted invention, and graph-native innovation systems.

</details>


### [154] [When Attention Closes: How LLMs Lose the Thread in Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12922)
*Vardhan Dongre,Joseph Hsieh,Viet Dac Lai,Seunghyun Yoon,Trung Bui,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"通道转换假说"解释大型语言模型在长对话中丢失目标的机制：目标定义token通过注意力机制变得不易访问，但目标相关信息可能持续存在于残差表示中。通过引入GAR指标和多种探测手段，研究发现不同架构在此转换过程中表现迥异，且残差可解码性与注意力损失之间的差距可预测模型是否在通道关闭后仍保持目标导向行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在单轮交互中能遵循复杂指令，但在长时多轮交互中常丢失指令、人物设定和规则的追踪线索；该现象已有行为测量，但缺乏机制层面的解释

Method: 1) 引入Goal Accessibility Ratio (GAR)指标，测量生成token对任务定义目标token的注意力；2) 结合滑动窗口消融实验；3) 使用残差流探针（residual-stream probes）；4) Mistral模型的强制闭合注意力通道的因果干预实验；5) 20-fact记忆任务的保留率测试；6) 人物设定约束违反率的对抗压力基线测试；7) 四种主要架构的线性探针分类器评估

Result: 1) 注意力对指令的关闭导致架构间出现定性不同的失败模式：部分模型在注意力接近消失时仍保持目标条件行为，另一些则在可解码残差目标信息存在的情况下仍然失败；2) 编码目标信息的关键层因架构而异，范围从第2层到第27层；3) 强制闭合Mistral注意力通道使20-fact记忆任务从近乎完美降至11%，并使人物约束违反率超过无用户压力的对抗基线，两种效果均出现在可预测的交叉轮次；4) 线性探针从残差表示中恢复每episode记忆结果，四个主要架构的AUC均高达0.99，而输入嵌入保持随机水平

Conclusion: 1) GAR可作为诊断工具预测模型失败时机；2) 通道转换框架提供了可控的机制解释；3) 注意力损失与残差可解码性之间的差距可预测目标条件行为是否存活；4) 残差表示在输入嵌入层面无法恢复目标信息（接近随机水平），但线性探针可从残差流中以AUC高达0.99恢复记忆结果；5) 不同架构在目标信息编码层上存在显著差异（2-27层）

Abstract: Large language models can follow complex instructions in a single turn, yet over long multi-turn interactions they often lose the thread of instructions, persona, and rules. This degradation has been measured behaviorally but not mechanistically explained. We propose a channel-transition account: goal-defining tokens become less accessible through attention, while goal-related information may persist in residual representations. We introduce the Goal Accessibility Ratio (GAR), measuring attention from generated tokens to task-defining goal tokens, and combine it with sliding-window ablations and residual-stream probes. When attention to instructions closes, what survives reveals architecture. Across architectures, the transition yields qualitatively distinct failure modes: some models preserve goal-conditioned behavior at vanishing attention, others fail despite decodable residual goal information, and the layer at which this encoding emerges varies from 2 to 27. A within-model causal ablation that force-closes the attention channel in Mistral collapses recall from near-perfect to 11% on a 20-fact retention task and raises persona-constraint violations above an adversarial-pressure baseline without user pressure, with both effects emerging at the predictable crossover turn. Linear probes recover per-episode recall outcomes from residual representations with AUC up to 0.99 across all four primary architectures, while input embeddings remain at chance. Across architectures and model scales, the gap between attention loss and residual decodability predicts whether goal-conditioned behavior survives channel closure. We contribute GAR as a diagnostic, the channel-transition framework as a controlled mechanistic account, and a parametric prediction of failure timing under windowed attention closure.

</details>


### [155] [AI-Generated Slides: Are They Good? Can Students Tell?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13532)
*Juho Leinonen,Lisa Zhang,Arto Hellas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了生成式AI工具在辅助教师从课程笔记生成教学幻灯片中的应用，比较了5种主流AI工具，并评估了教育者和学生对AI生成幻灯片的质量感知。研究发现编程辅助工具表现最佳，且学生难以区分AI生成与人工制作的幻灯片，但存在"AI质量偏见"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI工具的普及，探索其辅助教学设计的潜力具有重要意义。研究旨在回答两个核心问题：(1) AI能否生成高质量的教学幻灯片？(2) 学生是否能感知并区分AI生成的幻灯片？

Method: 研究采用混合方法设计：首先使用5种AI工具（NotebookLM、Claude、M365 Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code）从课程笔记生成幻灯片；然后由教育者进行叙事评估筛选优质幻灯片；最后在真实课程环境中对比学生对这些幻灯片的感知质量，并测试其识别AI来源的能力。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 编程辅助工具（Cursor、Claude Code）生成的幻灯片在准确性、完整性和教学设计方面表现最佳；(2) 学生认为AI生成幻灯片与教师自制幻灯片质量相当；(3) 学生无法可靠识别AI生成的幻灯片；(4) 存在负相关——学生将高质量与"非AI来源"关联，反映出对AI生成内容的质量偏见。

Conclusion: GenAI工具在教学设计工作流中具有显著应用前景，但需负责任地使用。研究建议教育者关注工具选择（优先考虑编程辅助类工具），并呼吁进一步研究如何有效、负责任地整合AI辅助教学设计。

Abstract: As generative AI (GenAI) tools become easily accessible, there is promise in using such tools to support instructors. To that end, this paper examines using GenAI to help generate slides from instructor authored course notes, emphasizing instructor and student perceptions. We examine an end-to-end education tool (NotebookLM), two general-purpose LLMs (Claude, M365 Copilot), and two coding assistants (Cursor, Claude Code). We first analyze whether GenAI generated slides are ``good'' via narrative assessment by educators. We choose the best slides to use (with some modification) in a real course setting, and compare the student perception of human vs. AI generated slides. We find that coding assistant tools produce slides that were most accurate, complete, and pedagogically sound. Additionally, students rate GenAI slides to be of similar quality as instructor-created slides, and cannot reliably identify which slides are AI-generated. Additionally, we find a negative correlation between a high quality rating and a high ``AI-generated'' rating, suggesting students associate poor quality with the source of the slides being AI. These findings highlight promising opportunities for integrating GenAI into instructional design workflows and call for further research on how educators can best harness such tools responsibly and effectively.

</details>


### [156] [Sustaining AI safety: Control-theoretic external impossibility, intrinsic necessity, and structural requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12963)
*James M. Mazzu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过控制论方法证明：当AI系统能力超过有界外部控制的应对范围时，任何依赖持续外部强制的安全策略都将失效。只有内在的（非外部强制的）安全策略才可能存活，且必须满足四项结构要求：去除外源性依赖、目标安全相容性、自我修改稳定性和能力增长下的持续安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统能力日益增强，现有的安全策略评估存在根本性缺陷：仅关注当前风险降低是不够的，必须考虑当外部控制不再可靠时，安全策略能否继续维持。这一问题在AI能力不断增长的背景下变得尤为紧迫。

Method: 运用控制论框架进行分析，在明确的前提条件（包括可达性条件）下进行数学化证明。具体采用类级别的证明方法，区分了"不可能结果"和"必要结果"两种论证策略。

Result: 第一，建立类级别的外部不可行性定理：一旦系统的影响力超出有界外部控制所能抵消的范围，任何依赖持续外部强制的策略都无法维持AI安全。这一失败是结构性的，跨越整个外部强制类别而非偶发于特定策略。第二，建立条件性类级别必要性定理：如果消除后仍存在至少一个候选安全维持策略，则所有剩余策略必然是内在的（非外部强制的）。

Conclusion: 提出四项可行性必要条件：安全性不能依赖持续的外部强制；系统的终极目标在形成时必须与安全相容；该目标在自我修改时必须保持稳定；以及安全性必须随能力增长而持续保持。但本文并未提出完整的策略方案，其贡献在于为关于外部控制局限性的广泛担忧提供形式化结构。

Abstract: As AI systems become increasingly capable, safety strategies must be evaluated not only by how much they reduce present risk, but by whether they could sustain safety once external control can no longer reliably constrain system behavior. This paper addresses that problem by using control theory to clarify, at a structural level, whether externally enforced safety-sustaining strategies can succeed and, if not, what any alternative strategy would have to satisfy in order to be viable. It establishes two main results. First, under explicit premises including a reachability condition, it proves a class-wide external impossibility result: once the system's effects exceed what bounded external control can counteract, no strategy that depends in any degree on continued external enforcement can sustain AI safety. This failure is structural across the entire externally enforced class rather than contingent on any particular strategy. Second, it establishes a conditional class-level necessity result: if at least one candidate safety-sustaining strategy remains after that elimination, then all such remaining strategies must be intrinsic. It then states four structural requirements for viability: safety may not depend on continued external enforcement; the system's terminal objective must be safety-compatible when first formed; that objective must remain stable under self-modification; and safety must continue to be preserved as capability grows. The paper does not propose a complete strategy for sustaining AI safety. Its contribution is to give formal structure to a widely held concern about the limits of external control. It does so by deriving explicit conditional results that identify which safety-sustaining strategies are ruled out and what any remaining strategies must satisfy.

</details>


### [157] [Position: Agentic AI System Is a Foreseeable Pathway to AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12966)
*Junwei Liao,Shuai Li,Muning Wen,Jun Wang,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"缩放定律万能"的传统观点，论证Agentic AI是实现AGI的必要范式。通过理论推导对比单体学习器与Agentic系统的优化约束，证明了Agentic AI在泛化能力和样本效率上具有指数级优势，同时重新诠释了多智能体框架的不稳定性问题，并呼吁学界加大对Agentic AI的研究投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI领域存在"单体缩放至上"的教条观点，即认为单纯通过扩大单一模型的规模即可实现AGI。本文认为这一假设存在根本性缺陷，因为现实世界任务具有复杂异构的分布特性，单一模型难以有效覆盖。

Method: 1) 从理论基础出发，推导单体学习器与Agentic系统的优化约束差异；2) 从简单路由机制逐步扩展到通用DAG拓扑结构进行建模；3) 建立理论框架对比两类系统在泛化与样本效率上的表现。

Result: Agentic AI在泛化能力和样本效率方面均优于单体学习器，且优势呈指数级。该优势源于Agentic系统能够将复杂异构任务分布分解为可专业化处理的子任务。

Conclusion: 本文将Agentic AI与MoE（混合专家）进行关联对比，同时对当前多智能体框架的不稳定性现象提供了新的理论解释。最终呼吁学界重新审视研究重心，从盲目追求单体缩放转向Agentic AI的系统架构设计。

Abstract: Is monolithic scaling the only path to AGI? This paper challenges the dogma that purely scaling a single model is sufficient to achieve Artificial General Intelligence. Instead, we identify Agentic AI as a necessary paradigm for mastering the complex, heterogeneous distribution of real-world tasks. Through rigorous theoretical derivations, we contrast the optimization constraints of monolithic learners against the efficiency of Agentic systems, progressing from simple routing mechanisms to general Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) topologies. We demonstrate that Agentic AI achieves exponentially superior generalization and sample efficiency. Finally, we discuss the connection to Mixture-of-Experts, reinterpret the instability of current multi-agent frameworks, and call for greater research focus on Agentic AI.

</details>


### [158] [Retrieval is Cheap, Show Me the Code: Executable Multi-Hop Reasoning for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12975)
*Jiashuo Sun,Jimeng Shi,Yixuan Xie,Saizhuo Wang,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Pengcheng Jiang,Zhiyi Shi,Jiajun Fan,Qinglong Zheng,Peiran Li,Shaowen Wang,Ge Liu,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PyRAG通过程序合成范式革新多跳检索增强生成，将结构化推理过程表示为可执行的Python程序，暴露中间状态并产生确定性反馈，实现编译器引导的自我修复和自适应检索，在多个基准数据集上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在处理多跳问题时存在三大缺陷：(1)自然语言推理轨迹中中间状态隐式表达；(2)检索查询容易偏离目标实体；(3)错误检测与生成由同一模型完成，导致自我反思不可靠、无根可依。

Method: 将多跳RAG重新定义为程序合成与执行问题。PyRAG使用Python程序表示推理过程，通过变量暴露中间状态，通过程序执行产生确定性反馈，生成可检查的执行轨迹。该框架还支持编译器引导的自我修复和执行驱动的自适应检索，无需额外训练。

Result: 在5个QA基准数据集（PopQA、HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA、MuSiQue、Bamboogle）上，PyRAG在无需训练和RL训练两种设置下均一致优于强基线方法，尤其在组合式多跳数据集上提升最大。

Conclusion: PyRAG通过将多跳RAG重新定义为程序合成与执行，为知识密集型问答提供了一种更鲁棒、可解释的解决框架，在多个基准数据集上均优于强基线方法，尤其在组合式多跳数据集上提升显著。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a standard approach for knowledge-intensive question answering, but existing systems remain brittle on multi-hop questions, where solving the task requires chaining multiple retrieval and reasoning steps. Key challenges are that current methods represent reasoning through free-form natural language, where intermediate states are implicit, retrieval queries can drift from intended entities, and errors are detected by the same model that produces them making self-reflection an unreliable, ungrounded signal.
  We observe that multi-hop question answering is a typical form of step-by-step computation, and that this structured process aligns closely with how code-specialized language models are trained to operate. Motivated by this, we introduce \pyrag, a framework that reformulates multi-hop RAG as program synthesis and execution. Instead of free-form reasoning trajectories, \pyrag represents the reasoning process as an executable Python program over retrieval and QA tools, exposing intermediate states as variables, producing deterministic feedback through execution, and yielding an inspectable trace of the entire reasoning process. This formulation further enables compiler-grounded self-repair and execution-driven adaptive retrieval without any additional training.
  Experiments on five QA benchmarks (PopQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, MuSiQue, and Bamboogle) show that \pyrag consistently outperforms strong baselines under both training-free and RL-trained settings, with especially large gains on compositional multi-hop datasets. Our code, data and models are publicly available at https://github.com/GasolSun36/PyRAG.

</details>


### [159] [Useful Memories Become Faulty When Continuously Updated by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12978)
*Dylan Zhang,Yanshan Lin,Zhengkun Wu,Yihang Sun,Bingxuan Li,Dianqi Li,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前LLM智能体记忆系统的一个核心缺陷：整合式记忆（将轨迹改写为抽象知识）反而导致性能下降。研究通过ARC-AGI任务证明，原始经验轨迹比LLM生成的整合记忆更可靠，智能体应优先保留原始情节而非自动触发整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体记忆系统追求LLM将过去轨迹改写为文本记忆库并持续更新，承诺无需参数更新即可自我改进。但作者发现这种整合式记忆即使来自有用经验也往往存在缺陷——记忆效用先升后降，甚至低于无记忆基线

Method: 1)对比有记忆与无记忆基线的ARC-AGI任务准确率；2)对同一轨迹在不同更新策略下生成记忆质量；3)受控ARC-AGI Stream环境测试Retain/Delete/Consolidate三种操作；4)对比整合式记忆与纯情景式控制的性能

Result: 1)GPT-5.4对曾独立解决的问题，整合后失败率达54%；2)同一轨迹在不同更新策略下产生质量截然不同的记忆；3)默认保留原始情节 Episodic-only 控制与主动整合系统性能相当；4)在受控环境中，自动保留原始情节的智能体准确率是强制整合的两倍

Conclusion: 当前LLM的整合步骤会引入错误而非提升性能；可靠智能体记忆需将原始经验视为一等公民，并显式控制整合过程而非每次交互后自动触发；最终需要能整合经验但不覆盖原始证据的LLM

Abstract: Learning from past experience benefits from two complementary forms of memory: episodic traces -- raw trajectories of what happened -- and consolidated abstractions distilled across many episodes into reusable, schema-like lessons. Recent agentic-memory systems pursue the consolidated form: an LLM rewrites past trajectories into a textual memory bank that it continuously updates with new interactions, promising self-improving agents without parameter updates. Yet we find that such consolidated memories produced by today's LLMs are often faulty even when derived from useful experiences. As consolidation proceeds, memory utility first rises, then degrades, and can fall below the no-memory baseline. More surprisingly, even when consolidating from ground-truth solutions, GPT-5.4 fails on 54% of a set of ARC-AGI problems it had previously solved without memory. We trace the regression to the consolidation step rather than the underlying experience: the same trajectories yield qualitatively different memories under different update schedules, and an episodic-only control that simply retains those trajectories remains competitive with the consolidators we test. In a controlled ARC-AGI Stream environment that exposes Retain, Delete, and Consolidate actions, agents preserve raw episodes by default and double the accuracy of their forced-consolidation counterparts; disabling consolidation entirely (episodic management only) matches this auto regime. Practically, robust agent memory should treat raw episodes as first-class evidence and gate consolidation explicitly rather than firing it after every interaction. Looking forward, reliable agentic memory will require LLMs that can consolidate without overwriting the evidence they depend on.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Agent Systems in Emergency Departments: Validation Study on a ED Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13345)
*Markus Wenzel,Tobias Strapatsas,Jessika Kress,Dorothea Sauer,Nele Gessler,Horst K. Hahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合离散事件模拟(DES)和基于智能体建模(ABM)的仿真框架，用于模拟急诊科环境并探索资源优化策略。通过与文献数据对比验证了模型的有效性，并将多智能体系统(MAS)集成到框架中以实现资源分配策略的自主探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科(ED)在患者护理和资源管理方面面临严峻挑战，需要一个灵活、可配置的模型来探索和验证资源优化策略。

Method: 1) 建立混合DES-ABM模型模拟可配置的急诊科环境；2) 从真实世界研究数据中提取ED规模、患者负荷和人员配置参数；3) 通过与文献中的关键绩效指标对比验证模型表达力；4) 实施科学验证和实践验证的资源优化策略；5) 集成多智能体系统(MAS)基于急诊科事件记录的时间账本自主探索资源分配策略。

Result: 验证结果表明，基于DES-ABM的仿真模型能够有效复现干预措施下的真实急诊科动态特征。

Conclusion: 模块化的DES-ABM-MAS框架为探索急诊科资源优化策略提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: Emergency departments (ED) face challenges in patient care and resource management. We propose to explore optimization strategies in a realistic and flexible model and develop a hybrid Discrete Event Simulation (DES) and Agent-Based Model (ABM) simulating highly configurable ED environments. We specifically focus on the validation of the modeling approach. We derive configurations for ED sizes, patient load, and staffing from real-world studies. We then validate the model expressivity by matching its key performance indicators and metrics with their values known from literature. We proceed by implementing scientifically established and practice-proven resource optimization strategies. Comparing the documented real-world outcomes with our model's results demonstrates that the DES-ABM based simulation can effectively replicate real-world ER dynamics under interventions. We lastly integrate a Proof-of-Concept multi-agent system (MAS) that can autonomously explore resource allocation strategies within the simulated ER environment based on a temporal ledger of ED event records. This modular DES-ABM-MAS framework offers a powerful tool to explore resource optimization strategies in emergency departments.

</details>


### [161] [Improving Code Translation with Syntax-Guided and Semantic-aware Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13229)
*Yuhan Wu,Huan Zhang,Wei Cheng,Chen Shen,Jingyue Yang,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CTO方法，通过对比学习训练的跨语言语义模型直接评估功能等价性，并结合编译器语法反馈，在直接偏好优化框架下解决大语言模型代码翻译中的语法正确性和语义一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的学习方法受限于不可靠的语义奖励——这些奖励依赖于稀疏的测试用例或受限的参考翻译，导致代码翻译难以同时保证语法正确性和语义一致性。

Method: (1) 对比学习：训练跨语言语义模型直接评估源代码与翻译代码的功能等价性；(2) 多目标优化：将鲁棒语义信号与编译器语法反馈统一于直接偏好优化(DPO)框架中。

Result: 在C++、Java、Python三种语言的翻译任务上，CTO显著优于现有基线和其他偏好优化策略。

Conclusion: CTO通过将语义信号与语法反馈统一在DPO框架中，显著提升了代码翻译质量，在C++、Java、Python三种语言翻译任务上均超越现有基线方法。

Abstract: LLMs have shown immense potential for code translation, yet they often struggle to ensure both syntactic correctness and semantic consistency. While preference-based learning offers a promising alignment strategy, it is hindered by unreliable semantic rewards derived from sparse test cases or restrictive reference translations. We argue that a robust semantic reward for code translation must be derived directly from the source code. In this paper, we propose CTO to improve code translation with syntax-guided and semantic-aware preference optimization. Through contrastive learning, we train a cross-lingual semantic model to directly assess functional equivalence between source and translated code. By formulating code translation as a multi-objective optimization problem, this robust semantic signal is seamlessly unified with compiler-based syntactic feedback within the direct preference optimization framework. Extensive experiments on C++, Java, and Python translations demonstrate that CTO significantly outperforms existing baselines and alternative preference optimization strategies.

</details>


### [162] [RealICU: Do LLM Agents Understand Long-Context ICU Data? A Benchmark Beyond Behavior Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13542)
*Chengzhi Shen,Weixiang Shen,Tobias Susetzky,Chen,Chen,Jun Li,Yuyuan Liu,Xuepeng Zhang,Zhenyu Gong,Daniel Rueckert,Jiazhen Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RealICU是一个用于评估LLM在真实ICU条件下决策支持能力的基准测试，通过资深医生 hindsight-annotated标注解决了传统基准依赖次优历史决策的问题，包含Gold和Scale两个数据集，揭示了当前模型在安全性和认知偏差上的关键局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU基准测试依赖历史临床医生的决策作为ground truth，但这些决策往往在信息不完整的情况下做出，可能并非最优，无法真实评估AI系统的推理能力。ICU环境具有长时间跨度、高信息密度、快速演变等特点，对AI决策支持系统提出了更高要求。

Method: 提出RealICU基准测试，采用 hindsight-annotated方式：由资深医生在回顾完整患者轨迹后创建标签，比实时决策更准确。设计四个医生驱动的评估任务：患者状态评估、急性问题识别、推荐行动和红色警戒行动。每个轨迹按30分钟窗口划分，使用Oracle（医生验证的LLM标注器）扩展数据集规模。

Result: 现有LLM（包括增强记忆的模型）在RealICU上表现不佳，揭示两个主要失败模式：临床建议的recall-safety tradeoff（召回率与安全性之间的权衡）和对患者早期信息的anchoring bias（锚定偏差）。ICU-Evo虽能改善长期推理但仍无法完全消除安全失败。

Conclusion: RealICU提供了一个临床可解释的评估框架，揭示了当前LLM在ICU决策支持中的关键局限：recall-safety tradeoff和anchoring bias；ICU-Evo的结构化记忆机制可改善长期推理但无法完全消除安全风险。

Abstract: Intensive care units (ICU) generate long, dense and evolving streams of clinical information, where physicians must repeatedly reassess patient states under time pressure, underscoring a clear need for reliable AI decision support. Existing ICU benchmarks typically treat historical clinician actions as ground truth. However, these actions are made under incomplete information and limited temporal context of the underlying patient state, and may therefore be suboptimal, making it difficult to assess the true reasoning capabilities of AI systems. We introduce RealICU, a hindsight-annotated benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) under realistic ICU conditions, where labels are created after senior physicians review the full patient trajectory. We formulate four physician-motivated tasks: assess Patient Status, Acute Problems, Recommended Actions, and Red Flag actions that risk unsafe outcomes. We partition each trajectory with 30-min windows and release two datasets: RealICU-Gold with 930-window annotations from 94 MIMIC-IV patients, and RealICU-Scale with 11,862 windows extended by Oracle, a physician-validated LLM hindsight labeler. Existing LLMs including memory-augmented ones performed poorly on RealICU, exposing two failure modes: a recall-safety tradeoff for clinical recommendations, and an anchoring bias to early interpretations of the patient. We further introduce ICU-Evo to study structured-memory agents that improves long-horizon reasoning but does not fully eliminate safety failures. Together, RealICU provides a clinically grounded testbed for measuring and improving AI sequential decision-support in high-stakes care. Project page: https://chengzhi-leo.github.io/RealICU-Bench/

</details>


### [163] [A Constraint Programming Approach for $n$-Day Lookahead Playoff Clinching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13142)
*Gili Rosenberg,Kyle E. C. Booth,J. Kyle Brubaker,Ruben S. Andrist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了国家冰球联盟(NHL)季后赛席位的提前确定问题，提出了一种结合树搜索和约束规划的算法，能够高效计算球队在接下来n天内如何确保季后赛资格，并验证了2021-22至2024-25赛季数百个场景的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在职业体育中，球迷和利益相关者关心的是球队何时以及在什么条件下能够锁定季后赛席位。NHL的季后赛资格判定涉及复杂的平局决胜规则，使得计算特定球队的锁定条件在计算上具有挑战性。

Method: 采用自定义树搜索方法，结合预处理技术、剪枝策略和节点排序启发式算法来高效探索可能的比赛结果空间。核心是一个基于约束规划(CP)的子程序，用于判断某球队在特定时间点是否已锁定季后赛资格（即"0日预判"），其原理是寻找该球队被淘汰的反例。

Result: 使用2021-22至2024-25赛季的公开NHL数据进行验证，通过数百个场景证明了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展至其他度量指标，包括季后赛数学淘汰的证明、总统奖杯(常规赛冠军)的锁定，以及任何其他排名的锁定或淘汰判定。

Abstract: In professional sports, a team has clinched the playoffs if they are guaranteed a postseason spot, regardless of the outcomes of any remaining games. As the season progresses, sports fans and other stakeholders are interested in precisely when, and under what conditions, their team will clinch the playoffs. In this paper, we investigate playoff clinching in the context of the National Hockey League (NHL), where it is computationally challenging to produce clinching scenarios due, in part, to complex tie-breakers. We present an algorithm that determines under which combinations of game outcomes in the next $n$ days a team will clinch the playoffs (i.e., "$n$-day lookahead clinching"). Our approach is a custom tree search which employs various preprocessing techniques, pruning strategies, and node ordering heuristics to efficiently explore the space of possible outcomes. The tree search leverages a constraint programming (CP)-based subroutine for inference that determines if a team has clinched the playoffs for some snapshot in time of the regular season (i.e., "0-day lookahead clinching"). This CP subroutine aims to find a counter-example in which the team being evaluated is eliminated, taking into account qualification rules and the NHL's extensive list of tie-breakers. We validate the efficacy of our algorithm using hundreds of scenarios based on public NHL data for the seasons 2021-22 through 2024-25. The methods introduced can be readily extended to other metrics of interest, including mathematical proof of playoff elimination, clinching the President's Trophy, as well as clinching (or being eliminated from clinching) any other seed in the standings.

</details>


### [164] [Formal Conjectures: An Open and Evolving Benchmark for Verified Discovery in Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13171)
*Moritz Firsching,Paul Lezeau,Salvatore Mercuri,Miklós Z. Horváth,Yaël Dillies,Calle Sönne,Eric Wieser,Fred Zhang,Thomas Hubert,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Formal Conjectures是一个包含2615个形式化数学问题的大规模基准数据集，其中1029个为开放的未解决研究猜想，836个为已解决的证明问题，全部使用Lean 4形式化。该基准已被用于辅助数学发现，并建立了AI生成证明作为审核机制来确保形式化正确性的协作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
