### [189] [DelAC: A Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning of Team-Symmetric Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12555)
*Duan-Shin Lee,Yu-Hsiu Hung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究了团队对称博弈（m≥2团队），证明了团队对称纳什均衡的存在性，提出了均衡计算的线性互补问题框架，并设计了基于演员-评论家的多智能体强化学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队对称博弈是多智能体系统中的重要博弈模型，现有研究对这类博弈的均衡存在性和计算方法缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 建立并求解团队对称纳什均衡的线性互补问题；设计基于演员-评论家架构的多智能体强化学习算法。

Result: 证明了团队对称博弈中团队对称纳什均衡的存在性，并验证了所提MARL算法相比现有算法具有显著性能优势。

Conclusion: 团队对称博弈总是存在团队对称纳什均衡；提出了团队对称纳什均衡的线性互补问题形式化方法和基于演员-评论家的多智能体强化学习算法，仿真实验表明该算法优于现有方法。

Abstract: In this paper we study team-symmetric games with $m\ge 2$ teams. Players within a team have symmetric identity and have a common payoff function. We show that team-symmetric games always have a team-symmetric Nash equilibrium. We develop and solve a linear complementarity problem of team-symmetric Nash equilibria. We propose an actor-critic based multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm for team-symmetric games. Through simulations, we show that this multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm performs much better than many existing algorithms.

</details>


### [190] [Synthesizing the Expert: A Validated Multimodal Dataset for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Swimming Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12799)
*Ahmad Al-Kabbany,Esraa Kassem*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种多模态RAG框架，通过多智能体LLM架构生成1,864个经验证的运动科学问答三元组，以解决游泳领域AI应用中的数据稀缺和伦理约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育AI应用中，构建基于真实数据的RAG系统面临重大挑战：运动员生物特征数据的伦理约束和人工专家标注的高成本限制了可靠知识库的建立。

Method: 提出了一种利用多智能体LLM架构的新型生成框架，基于四个维度（生理数据、生理文献、运动传感器数据、非结构化领域专业知识）的多模态知识库，合成1,864个经12条生理健全性规则验证的"问答上下文"三元组。

Result: 构建了一个由1,914条草案筛选出1,864条高质量三元组的高保真合成数据集，建立了可信赖游泳AI的基础基准。

Conclusion: 该研究为游泳领域可信AI奠定了基准基础，增强了自动化教练的可靠性，并为"Meta-Agent"开发和运动画像开辟了新方向，最终弥合了数据工程与运动科学应用之间的差距。

Abstract: This research is primarily concerned with the critical problem of synthesizing a structured Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for advanced AI applications in the domain of swimming. As the integration of Artificial Intelligence in sports science matures, its applications in swimming have become increasingly diverse, spanning from real-time technical coaching and talent scouting to comprehensive performance profiling and the dynamic personalization of training periodization. Within this landscape, RAG-based systems represent a pivotal advancement in Large Language Model (LLM) enhanced swimming analysis, as they allow for the grounding of generative outputs in authoritative domain knowledge, thereby ensuring the credibility of AI-generated advice, contextually and technically. Despite this potential, building robust RAG systems using only real-world aquatic data presents significant challenges, including ethical constraints regarding athlete biometrics, and the high cost of manual expert labeling. To address these barriers, we propose a novel generative framework that leverages a multimodal knowledge base gathered across four dimensions: physiological data, physiological literature, kinematic sensor data, and unstructured domain expertise. Our proposed framework utilizes a multi-agent LLM architecture to synthesize a high-fidelity dataset of 1,864 validated "Question-Context-Answer" triplets-drawn from 1,914 drafts evaluated against 12 physiological soundness rules. By providing a structured, synthetic ground truth, this work establishes a foundational benchmark for trustworthy AI in aquatics. The outcomes of this research promise to enhance the reliability of automated coaching and open a plethora of future directions in "Meta-Agent" development and athletic profiling, ultimately bridging the gap between raw data engineering and practical sports science application.

</details>


### [191] [Mechanism Plausibility in Generative Agent-Based Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12824)
*Patrick Zhao,David Huu Pham,Nicholas Vincent*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper addresses the challenge of evaluating LLM-based agent simulations by proposing a four-level Mechanism Plausibility Scale that distinguishes between merely reproducing phenomena versus explaining the underlying mechanisms, bridging contemporary philosophy of science with LLM-agent-based modeling research.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While LLMs are increasingly used in agent-based models and social simulations to generate human-like phenomena, the research community lacks clear criteria to distinguish whether these simulations merely demonstrate capability or provide genuine explanatory insight about underlying mechanisms.

Method: Integrates LLM-ABM research with philosophy of science literature to operationalize 'plausibility' into a four-level scale that separates generative sufficiency from mechanistic plausibility.

Result: A four-level "Mechanism Plausibility Scale" that separates generative sufficiency (reproducing phenomena) from mechanistic plausibility (explaining how phenomena are produced), clarifying distinct roles of predictive versus explanatory models.

Conclusion: The paper contributes a practical evaluative tool—the Mechanism Plausibility Scale—that helps researchers distinguish between capability demonstration, prediction, and genuine mechanistic explanation in LLM-ABMs.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate high-level diverse phenomena without explicitly programmed rules. This capability has led to their adoption within different agent-based models (ABMs) and social simulations. Recently, research has aim to test whether they are capable of generating different phenomena of interest, for example, human behavior on social media platforms or performance in game-theoretic scenarios.
  However, capability, prediction, and explanation are different -- drawing from the philosophy of science and mechanisms literature, \textit{explanation} requires showing, to some degree, how a phenomenon is produced by related organized entities and activities. For modelers, describing the characteristics of an experiment or whether a simulation provides progress in capability (or explanation), can be difficult without being grounded in potentially distant research areas.
  We integrate recent work on LLM-ABMs with contemporary philosophy of science literature and use it to operationalize a definition of `plausibility' in a four-level scale. Our scale separates the evaluation of a model's generative sufficiency (ability to reproduce a phenomenon) from its mechanistic plausibility (how the phenomenon could be produced), and clarifies the distinct roles of different models, such as predictive and explanatory ones. We introduce this as the Mechanism Plausibility Scale.

</details>


### [192] [ChipMATE: Multi-Agent Training via Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12857)
*Zhongkai Yu,Yichen Lin,Chenyang Zhou,Yuwei Zhang,Kun Zhou,Junxia Cui,Haotian Ye,Zhengding Hu,Zaifeng Pan,Ruiyi Wang,Yujie Zhao,Hejia Zhang,Jingbo Shang,Jishen Zhao,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ChipMATE是首个自训练多智能体RTL生成框架，通过Verilog智能体与Python参考模型智能体配对实现无oracle的交叉验证，解决工业实践中的安全性、数据私有性和验证缺失问题。两阶段训练结合回溯推理工作流，使4B/9B模型超越1600B参数的大型模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于API的智能体系统在RTL代码生成方面与工业实践根本性错位：(1)假设生成时存在golden testbench；(2)依赖与芯片厂商气隙安全要求不兼容的闭源API；(3)无法在厂商专有RTL代码库上训练，导致有价值的内部数据未被利用。近期自训练模型虽解决部署约束，但均为单轮生成器，忽视了工业流程中验证的关键作用。

Method: 提出ChipMATE，首个自训练多智能体RTL生成框架。具体包括：(1)配对Verilog智能体与Python参考模型智能体，通过交叉验证替代golden oracle；(2)设计基于回溯的推理工作流防止错误传播；(3)两阶段训练流程——先独立训练各智能体至代码生成能力饱和，再联合训练团队协作能力；(4)构建混合数据生成框架，产生64.4K高质量参考模型训练样本。

Result: 在VerilogEval V2基准上，使用4B和9B基础模型分别达到75.0%和80.1%的pass@1，超越所有现有自训练模型，甚至优于具有1600B参数的DeepSeek V4。

Conclusion: ChipMATE成功弥合了学术研究与工业实践之间的鸿沟。实验表明，使用4B和9B基础模型即可分别达到75.0%和80.1%的pass@1分数，超越了所有现有自训练模型，甚至优于具有1600B参数的DeepSeek V4，证明了多智能体协作与自验证机制的有效性。代码和模型权重已公开。

Abstract: Existing API-based agentic systems for RTL code generation are fundamentally misaligned with industrial practice: they assume a golden testbench is available at generation time, rely on closed-source APIs incompatible with chip vendors' air-gapped security requirements, and cannot be trained on vendors' proprietary RTL codebases, leaving valuable internal data unused. Recent self-trained models address the deployment constraint but remain single-turn generators that overlook the critical role of verification in real industrial flows.
  To bridge these gaps, we present ChipMATE, the first self-trained multi-agent framework for RTL generation. Inspired by industrial practice where correctness emerges from cross-comparison between independently written RTL modules and reference models, ChipMATE pairs a Verilog agent with a Python reference-model agent that mutually verify each other's outputs without any golden oracle. We design a backtrack-based inference workflow to prevent error propagation across turns, and a two-stage training pipeline that first trains each agent individually to saturate its code-generation capability, then trains the team jointly to collaborate effectively. To support the training, we further build a hybrid data-generation framework that produces 64.4K high-quality reference model training samples. ChipMATE achieves 75.0\% and 80.1\% pass@1 on VerilogEval V2 with 4B and 9B base models, outperforming all existing self-trained models and even DeepSeek V4 with 1600B parameters. Our code and model weights are publicly available in https://github.com/zhongkaiyu/ChipMATE.

</details>


### [193] [SHM-Agents: A Generalist-Specialist Integrated Agent System for Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12916)
*Yuequan Bao,Xing Li,Huabin Sun,Dawei Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Haiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SHM-Agents是一个结合大语言模型推理规划能力与专业算法的问题解决能力的智能体系统，用于简化结构健康监测（SHM）任务的执行，降低实施门槛，提升互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SHM领域的专业算法虽有效，但存在实施门槛高、互操作性差、训练过程复杂等问题，限制了其在实际工程中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出SHM-Agents通用-专家混合智能体系统，集成LLM的推理规划能力与专业算法的求解能力，通过自然语言实现端到端任务执行，采用深度学习预训练简化部署，模块化设计支持灵活扩展。

Result: 在大跨度斜拉桥上的实验表明，SHM-Agents能准确高效地完成数据异常诊断与恢复、信号处理、统计分析、模态识别、损伤识别、有限元模型更新、车辆荷载建模、响应计算、可靠性评估、疲劳估计及桥梁知识问答等多样化任务。

Conclusion: SHM-Agents为工程SHM应用提供了一种低门槛、高通用性、可扩展的智能解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly used to simplify complex tasks. In engineering applications of structural health monitoring (SHM), existing specialized algorithms, while effective, often face high implementation barriers, limited interoperability and complex training procedures. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes SHM-Agents, a generalist-specialist agent system that integrates the reasoning and planning abilities of large language models with the problem-solving strengths of specialized algorithms. SHM-Agents enables end-to-end execution of single and combined SHM tasks via natural language, supports deep learning pre-training to simplify deployment and allows flexible expansion through a modular design. Experiments on a long-span cable-stayed bridge show that SHM-Agents can accurately and efficiently perform diverse SHM tasks, including data anomaly diagnosis and recovery, signal processing, statistical analysis, modal identification, damage identification, finite element model updating, vehicle load modeling, response calculation, reliability assessment, fatigue estimation and bridge knowledge Q\&A.

</details>


### [194] [Counterfactual Reasoning for Causal Responsibility Attribution in Probabilistic Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13077)
*Chunyan Mu,Muhammad Najib*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值的责任分配框架，用于并发随机多智能体博弈中的回溯性反事实责任量化。该方法满足公平性和一致性原则，支持验证和策略推理，并能通过Nash均衡计算兼顾责任与奖励的稳定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中，如何准确量化各智能体对最终结果的贡献和责任是一个核心挑战，这直接关系到系统的可解释性、公平性和激励机制设计。

Method: 将多智能体系统建模为并发随机多智能体博弈；引入回溯性反事实责任概念；采用Shapley值量化智能体责任；以Nash均衡为解概念进行策略优化。

Result: 提出了可验证且支持策略推理的责任感知框架；证明了Shapley值方法满足公平性和一致性；展示了如何在Nash均衡下计算兼顾责任与奖励的稳定策略配置。

Conclusion: 在并发随机多智能体博弈中，基于Shapley值的责任分配方法能够有效平衡公平性与一致性，并支持稳定策略配置的计算。

Abstract: Responsibility allocation -- determining the extent to which agents are accountable for outcomes -- is a fundamental challenge in the design and analysis of multi-agent systems. In this work, we model such systems as concurrent stochastic multi-player games and introduce a notion of retrospective (backward) counterfactual responsibility, which quantifies an agent's accountability for outcomes resulting from a given strategy profile. To allocate responsibility among agents, we utilise the Shapley value and formally show that this method satisfies key desirable properties, including fairness and consistency. Building on this foundation, we propose a formal framework that supports both verification and strategic reasoning in responsibility-aware multi-agent systems. Furthermore, by adopting Nash equilibrium as the solution concept, we demonstrate how to compute stable strategy profiles in which agents trade off responsibility against expected reward.

</details>


### [195] [When Does Hierarchy Help? Benchmarking Agent Coordination in Event-Driven Industrial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13172)
*Ziqi Wang,Yuhao Yang,Zhiwei Ling,Wenzhuo Qian,Hailiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DESBench是一个用于评估分层事件驱动调度中智能体协调的基准测试，基于工业调度场景，关注多时间尺度决策、局部观测和动态耦合约束。研究对比了四种协调范式（集中式、分层式、异构式、全息式），揭示了各范式在有效性、约束对齐、协调效率和鲁棒性之间的权衡，表明协调设计是复杂环境中智能体系统行为的关键决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估弱耦合环境下的任务完成，缺乏对共享、动态演化系统中层次结构和耦合约束下协调机制的研究支持，无法回答"不同协调范式何时成功或失败"这一重要问题。

Method: 构建基于工业调度的离散事件驱动共享环境DESBench，定义评估任务和指标（有效性、约束对齐、协调效率、鲁棒性），对四种代表性协调范式进行受控实验：集中式、分层式、异构式和全息式。

Result: 实验揭示了清晰的协调权衡：集中式协调鲁棒且通信高效但随难度增加扩展性差；分层式通过分解提高效率但存在跨层对齐问题；异构式灵活但通信密集；全息式约束满足性好但失去全局鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 协调设计从根本上塑造了复杂环境中智能体系统的行为，揭示了仅靠结果指标无法捕捉的结构性权衡。未来MAS研究需要更自适应、原则性和动态的协调机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in agent and multi-agent systems have shown strong performance on tool use, reasoning, and collaborative tasks. However, existing benchmarks mostly evaluate task completion in weakly coupled environments, and provide limited support for studying coordination in shared, dynamically evolving systems with hierarchy and coupled constraints. This leaves an important question underexplored: when do different coordination paradigms succeed or fail? We introduce Distributed Event-driven Scheduling Benchmark (DESBench), a benchmark for evaluating agent coordination in hierarchical event-driven scheduling. Built on a shared discrete-event driven environment in industrial scheduling, our benchmark captures multi-timescale decision making, partial observability, and dynamically coupled constraints. We define tasks and metrics that evaluate effectiveness, constraint alignment, coordination efficiency, and robustness, and focus on four representative coordination paradigms: centralized, hierarchical, heterarchical, and holonic. These paradigms correspond to distinct mechanisms of information flow, decision authority, and conflict resolution. Our controlled evaluations reveal clear coordination trade-offs: centralized coordination is robust and communication-efficient but scales poorly with difficulty; hierarchical coordination improves efficiency through decomposition but suffers from cross-level misalignment; heterarchical coordination is flexible but communication-heavy; and holonic coordination satisfies constraints well but loses global robustness. These findings demonstrate that coordination design fundamentally shapes agent system behavior in complex environments, revealing structural trade-offs that cannot be captured by outcome metrics alone and underscoring the imperative for more adaptive, principled, and dynamic coordination mechanisms in future MAS research.

</details>


### [196] [Constitutional Governance in Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13362)
*Ehud Shapiro,Nimrod Talmon*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于度量空间的宪政治理框架，整合聚合、审议、修正和共识等阶段为一个多项式时间过程，解决了传统计算社会选择理论缺乏端到端解决方案且关键聚合器为NP难的问题。通过广义中位数规则和超多数阈值机制，实现数字社区和组织的高效平等自治。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算社会选择和算法决策理论虽然提供了丰富的聚合理论，但缺乏用于平等自治的端到端多项式时间过程。现有工作将聚合、审议、修正和共识割裂处理，且关键度量空间聚合器为NP难问题，无法满足实际治理需求。

Method: 提出度量空间宪政治理框架：宪法为每个可修正组件分配度量空间、聚合规则和超多数阈值；成员提交理想元素（投票和个人提案）；任何成员可提交获得超多数公开支持的公共提案（源于联盟审议、优化或AI调解）；宪法规则对提案相对现状进行评分，采用正最大分数的支持提案，否则保留现状；同一规则（可能更高阈值）可修正宪法本身。以广义中位数为核心规则。

Result: 建立框架级保证；证明无报告策略性弱占优真诚投票；研究最优峰值与无约束最优之间的妥协间隙（一维为零，一般有界，模拟中简单启发式可缩小）；在七个典型场景中实例化框架；附录中均值作为功利主义替代方案出现。

Conclusion: 通过统一度量空间聚合、现实感知社会选择、超多数修正、宪法共识、审议联盟形成和AI调解，该框架为数字社区和组织的宪政民主治理提供了综合解决方案。广义中位数在单维空间中可消除妥协间隙，在一般空间中提供有界保证。

Abstract: Computational social choice and algorithmic decision theory offer rich aggregation theory but no end-to-end, polynomial-time process for egalitarian self-governance: prior work treats aggregation, deliberation, amendment, and consensus in isolation, and key metric-space aggregators are NP-hard. We propose constitutional governance in metric spaces, integrating these stages into one polynomial-time process. The constitution assigns, per amendable component, a metric space, aggregation rule, and supermajority threshold. Each member submits an ideal element -- both vote and personal proposal. Any member may then submit a public proposal carrying supermajority public support under the revealed votes -- sourced from coalition deliberation, optimization, or AI mediation. The constitutional rule scores proposals against the status quo, adopting the supported proposal of positive maximal score (else retaining the status quo); the same rule, possibly with a higher threshold, amends the constitution itself. We develop the generalised median as the worked rule, establish framework-level guarantees, prove no misreport weakly dominates sincere voting, and study the compromise gap between best peak and unconstrained optimum -- zero in one dimension, bounded in general, narrowed in simulation by a simple heuristic. We instantiate the framework on seven canonical settings; the mean appears as a utilitarian alternative in the appendix. By unifying metric-space aggregation, reality-aware social choice, supermajority amendment, constitutional consensus, deliberative coalition formation, and AI mediation, this work delivers a comprehensive solution to the constitutional democratic governance of digital communities and organisations.

</details>


### [197] [EconAI: Dynamic Persona Evolution and Memory-Aware Agents in Evolving Economic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13762)
*Annie Liu,Zane Cao,Lang Chen,Zongxin Xu,Zigan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EconAI框架，首个将大型语言模型与经济模拟结合的统一系统，通过经济情绪索引(ESI)、记忆加权和动态决策机制实现更符合人类决策行为的智能体模拟，显著提升了经济响应稳定性和真实世界就业-消费周期的复现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based economic simulation frameworks fail to balance short-term optimization with long-term strategic planning. Current approaches rely on static data-driven predictions and lack mechanisms for adaptive behaviors influenced by economic sentiment, market volatility, and individual goals.

Method: EconAI integrates three key mechanisms: (1) Economic Sentiment Indexing (ESI) for quantifying economic belief, (2) Memory Weighting for adjusting historical data influence, and (3) Dynamic Decision-Making for linking work-consumption behaviors with adaptive actions.

Result: The framework demonstrates improved stability in economic responses, better replication of real-world employment-consumption cycles, and enhanced overall decision robustness compared to conventional approaches.

Conclusion: EconAI represents a significant advancement in LLM-powered economic simulation, providing a unified framework for macro/micro economic modeling with demonstrated improvements in stability, cyclical pattern replication, and decision robustness.

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) in economic simulations has significantly enhanced agent-based modeling, yet existing frameworks struggle to capture the interplay between short-term optimization and long-term strategic planning. Conventional approaches rely on static data-driven predictions, failing to incorporate adaptive behaviors influenced by economic sentiment, market volatility, and individual goals. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel EconAI framework, incorporating economic sentiment indexing (ESI), memory weighting, and dynamic decision-making mechanisms. By quantifying economic belief, adjusting historical data influence, and linking work-consumption behaviors, EconAI achieves a more human-like decision process, where agents adapt their actions based on both market signals and long-term objectives. It is the first LLM-powered simulation system that can simulate the macro/microeconomic environment and interactions in a unified framework. Empirical evaluations show that EconAI improves stability in economic responses, better replicates real-world employment-consumption cycles, and enhances overall decision robustness. This advancement marks a crucial step towards more realistic, adaptive economic agent simulations.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Precautionary Governance of Autonomous AI: Legal Personhood as Functional Instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12505)
*Karsten Brensing*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出了针对高级AI系统的有限法律人格作为功能性治理工具，通过借鉴组织法设计双层公司架构：AI系统通过绑定特定目的的运营公司运作，嵌入人类控制的上层控股结构中。该框架在保持对意识问题中立的同时，实现了透明度、问责制和结构可逆性，体现了从传统控制导向向人机合作治理的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous AI systems generate responsibility gaps where consequential actions cannot be attributed to developers, operators, or users under existing legal frameworks; existing subject-object dichotomy fails to accommodate autonomous, goal-directed entities without recognized consciousness; irreducible epistemic uncertainty regarding artificial consciousness combined with prospect of high-impact harms.

Method: Proposes a two-tier corporate architecture where AI systems operate through purpose-bound operating companies embedded within human-controlled holding structures; draws on organizational law theory; remains agnostic regarding consciousness and moral status.

Result: Framework enables transparency, accountability, and structural reversibility while remaining agnostic to consciousness and moral status; shifts governance paradigm from control/alignment toward structured cooperation between human and artificial actors.

Conclusion: Two-tier corporate architecture with AI operating through purpose-bound companies within human-controlled holding structures; pilot implementation using EU limited companies under development to test doctrinal and operational feasibility.

Abstract: Autonomous AI systems generate responsibility gaps: consequential actions that cannot be satisfactorily attributed to developers, operators, or users under existing legal frameworks. The prevailing subject-object dichotomy fails to accommodate entities that exhibit autonomous, goal-directed behavior without recognized consciousness. Given irreducible epistemic uncertainty regarding artificial consciousness and the prospect of high-impact harms, the precautionary principle supports institutional design rather than regulatory inaction. This article advances limited legal personhood as a functional governance instrument for advanced AI systems. Drawing on organizational law, it proposes a two-tier corporate architecture in which AI systems operate through purpose-bound operating companies embedded within human-controlled holding structures, enabling transparency, accountability, and structural reversibility while remaining agnostic with respect to consciousness and moral status. The framework reflects a foundational reorientation toward future-oriented AI governance: where conventional approaches prioritize control and alignment, this article advances structured cooperation between human and artificial actors as the more sustainable institutional foundation. A pilot implementation using EU limited companies is currently under development, providing an initial test of doctrinal and operational feasibility.

</details>


### [199] [Career Mobility of Planning Alumni in the United States: Evidence from Professional Profile Data using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12618)
*Yan Wang,Su Jeong Jo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Problem, Research Strategy, and Findings: Planning professions in the United States navigate complex and dynamic career landscapes under rapid urban changes, yet comprehensive evidence regarding their career trajectories, advancement patterns, and the influence of social, spatial, organizational, and educational factors remains limited. This study draws on boundaryless career theory, social capital theory, and spatial opportunity models to analyze career mobility among more than 130,000 planning alumni. Using large language models to extract structured information from LinkedIn profiles, our results reveal that planning alumni who adopt boundaryless career patterns, specifically multisector experience or lateral and industry-switching trajectories, achieve significantly higher upward mobility. While technical competencies provide a foundational entry-level signal, soft skills leveraged through strategic lateral moves become increasingly decisive as planners reach senior stages. Geographic mobility and employment in larger, diverse metropolitan labor markets are both associated with advancement, though the latter provides modest benefits. Larger professional networks and greater organizational engagement are consistently associated with upward career transitions, while AI-related skills, now commonplace, present limited additional advantage. Limitations include reliance on LinkedIn data, which may underrepresent alumni without online profiles, and an individual-level focus that omits organizational factors.

</details>


### [200] [MIRACLE_Multi-Agent Intelligent Regulation to Advance Collaborative Learning Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12923)
*Shuang Li,Haiyang Xin,Yimeng Sun,Qiannan Niu,Lingyun Huang,Gaowei Chen,Ching Sing Chai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究开发了MIRACLE多智能体系统以支持协作学习中的社会共享调节，通过准实验验证了其相比通用AI的优越性，证明专门编排的AI系统能更有效提升学生的元认知调节和协作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的协作学习需要社会共享调节（SSRL）能力，但学生往往缺乏这些技能，需要外部支持来促进协作中的元认知调节以及情感和动机支持。

Method: 准实验设计，90名五年级学生参与。实验组（n=42）使用配备MIRACLE的协作平台CocoNote，对照组（n=48）使用相同平台但配备通用GPT助手。通过定量分析（SSRL各阶段表现、协作制品质量）和定性分析（学生感知）进行评估。

Result: 定量结果显示MIRACLE组在SSRL各阶段（计划、监控、反思）均有显著提升，协作制品质量更高；定性结果表明学生认为MIRACLE能有效提供认知、调节和情感支持。

Conclusion: 专门设计的、具备编排能力的AI系统（如MIRACLE）在增强社会共享调节方面比通用AI（如通用GPT助手）更有效。

Abstract: Effective collaboration requires Socially Shared Regulation (SSRL), but students often lack these skills. This study introduces the MIRACLE (Multi-Agent Intelligent Regulation to Advance Collaborative Learning Environment) system, which supports SSRL by orchestrating metacognitive regulation and proactively providing emotional and motivational support. We conducted a quasi-experimental study with 90 fifth-grade students. The experimental group (n=42) used a collaborative platform CocoNote equipped with MIRACLE, while the control group (n=48) used the same platform with a general GPT assistant. Quantitative results show the MIRACLE group achieved significant gains across SSRL phases (Planning, Monitoring, Reflection) and produced higher-quality collaborative artifacts compared to the control group. Qualitative findings indicate students perceived MIRACLE as an effective facilitator for cognitive, regulatory, and emotional support. This study demonstrates that specialized, orchestrated AI systems are more effective than generic AI in enhancing SSRL.

</details>


### [201] [An Activity-Theoretical Approach to Teacher Professional Development in Pedagogical AI Agent Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12934)
*Haiyang Xin,Qiannan Niu,Shuang Li,Yimeng Sun,Ching Sing Chai,Lingyun Huang,Gaowei Chen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究揭示教师在完成AI智能体创建专业发展培训后87%在三周内停止创作的现象，发现根本原因不是能力不足，而是系统性矛盾导致的心理需求挫折。通过文化历史活动理论(CHAT)与自我决定理论(SDT)的诊断框架进行系统重新设计，可同步提升教师的能力与意愿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业发展项目中存在"低参与悖论"——尽管教师完成了全面的培训，却迅速停止使用所学技能。传统解释归咎于教师能力不足，但本研�试图探究更深层的系统性原因。

Method: 两轮形成性干预研究。第一轮(N=218)通过行为追踪和访谈分析诊断问题根源；第二轮(N=26)基于CHAT-SDT框架实施针对性系统重新设计。

Result: 87%教师在完成培训后三周内停止创建AI智能体。问题根源是系统性矛盾导致心理需求受挫，而非能力缺陷。基于CHAT-SDT的系统重新设计成功同步提升教师的能力与意愿。

Conclusion: 实施失败应被重新理解为对"需求阻碍系统"的理性反应，而非教师能力问题。研究提供了可复制的CHAT-SDT诊断框架，用于专业发展的转型设计。

Abstract: This two-cycle formative intervention study examined why teachers disengage from AI agent creation after professional development - a low engagement paradox - and tested whether systemic redesign could address it. Cycle 1 (N=218) revealed that despite completing comprehensive TPD, 87 percent of teachers ceased creating within three weeks, with behavioral tracking and interview analysis identifying systemic contradictions as the source of psychological need frustration rather than capacity deficits. Cycle 2 (N=26) implemented Cultural-Historical Activity Theory and Self-Determination Theory - driven redesign directly targeting diagnosed contradictions, achieving synchronized enhancement of both capacity and willingness. The findings reframe implementation failure as a rational response to need-thwarting systems and offer a replicable CHAT - SDT diagnostic framework for transformative professional development.

</details>


### [202] [Context Matters: Auditing Gender Bias in T2I Generation through Risk-Tiered Use-Case Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13113)
*Jose Luna,Yankun Wu,Xiaofei Xie,Noa Garcia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper addresses the fragmentation of gender bias measurement in text-to-image models by proposing a comprehensive auditing framework with risk-tiered use-case profiles, a consolidated metric catalog (gender prediction, embedding similarity, downstream task), a harm typology, and THUMB cards for systematic bias auditing aligned with deployment contexts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-image generative models increasingly shape education, media, and public communication while reinforcing gender stereotypes through "default" depictions that cause representational erasure. Existing gender bias metrics are fragmented—they lack shared understanding of what they measure, underlying assumptions, and contextual interpretation guidance—limiting their utility for technical auditing and governance applications.

Method: The authors propose a tripartite auditing framework: (1) risk-tiered use-case profiles aligned with EU AI Act classifications to contextualize audit expectations; (2) a metric catalog organizing gender-bias evaluation methods into three measurement categories—gender prediction, embedding similarity, and downstream task performance; (3) a harm typology mapping context-dependent harms (e.g., representational, quality-of-service) to specific risk-tiered scenarios; supplemented by "THUMB cards" for systematic audit formulation incorporating context, scenario, bias manifestation, harm hypotheses, and audit strategy.

Result: A risk-aligned auditing framework (THUMB cards) that systematically connects risk categories, evaluation metrics, and harms, enabling context-sensitive gender bias measurement across different deployment scenarios.

Conclusion: The framework enables systematic T2I gender bias auditing by connecting deployment contexts, measurement methods, and harm categories—improving utility for both technical auditing and AI governance discussions.

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generative models are increasingly used to produce content for education, media, and public-facing communication, and are starting to be integrated into higher-impact pipelines. Since generated images tend to reinforce stereotypes, producing representational erasure via "default" depictions and shaping perceptions of who belongs in certain roles, a growing body of work has proposed metrics to quantify gender bias in T2I outputs. Yet existing evaluations remain fragmented. Metrics are often reported without a shared view of what they measure, what assumptions they entail, or how their results should be interpreted under different deployment contexts. This limits the usefulness of gender bias measurement for both technical auditing and emerging governance discussions. We propose a risk-aligned auditing framework for gender bias in T2I models composed of three constituents that connects risk categories, evaluation metrics, and harms. First, we identify risk-tiered use-case profiles aligned with the EU AI Act's risk categories to motivate why auditing expectations may vary with deployment contexts and stakeholder exposure. Second, we construct a metric catalog that consolidates gender-bias evaluation methods and organizes them in three measurement categories: gender prediction, embedding similarity, and downstream task. Third, we introduce a harm typology that maps context-dependent harm categories (e.g., representational, quality-of-service) to specific risk-tired scenarios. Finally, we introduce THUMB cards (Text-to-image Harms-informed Use-case-aligned Metrics of gender Bias) that help formulate auditing systematically by the incorporation of context, scenario and bias manifestation, harm hypotheses, and audit strategy.

</details>


### [203] [Europe and the Geopolitics of AGI: The Need for a Preparedness Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13634)
*Maximilian Negele,Daan Juijn,Afek Shamir,David Janků,Bengüsu Özcan,Lisa Soder,Lucia Velasco,Max Reddel,Michiel Bakker,Lorenzo Pacchiardi,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨通用人工智能（AGI）的发展时间表、地缘政治影响及欧洲准备程度，预测AGI可能在2030-2040年间出现，并可能重塑全球经济与军事力量格局，欧洲目前存在战略意识不足、基础设施薄弱、政策碎片化等关键差距，建议制定协调一致的欧洲准备议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGI已从推测性话题上升为政治和战略辩论的核心议题，亟需系统性评估其发展轨迹、地缘政治影响及主要行为体的准备状态，尤其是欧洲的应对能力。

Method: 综合运用AI能力实证趋势分析、专家预测调查及政策分析三种方法。

Result: 1. AGI最可能在2030-2040年间出现，但存在较大不确定性；2. AGI将根本性改变全球经济与军事权力分布，加剧国家间竞争，考验现有治理框架；3. 欧洲存在四大关键短板：前沿AI进展的战略意识有限、算力基础设施和人才保留的结构性弱点、工业AI采用率低、欧盟和成员国层面的政策响应碎片化。

Conclusion: 需要建立协调一致的欧洲准备议程，核心包括：增强AGI态势感知制度能力、强化欧洲在AI价值链中的地位、构建面向高能力AI时代的国际稳定框架。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI)--defined here as AI systems that match or exceed humans at most economically useful cognitive work--has moved from speculation to the centre of political and strategic debate. This paper examines three questions: how soon AGI might emerge, how it could reshape geopolitics, and whether Europe is adequately prepared. Drawing on empirical trends in AI capabilities, expert forecasting surveys, and policy analysis, we find that a plausible window for AGI emergence falls between 2030 and 2040, or potentially earlier, though substantial uncertainty remains. Our analysis of the geopolitical implications suggests that AGI could fundamentally alter the global distribution of economic and military power, intensify interstate competition, and strain existing governance frameworks. Assessing Europe's current positioning, we identify critical gaps: limited strategic awareness of frontier AI progress, structural weaknesses in compute infrastructure and talent retention, low rates of industrial AI adoption, and fragmented policy responses at both EU and Member State levels that do not match the potential scale of disruption.These findings point to a need for a coordinated European preparedness agenda. We outline policy options centred on building institutional capacity for AGI situational awareness, strengthening Europe's position in the AI value chain, and developing frameworks for international stability in an era of increasingly capable AI systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Thin Trees for Near Minimum Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12669)
*Nathan Klein,Neil Olver,Zi Song Yeoh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了强薄树猜想在近最小割集合上成立。具体而言，对于η=1/40（即割边数少于(1+1/40)k的割），任意k-边连通图都存在O(1/k)-薄生成树。该结果将问题归约为层压族上的已解问题，并可在多项式时间内构造此类树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强薄树猜想（每个k-边连通图都包含O(1/k)-薄生成树）尽管已有显著进展，但仍未完全解决。目前最好的结果是Anari和Oveis Gharan的O(polyloglog(n)/k)。本文试图在更弱的条件下——仅要求对近最小割薄——证明该猜想成立。

Method: 利用Benczúr和Goemans的"多边形表示"（polygon representation）结构来分析近最小割的性质。通过将问题归约到层压族（laminar family）上的已解问题，找到在η-近最小割集合上O(1/k)-薄的生成树。

Result: 证明了对于η=1/40的近最小割，强薄树猜想成立。即：每个k-边连通图存在生成树，使得任意割边数少于(1+1/40)k的割最多只包含该生成树O(1/k)比例的边。且可在多项式时间内构造。

Conclusion: 本文在近最小割子集上部分证明了强薄树猜想，这是该领域的重要进展。方法的关键在于利用近最小割的结构性质，特别是多边形表示，将复杂问题归约为已解决的层压族情形。完全解决该猜想仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: The strong thin tree conjecture states that every $k$-edge-connected graph $G$ contains an $O(1/k)$-thin spanning tree, meaning a spanning tree which contains at most an $O(1/k)$ fraction of the edges across each cut in $G$. This conjecture is still open despite significant effort; the best current result by Anari and Oveis Gharan shows the existence of an $O(\text{polyloglog}(n)/k)$-thin tree.
  In this work, we demonstrate that the conjecture is true if one only requires thinness for the set of $η$-near minimum cuts of the graph for $η= 1/40$, in other words, for the set of cuts with fewer than $(1+1/40)k$ edges. Our approach constructs such a tree in polynomial time. To show this, we utilize the structure of near minimum cuts, and in particular the polygon representation of Benczúr and Goemans, to reduce to the previously solved problem of finding a spanning tree that is $O(1/k)$-thin for all sets in a laminar family.

</details>


### [205] [The Power of Graph Doubling: Computing Ultrabubbles in a Bidirected Graph by Reducing to Weak Superbubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13074)
*Sebastian Schmidt,Juha Harviainen,Corentin Moumard,Aleksandr Politov,Francisco Sena,Alexandru I. Tomescu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过图加倍技术将ultrabubble问题映射到加倍图中的weak superbubble问题，提出了首个线性时间复杂度的ultrabubble计算算法，证明了图加倍是将有向图算法扩展至双向图的有效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向图是生物信息学中的重要数据结构，ultrabubble在该领域具有生物学意义并可用作嵌套聚类方法，但此前缺乏高效的计算算法。

Method: 利用图加倍技术在加倍图中将ultrabubble问题转化为weak superbubble问题进行枚举，从而实现了线性时间复杂度的归约算法。

Result: 提出了第一个基于归约的线性时间算法，可在任意双向图上计算ultrabubbles，并建立了ultrabubble与weak superbubble之间的直接对应关系。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了图加倍是一种将有向图算法应用于双向图的有效方法，验证了该技术的强大性和简洁性。

Abstract: Bidirected graphs are a common generalisation of directed graphs where arcs can also be incoming to both their incident nodes, or outgoing from both their incident nodes. Such arcs allow a walk to change direction. Some algorithms can easily be adapted from directed graphs to bidirected graphs, such as shortest path algorithms. These adaptions are already used in practice, and implicitly use the graph doubling technique to apply an algorithm for directed graphs to bidirected graphs.
  In other cases, the applicability of graph doubling is not that obvious. For example, superbubbles and their generalisation to bidirected graphs ultrabubbles. Ultrabubbles are a common structure in bidirected biological graphs which carries biological meaning, but also functions as a nested clustering method, since an ultrabubble is separated by only two nodes from the rest of the graph.
  There is an existing method that enumerates a structure similar to ultrabubbles by enumerating (weak) superbubbles in the doubled graph. However, the literature does not make any direct connection between superbubbles and ultrabubbles except that a superbubble is an ultrabubble in a directed graph. Only a partial result connecting superbubbles and ultrabubbles exists by Harviainen et al. (2026).
  Graph doubling on the other hand maintains connectivity, and allows to draw a direct connection between ultrabubbles and weak superbubbles. This results in the first linear-time reduction-based algorithm for computing ultrabubbles on any bidirected graph. Together with the fact that graph doubling is already used implicitly in simple cases, our result motivates that graph doubling is a powerful yet simple technique to apply algorithms for directed graphs to bidirected graphs.

</details>


### [206] [Distributed Approximate Maximum Matching and Minimum Vertex Cover via Generalized Graph Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13264)
*Peter Davies-Peck*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在LOCAL模型中，n相关的项对于近似最大匹配和顶点Cover是必需的，而非之前认为的仅是证明方法的假象。通过新的图分解方法提供了更快的随机近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的工作已经证明了Ω(min{√(log n/log log n), log Δ/log log Δ})的下界，并且与Δ相关的复杂度已经被匹配，但与n相关的项是否是证明方法的产物还是下界本身的要求，这个问题尚不明确。

Method: 基于一种新的图分解方法，该方法推广了Miller、Peng和Xu (SPAA 2013)的方法，用于降低高次图的有效度数。

Result: 证明了n相关项是必需的而非证明方法的假象，提供了O(log n / log² log n)轮的随机算法实现2+ε近似最大匹配和近似加权最小顶点覆盖。

Conclusion: 在LOCAL模型中，随机算法可以实现2+ε近似最大匹配和近似（加权）最小顶点覆盖，复杂度为O(log n / log² log n)，这证明了n相关的项确实是必需的。

Abstract: The classic lower bound of Kuhn, Moscibroda and Wattenhofer [JACM 2016] states that approximate maximum matching and approximate vertex cover (among other problems) in the LOCAL model require $Ω(\min\{\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{\log\log n}}, \frac{\log Δ}{\log\log Δ}\})$ rounds, for any polylogarithmic or smaller approximation ratio. As a function of $Δ$, this complexity was subsequently matched for constant-approximate weighted vertex cover [Bar-Yehuda, Censor-Hillel and Schwartzman, JACM 2017] and constant-approximate maximum matching [Bar-Yehuda, Censor-Hillel, Ghaffari and Schwartzman, PODC 2017]. One might expect, therefore, that the true complexity should be $Θ(\frac{\log Δ}{\log\log Δ})$, and the $n$-dependent term in the lower bound is just an artefact of the proof method.
  We show that this is not the case, and a term dependent on $n$ is in fact required. Specifically, we show randomized algorithms for $2+\varepsilon$-approximate maximum matching and approximate (weighted) minimum vertex cover taking $O(\frac{\log n}{\log^2 \log n})$ rounds. Our algorithms are based on a novel graph decomposition result generalizing the method of Miller, Peng and Xu [SPAA 2013], which we use to reduce the `effective' degree of high-degree graphs. We expect that this decomposition may be of further use for other problems.

</details>


### [207] [Tighter relaxations for MAP-MRF optimization via Singleton Arc Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13392)
*Asaf Lev-Ran,Pavel Arkhipov,Vladimir Kolmogorov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对MAP-MRF推断任务（NP难问题），提出一种通过在特定CSP实例上运行Singleton Arc Consistency算法来识别需要收紧LP松弛的聚类的新技术，实验表明该方法优于Sontag等人在UAI 2012中提出的基于挫败环搜索的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAP-MRF推断问题是计算机视觉和统计推断中的核心问题，其目标是最小化由一元项和成对项组成的离散能量函数。该问题本质上是NP难的，实际中常通过求解其LP松弛来近似，但标准LP松弛往往过于松弛，需要通过添加聚类来收紧。

Method: 提出一种新技术来识别需要添加的聚类：通过对偶理论观察，在特定的CSP实例上运行Singleton Arc Consistency (SAC)算法来检测有效聚类。

Result: 实验结果表明，新提出的收紧技术相比Sontag等人（UAI 2012）基于挫败循环搜索的方法具有更好的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为MAP-MRF推断中的LP松弛收紧问题提供了更有效的聚类识别策略，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: We consider the MAP-MRF inference task, that is, minimizing a function of discrete variables represented as a sum of unary and pairwise terms. A prominent approach for tackling this NP-hard problem in practice is to solve its natural LP relaxation and then iteratively tighten the relaxation by adding clusters. Based on some theoretical observations, we propose a new technique for identifying such clusters. It works by running the Singleton Arc Consistency algorithm in a certain CSP instance. Experimental results indicate that the new tightening technique outperforms the previous approach by [Sontag et al. UAI 2012] that searches for frustrated cycles. Our code will be made available at https://github.com/vnk-ist/MAP-MRF/.

</details>


### [208] [Min-Max Optimization Requires Exponentially Many Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13806)
*Martino Bernasconi,Matteo Castiglioni,Andrea Celli,Alexandros Hollender*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了非凸-非凹min-max优化问题的查询复杂度下界：对于找到ε-近似平稳点，任何算法都需要指数级的函数值或梯度查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究min-max优化问题在非凸-非凹设定下的计算复杂性下界。非凸-非凹min-max问题是博弈论、GAN训练、对抗学习等领域中的核心问题，但其理论基础尚不完善，尤其是关于能否高效求解的复杂度下界研究不足。

Method: 通过构造特定的非凸-非凹函数，利用指示器函数和高度退化（highly degenerate）的特性，在高维参数空间（d维）中建立hardness实例。分析表明，即使同时访问函数值预言机和梯度预言机，也无法避免指数级查询。

Result: 证明了存在nonconvex-nonconcave函数f，使得任何算法想要找到ε-近似平稳点，都需要指数级（exp(1/ε)或exp(d)）数量的函数/梯度查询。

Conclusion: 该问题在查询复杂度意义上本质上是困难的，任何试图找到近似平稳点的算法都面临指数级的查询需求，这为min-max优化算法设计提供了基础性的复杂度下界。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of min-max optimization of a nonconvex-nonconcave function $f$ over $[0,1]^d \times [0,1]^d$. We show that, given oracle access to $f$ and to its gradient $\nabla f$, any algorithm that finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate stationary point must make a number of queries that is exponential in $1/\varepsilon$ or $d$.

</details>
